00001: export async function fallbackBaseSearch({
00002:   getKingResultsByRange,
00003:   findLatestHistoricalBaseDraw,
00004:   addDaysYMD,
00005:   minDate,
00006:   lotteryKey,
00007:   targetYmd,
00008:   targetHourBucket,
00009:   uf,
00010: }) {
00011:   const key = String(lotteryKey || "").trim().toUpperCase();
00012:   const safeUf = String(uf || "").trim().toUpperCase();
00013:   const safeTargetYmd = String(targetYmd || "").trim();
00014:   const safeTargetHour = String(targetHourBucket || "").trim();
00015: 
00016:   if (!safeTargetYmd || !safeTargetHour || !safeUf) {
00017:     return { draw: null, ymd: "", hour: "", source: "none" };
00018:   }
00019: 
00020:   const searchFrom =
00021:     String(minDate || "").trim() ||
00022:     (key === "FEDERAL"
00023:       ? addDaysYMD(safeTargetYmd, -240)
00024:       : addDaysYMD(safeTargetYmd, -90));
00025: 
00026:   const hist =
00027:     (await getKingResultsByRange({
00028:       uf: safeUf,
00029:       dateFrom: searchFrom,
00030:       dateTo: safeTargetYmd,
00031:       mode: "detailed",
00032:       readPolicy: "cache",
00033:     })) || [];
00034: 
00035:   return findLatestHistoricalBaseDraw({
00036:     draws: hist,
00037:     lotteryKey: key,
00038:     targetYmd: safeTargetYmd,
00039:     targetHourBucket: safeTargetHour,
00040:   });
00041: }

00018: function clampProb(v) {
00019:   const n = Number(v);
00020:   if (!Number.isFinite(n)) return 0;
00021:   if (n > 1) return Math.max(0, Math.min(1, n / 100));
00022:   return Math.max(0, Math.min(1, n));
00023: }
00024: 
00025: function resolveProbValue(x) {
00026:   return clampProb(x?.scoreProb ?? x?.prob ?? x?.probCond ?? 0);
00027: }
00028: 
00029: function normalizeMilhar4(v) {
00030:   const dig = String(v || "").replace(/\D+/g, "");
00031:   if (!dig) return "";
00032:   return dig.length >= 4 ? dig.slice(-4) : dig.padStart(4, "0");
00033: }

00035: function build4ColsFromEngineOut(out, expectedCols = 4, perCol = 5) {
00036:   const dezenas = Array.isArray(out?.dezenas) ? out.dezenas : [];
00037:   const slots = Array.isArray(out?.slots) ? out.slots : [];
00038:   const cols = [];
00039: 
00040:   for (const dz of dezenas.slice(0, expectedCols)) {
00041:     const items = slots
00042:       .filter((s) => String(s?.dezena || "") === String(dz))
00043:       .map((s) => normalizeMilhar4(s?.milhar))
00044:       .map((m) => (/^\d{4}$/.test(m) ? m : ""))
00045:       .slice(0, perCol);
00046: 
00047:     while (items.length < perCol) items.push("");
00048:     cols.push({ dezena: dz, items });
00049:   }
00050: 
00051:   while (cols.length < expectedCols) {
00052:     cols.push({ dezena: "", items: Array(perCol).fill("") });
00053:   }
00054: 
00055:   return cols.slice(0, expectedCols);
00056: }

00058: function normalizeImgSrc(src, publicBase = "") {
00059:   const s = String(src || "").trim();
00060:   if (!s) return "";
00061:   if (/^(https?:)?\/\//i.test(s)) return s;
00062:   if (/^(data:|blob:)/i.test(s)) return s;
00063: 
00064:   const base = String(publicBase || "").trim().replace(/\/+$/, "");
00065: 
00066:   if (s.startsWith("/")) return `${base}${s}`;
00067:   if (s.startsWith("public/")) return `${base}/${s.slice("public/".length)}`;
00068:   return `${base}/${s}`;
00069: }

00071: function buildResultStyleImgVariants(grupo, publicBase = "", size = 96) {
00072:   const g = Number(grupo);
00073:   if (!Number.isFinite(g) || g < 1 || g > 25) return [];
00074: 
00075:   const seeds = [getImgFromGrupo?.(g, size), getImgFromGrupo?.(g)]
00076:     .map((x) => normalizeImgSrc(x, publicBase))
00077:     .filter(Boolean);
00078: 
00079:   const out = [];
00080: 
00081:   for (const seed of seeds) {
00082:     const clean = String(seed).split("?")[0];
00083:     if (!clean) continue;
00084: 
00085:     out.push(clean);
00086: 
00087:     if (/\.png$/i.test(clean)) {
00088:       out.push(clean.replace(/\.png$/i, ".jpg"));
00089:       out.push(clean.replace(/\.png$/i, ".jpeg"));
00090:       out.push(clean.replace(/\.png$/i, ".webp"));
00091:     } else if (/\.jpg$/i.test(clean)) {
00092:       out.push(clean.replace(/\.jpg$/i, ".png"));
00093:       out.push(clean.replace(/\.jpg$/i, ".jpeg"));
00094:       out.push(clean.replace(/\.jpg$/i, ".webp"));
00095:     } else if (/\.jpeg$/i.test(clean)) {
00096:       out.push(clean.replace(/\.jpeg$/i, ".png"));
00097:       out.push(clean.replace(/\.jpeg$/i, ".jpg"));
00098:       out.push(clean.replace(/\.jpeg$/i, ".webp"));
00099:     } else if (/\.webp$/i.test(clean)) {
00100:       out.push(clean.replace(/\.webp$/i, ".png"));
00101:       out.push(clean.replace(/\.webp$/i, ".jpg"));
00102:       out.push(clean.replace(/\.webp$/i, ".jpeg"));
00103:     }
00104:   }
00105: 
00106:   return Array.from(new Set(out.filter(Boolean)));
00107: }

00109: function top3ItemMatchesSlot(item, slot) {
00110:   const targetY = safeStr(slot?.targetYmd || "");
00111:   const targetH = toHourBucket(slot?.targetHour || "");
00112: 
00113:   const nextY = safeStr(item?.meta?.next?.ymd || "");
00114:   const nextH = toHourBucket(item?.meta?.next?.hour || "");
00115: 
00116:   if (!isYMD(targetY) || !targetH) return false;
00117:   if (!isYMD(nextY) || !nextH) return false;
00118: 
00119:   return nextY === targetY && nextH === targetH;
00120: }

00001: import {
00002:   pickPrize1GrupoFromDraw,
00003:   pickDrawYMD,
00004:   pickDrawHour,
00005: } from "./top3.engine";
00006: 
00007: import { isYMD, toHourBucket, hourToInt } from "./top3.formatters";
00008: 
00009: /*
00010: TOP3+ — Cadeia de Bichos
00011: Modelo de transição de grupos
00012: 
00013: - Entende targetYmd / targetHourBucket
00014: - Encontra o draw imediatamente anterior ao alvo
00015: - Separa transições por faixa de horário
00016: - Evita ordenar sorteio sem hora como 00h
00017: - Evita vazamento usando apenas histórico anterior ao alvo no modo target_aware
00018: */
00019: 
00020: function safeInt(v, fallback = 0) {
00021:   const n = Number(v);
00022:   return Number.isFinite(n) ? n : fallback;
00023: }
00024: 
00025: function drawSortTs(draw) {
00026:   const y = pickDrawYMD(draw);
00027:   const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(draw));
00028: 
00029:   if (!isYMD(y) || !h) return Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY;
00030: 
00031:   const mins = hourToInt(h);
00032:   if (!Number.isFinite(mins) || mins < 0) return Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY;
00033: 
00034:   const [Y, M, D] = y.split("-").map(Number);
00035:   const base = Date.UTC(Y, M - 1, D);
00036: 
00037:   return base + mins * 60 * 1000;
00038: }
00039: 
00040: function slotTs(ymd, hourBucket) {
00041:   const safeYmd = String(ymd || "").trim();
00042:   const h = toHourBucket(hourBucket);
00043: 
00044:   if (!isYMD(safeYmd) || !h) return Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY;
00045: 
00046:   const mins = hourToInt(h);
00047:   if (!Number.isFinite(mins) || mins < 0) return Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY;
00048: 
00049:   const [Y, M, D] = safeYmd.split("-").map(Number);
00050:   const base = Date.UTC(Y, M - 1, D);
00051: 
00052:   return base + mins * 60 * 1000;
00053: }
00054: 
00055: function dedupeAndSortDraws(draws) {
00056:   const list = Array.isArray(draws) ? draws : [];
00057:   const map = new Map();
00058: 
00059:   for (const d of list) {
00060:     const y = pickDrawYMD(d);
00061:     const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));
00062:     const ts = drawSortTs(d);
00063: 
00064:     if (!Number.isFinite(ts)) continue;
00065: 
00066:     const key =
00067:       String(d?.id || d?.drawId || "").trim() ||
00068:       (isYMD(y) && h ? `${y}|${h}` : "");
00069: 
00070:     if (!key) continue;
00071: 
00072:     const prev = map.get(key);
00073: 
00074:     if (!prev || ts < drawSortTs(prev)) {
00075:       map.set(key, d);
00076:     }
00077:   }
00078: 
00079:   return Array.from(map.values()).sort((a, b) => drawSortTs(a) - drawSortTs(b));
00080: }
00081: 
00082: function makeEdgeKey(fromHour, toHour) {
00083:   const from = toHourBucket(fromHour);
00084:   const to = toHourBucket(toHour);
00085: 
00086:   if (!from || !to) return "";
00087: 
00088:   return `${from}->${to}`;
00089: }
00090: 
00091: function getNestedMap(map, key) {
00092:   if (!map.has(key)) map.set(key, new Map());
00093:   return map.get(key);
00094: }
00095: 
00096: function getHistoryBeforeTarget(draws, targetYmd, targetHourBucket) {
00097:   const list = dedupeAndSortDraws(draws);
00098:   const target = slotTs(targetYmd, targetHourBucket);
00099: 
00100:   if (!list.length || !Number.isFinite(target)) return [];
00101: 
00102:   return list.filter((d) => {
00103:     const ts = drawSortTs(d);
00104:     return Number.isFinite(ts) && ts < target;
00105:   });
00106: }
00107: 
00108: export function computeChainTransitions(draws) {
00109:   const list = dedupeAndSortDraws(draws);
00110:   const transitionsAny = new Map();
00111:   const transitionsByEdge = new Map();
00112: 
00113:   for (let i = 0; i < list.length - 1; i += 1) {
00114:     const fromDraw = list[i];
00115:     const toDraw = list[i + 1];
00116: 
00117:     const fromGrupo = Number(pickPrize1GrupoFromDraw(fromDraw));
00118:     const toGrupo = Number(pickPrize1GrupoFromDraw(toDraw));
00119:     const fromHour = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(fromDraw));
00120:     const toHour = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(toDraw));
00121: 
00122:     if (!Number.isFinite(fromGrupo) || !Number.isFinite(toGrupo)) continue;
00123:     if (fromGrupo < 1 || fromGrupo > 25) continue;
00124:     if (toGrupo < 1 || toGrupo > 25) continue;
00125:     if (!fromHour || !toHour) continue;
00126: 
00127:     const anyRow = getNestedMap(transitionsAny, fromGrupo);
00128:     anyRow.set(toGrupo, safeInt(anyRow.get(toGrupo), 0) + 1);
00129: 
00130:     const edgeKey = makeEdgeKey(fromHour, toHour);
00131:     if (!edgeKey) continue;
00132: 
00133:     const edgeMap = getNestedMap(transitionsByEdge, edgeKey);
00134:     const edgeRow = getNestedMap(edgeMap, fromGrupo);
00135:     edgeRow.set(toGrupo, safeInt(edgeRow.get(toGrupo), 0) + 1);
00136:   }
00137: 
00138:   return { transitionsAny, transitionsByEdge };
00139: }
00140: 
00141: function findPreviousDrawForTarget(draws, targetYmd, targetHourBucket) {
00142:   const list = dedupeAndSortDraws(draws);
00143:   if (!list.length) return null;
00144: 
00145:   const target = slotTs(targetYmd, targetHourBucket);
00146:   if (!Number.isFinite(target)) return null;
00147: 
00148:   let prev = null;
00149: 
00150:   for (const d of list) {
00151:     const ts = drawSortTs(d);
00152:     if (!Number.isFinite(ts)) continue;
00153: 
00154:     if (ts < target) {
00155:       prev = d;
00156:     } else {
00157:       break;
00158:     }
00159:   }
00160: 
00161:   return prev;
00162: }
00163: 
00164: function rankTransitionMap(rowMap, basedOnGrupo, topN) {
00165:   if (!rowMap || !rowMap.size) return [];
00166: 
00167:   const total = Array.from(rowMap.values()).reduce(
00168:     (acc, n) => acc + safeInt(n, 0),
00169:     0
00170:   );
00171: 
00172:   if (total <= 0) return [];
00173: 
00174:   return Array.from(rowMap.entries())
00175:     .sort((a, b) => {
00176:       if (Number(b[1]) !== Number(a[1])) return Number(b[1]) - Number(a[1]);
00177:       return Number(a[0]) - Number(b[0]);
00178:     })
00179:     .slice(0, Math.max(1, safeInt(topN, 3)))
00180:     .map(([grupo, freq], i) => {
00181:       const prob = Number(freq) / total;
00182: 
00183:       return {
00184:         rank: i + 1,
00185:         grupo: Number(grupo),
00186:         freq: Number(freq),
00187:         prob,
00188:         probPct: Number((prob * 100).toFixed(2)),
00189:         basedOnGrupo: Number(basedOnGrupo),
00190:         totalTransitionsFromGrupo: total,
00191:       };
00192:     });
00193: }
00194: 
00195: export function predictNextGrupoFromChain(arg1, arg2 = 3) {
00196:   if (Array.isArray(arg1)) {
00197:     const draws = arg1;
00198:     const topN = arg2;
00199: 
00200:     const list = dedupeAndSortDraws(draws);
00201:     if (!list.length) return [];
00202: 
00203:     const { transitionsAny } = computeChainTransitions(list);
00204:     const lastDraw = list[list.length - 1];
00205:     const lastGrupo = Number(pickPrize1GrupoFromDraw(lastDraw));
00206: 
00207:     if (!Number.isFinite(lastGrupo) || lastGrupo < 1 || lastGrupo > 25) {
00208:       return [];
00209:     }
00210: 
00211:     const rowMap = transitionsAny.get(lastGrupo);
00212:     const ranked = rankTransitionMap(rowMap, lastGrupo, topN);
00213: 
00214:     return ranked.map((x) => ({
00215:       ...x,
00216:       mode: "legacy_any",
00217:       basedOnHour: toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(lastDraw)),
00218:       basedOnYmd: pickDrawYMD(lastDraw),
00219:       targetHour: "",
00220:       targetYmd: "",
00221:       transitionType: "ANY",
00222:       transitionEdge: "",
00223:     }));
00224:   }
00225: 
00226:   const { draws, targetYmd, targetHourBucket, topN = 3 } = arg1 || {};
00227: 
00228:   const targetHour = toHourBucket(targetHourBucket);
00229:   const safeTargetYmd = String(targetYmd || "").trim();
00230: 
00231:   if (!isYMD(safeTargetYmd) || !targetHour) return [];
00232: 
00233:   const historyBeforeTarget = getHistoryBeforeTarget(
00234:     draws,
00235:     safeTargetYmd,
00236:     targetHour
00237:   );
00238: 
00239:   if (!historyBeforeTarget.length) return [];
00240: 
00241:   const prevDraw = historyBeforeTarget[historyBeforeTarget.length - 1];
00242:   if (!prevDraw) return [];
00243: 
00244:   const prevGrupo = Number(pickPrize1GrupoFromDraw(prevDraw));
00245:   const prevHour = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(prevDraw));
00246:   const prevYmd = pickDrawYMD(prevDraw);
00247: 
00248:   if (
00249:     !Number.isFinite(prevGrupo) ||
00250:     prevGrupo < 1 ||
00251:     prevGrupo > 25 ||
00252:     !prevHour ||
00253:     !isYMD(prevYmd)
00254:   ) {
00255:     return [];
00256:   }
00257: 
00258:   const { transitionsAny, transitionsByEdge } =
00259:     computeChainTransitions(historyBeforeTarget);
00260: 
00261:   const edgeKey = makeEdgeKey(prevHour, targetHour);
00262:   const edgeMap = edgeKey ? transitionsByEdge.get(edgeKey) || null : null;
00263:   const edgeRow = edgeMap ? edgeMap.get(prevGrupo) : null;
00264: 
00265:   let ranked = rankTransitionMap(edgeRow, prevGrupo, topN);
00266:   let transitionType = "EDGE";
00267: 
00268:   if (!ranked.length) {
00269:     const anyRow = transitionsAny.get(prevGrupo) || null;
00270:     ranked = rankTransitionMap(anyRow, prevGrupo, topN);
00271:     transitionType = "ANY_FALLBACK";
00272:   }
00273: 
00274:   return ranked.map((x) => ({
00275:     ...x,
00276:     mode: "target_aware",
00277:     basedOnHour: prevHour,
00278:     basedOnYmd: prevYmd,
00279:     targetHour,
00280:     targetYmd: safeTargetYmd,
00281:     transitionType,
00282:     transitionEdge: edgeKey,
00283:   }));
00284: }

00037: function findPreviousValidDraw(draws, currentYmd, currentHour) {
00038:   const list = Array.isArray(draws) ? draws : [];
00039:   const targetY = safeStr(currentYmd);
00040:   const targetH = toHourBucket(currentHour);
00041: 
00042:   if (!isYMD(targetY) || !targetH) return null;
00043: 
00044:   const targetTs = ymdHourToTs(targetY, targetH);
00045: 
00046:   return list
00047:     .filter((draw) => Array.isArray(draw?.prizes) && draw.prizes.length > 0)
00048:     .map((draw) => {
00049:       const y = pickDrawYMD(draw);
00050:       const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(draw));
00051:       return { draw, ts: ymdHourToTs(y, h) };
00052:     })
00053:     .filter((x) => Number.isFinite(x.ts) && x.ts < targetTs)
00054:     .sort((a, b) => b.ts - a.ts)[0]?.draw || null;
00055: }

00061: function grupoFromDezena2(dezena2) {
00062:   const s = safeStr(dezena2);
00063:   if (!/^\d{2}$/.test(s)) return null;
00064: 
00065:   const n = Number(s);
00066:   if (!Number.isFinite(n) || n < 0 || n > 99) return null;
00067:   if (n === 0) return 25;
00068: 
00069:   const g = Math.ceil(n / 4);
00070:   return g >= 1 && g <= 25 ? g : null;
00071: }
00072: 
00073: function drawQualityScore(draw) {
00074:   const prizes = Array.isArray(draw?.prizes) ? draw.prizes : [];
00075:   const y = pickDrawYMD(draw);
00076:   const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(draw));
00077: 
00078:   let score = 0;
00079:   if (isYMD(y)) score += 20;
00080:   if (h) score += 20;
00081:   score += Math.min(10, prizes.length) * 10;
00082: 
00083:   const hasP1 = prizes.some((p) => guessPrizePos(p) === 1);
00084:   if (hasP1) score += 15;
00085: 
00086:   return score;
00087: }

00089: export function guessPrizePos(p) {
00090:   const pos = Number.isFinite(Number(p?.position))
00091:     ? Number(p.position)
00092:     : Number.isFinite(Number(p?.posicao))
00093:       ? Number(p.posicao)
00094:       : Number.isFinite(Number(p?.pos))
00095:         ? Number(p.pos)
00096:         : Number.isFinite(Number(p?.colocacao))
00097:           ? Number(p.colocacao)
00098:           : null;
00099: 
00100:   return pos;
00101: }

00103: export function guessPrizeGrupo(p) {

00106:     p?.group2,

00110:     p?.grupo_animal,
00111:     p?.grupoAnimal,
00112:     p?.g,
00113:   ];
00114: 
00115:   for (const v of directCandidates) {
00116:     const g = Number(v);
00117:     if (Number.isFinite(g) && g >= 1 && g <= 25) {
00118:       return g;
00119:     }
00120:   }
00121:

00123:

00125:     const dezena2 = getDezena2(milhar4);
00126:     const derived = grupoFromDezena2(dezena2);

00129:   }

00131:   return null;

00190: function safeInt(v, fallback) {
00191:   const n = Number(v);
00192:   return Number.isFinite(n) ? n : fallback;
00193: }

00195: function getDayOfMonth(ymd) {
00196:   if (!isYMD(ymd)) return NaN;
00197:   return Number(String(ymd).slice(8, 10));
00198: }
00199: 
00200: function ymdHourToTs(ymd, hourBucket) {
00201:   const y = safeStr(ymd);
00202:   const h = toHourBucket(hourBucket);
00203: 
00204:   if (!isYMD(y) || !h) return Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY;
00205: 
00206:   const mins = hourToInt(h);
00207:   if (!Number.isFinite(mins) || mins < 0) {
00208:     return Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY;
00209:   }
00210: 
00211:   const [Y, M, D] = y.split("-").map((x) => Number(x));
00212:   const base = Date.UTC(Y, M - 1, D);
00213: 
00214:   return base + mins * 60 * 1000;
00215: }

00217: /* =========================

00221: const FEDERAL_SUNDAY_START_YMD = "2026-07-19";
00222: const PT_RIO_SUNDAY_REDUCED_START_YMD = "2026-07-18";
00223: const FEDERAL_20_REMOVES_PT_RIO_18_START_YMD = "2025-11-03";
00224: 
00225: /**
00226:  * Calendário Federal com preservação histórica.
00227:  *
00228:  * Até 18/07/2026:
00229:  * - quarta-feira às 20h;
00230:  * - sábado às 20h.
00231:  *
00232:  * A partir de 19/07/2026:
00233:  * - quarta-feira às 20h;
00234:  * - domingo às 11h;
00235:  * - sábado deixa de ter sorteio Federal.
00236:  */
00237: export function isFederalDrawDay(ymd) {
00238:   const y = safeStr(ymd);
00239:   if (!y) return false;
00240: 
00241:   const dow = Number(getDowKey(y));
00242: 
00243:   if (y >= FEDERAL_SUNDAY_START_YMD) {
00244:     return dow === 0 || dow === 3;
00245:   }
00246: 
00247:   return dow === 3 || dow === 6;
00248: }
00249: 
00250: function getFederalScheduleForYmd(ymd, FEDERAL_SCHEDULE) {
00251:   const y = safeStr(ymd);
00252: 
00253:   if (!y || !isFederalDrawDay(y)) {
00254:     return [];
00255:   }
00256: 
00257:   const dow = Number(getDowKey(y));
00258: 
00259:   if (y >= FEDERAL_SUNDAY_START_YMD && dow === 0) {
00260:     return ["11:00"];
00261:   }
00262: 
00263:   const historical = Array.isArray(FEDERAL_SCHEDULE)
00264:     ? FEDERAL_SCHEDULE
00265:     : [];
00266: 
00267:   const normalized = historical
00268:     .map(toHourBucket)
00269:     .filter(Boolean);
00270: 
00271:   if (normalized.includes("20:00")) {
00272:     return ["20:00"];
00273:   }
00274: 
00275:   return normalized.length
00276:     ? normalized
00277:     : ["20:00"];
00278: }

00280: /**
00281:  * Determina apenas os slots esperados.
00282:  * Nenhum resultado histórico é removido.
00283:  */
00284: export function getPtRioScheduleForYmd(
00285:   ymd,
00286:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
00287:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT
00288: ) {
00289:   const y = safeStr(ymd);
00290:   const dow = Number(getDowKey(y));
00291: 
00292:   if (dow === 0) {
00293:     const sundaySchedule = Array.isArray(PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_SUNDAY)
00294:       ? [...PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_SUNDAY]
00295:       : [];
00296: 
00297:     if (y >= PT_RIO_SUNDAY_REDUCED_START_YMD) {
00298:       return sundaySchedule.filter((hour) => {
00299:         const h = normalizeHourLike(hour);
00300: 
00301:         return h === "14:00" || h === "16:00";
00302:       });
00303:     }
00304: 
00305:     return sundaySchedule;
00306:   }
00307: 
00308:   if (dow === 6) {
00309:     const saturdaySchedule = Array.isArray(
00310:       PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT
00311:     )
00312:       ? [...PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT]
00313:       : [];
00314: 
00315:     if (y >= "2026-07-18") {
00316:       const transitionedSchedule = saturdaySchedule
00317:         .map(normalizeHourLike)
00318:         .filter(Boolean)
00319:         .filter((hour) => hour !== "18:00");
00320: 
00321:       if (!transitionedSchedule.includes("19:00")) {
00322:         transitionedSchedule.push("19:00");
00323:       }
00324: 
00325:       return transitionedSchedule
00326:         .filter(
00327:           (hour, index, schedule) =>
00328:             schedule.indexOf(hour) === index
00329:         )
00330:         .sort(
00331:           (hourA, hourB) =>
00332:             hourToInt(hourA) - hourToInt(hourB)
00333:         );
00334:     }
00335: 
00336:     return saturdaySchedule;
00337:   }
00338: 
00339:   if (dow === 3) {
00340:     return Array.isArray(PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT)
00341:       ? [...PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT]
00342:       : [];
00343:   }
00344: 
00345:   return Array.isArray(PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL)
00346:     ? [...PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL]
00347:     : [];
00348: }

00350: export function getScheduleForLottery({
00351:   lotteryKey,
00352:   ymd,
00353:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
00354:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
00355:   FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,

00360:     return getFederalScheduleForYmd(

00367:     return [...LOOK_SCHEDULE];
00368:   }
00369: 
00370:   if (key === "NACIONAL") {
00371:     return [...NACIONAL_SCHEDULE];
00372:   }
00373: 
00374:   return getPtRioScheduleForYmd(
00375:     ymd,
00376:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,

00378:   );

00381: export function scheduleSet(schedule) {
00382:   return new Set((Array.isArray(schedule) ? schedule : []).map(toHourBucket));
00383: }

00385: export function isHourInSchedule(schedule, hhmm) {

00390: export function findLastDrawInList(draws, schedule) {
00391:   const list = Array.isArray(draws) ? draws : [];
00392:   if (!list.length) return null;
00393: 
00394:   const sorted = [...list]
00395:     .filter((d) => isHourInSchedule(schedule, pickDrawHour(d)))
00396:     .sort((a, b) => {
00397:       const ta = ymdHourToTs(pickDrawYMD(a), toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(a)));
00398:       const tb = ymdHourToTs(pickDrawYMD(b), toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(b)));
00399:       return tb - ta;
00400:     });
00401: 
00402:   return sorted[0] || null;
00403: }
00404: 
00405: export function findPrevDrawBeforeTargetInSameDay(
00406:   draws,
00407:   targetHourBucket,
00408:   schedule
00409: ) {
00410:   const list = Array.isArray(draws) ? draws : [];
00411:   if (!list.length) return null;
00412: 
00413:   const t = hourToInt(targetHourBucket);
00414:   if (t < 0) return null;
00415: 
00416:   const candidates = list
00417:     .filter((d) => isHourInSchedule(schedule, pickDrawHour(d)))
00418:     .map((d) => ({ d, h: hourToInt(toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d))) }))
00419:     .filter((x) => x.h >= 0 && x.h < t)
00420:     .sort((a, b) => b.h - a.h);
00421: 
00422:   return candidates.length ? candidates[0].d : null;
00423: }

00425: export async function getPreviousDrawRobust({
00426:   getKingResultsByDate,
00427:   lotteryKey,
00428:   ymdTarget,
00429:   targetHourBucket,
00430:   todayDraws,
00431:   schedule,
00432:   maxBackDays = 10,
00433:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
00434:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
00435:   FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
00436: }) {
00437:   const prevSameDay = findPrevDrawBeforeTargetInSameDay(
00438:     todayDraws,
00439:     targetHourBucket,
00440:     schedule
00441:   );
00442: 
00443:   if (prevSameDay) {
00444:     return {
00445:       draw: prevSameDay,
00446:       ymd: pickDrawYMD(prevSameDay) || ymdTarget,
00447:       hour: toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(prevSameDay)),
00448:       source: "same_day",
00449:     };
00450:   }
00451: 
00452:   for (let i = 1; i <= maxBackDays; i += 1) {
00453:     const day = addDaysYMD(ymdTarget, -i);
00454: 
00455:     const daySchedule = getScheduleForLottery({
00456:       lotteryKey,
00457:       ymd: day,
00458:       PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
00459:       PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
00460:       FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
00461:     });
00462:

00464:       safeStr(lotteryKey).toUpperCase() === "FEDERAL" &&
00465:       !daySchedule.length
00466:     ) {
00467:       continue;
00468:     }
00469: 
00470:     const out = await getKingResultsByDate({
00471:       uf: lotteryKey,
00472:       date: day,
00473:       closeHour: null,
00474:       positions: null,
00475:       readPolicy: "server",
00476:     });
00477: 
00478:     const last = findLastDrawInList(out, daySchedule);
00479:     if (last) {
00480:       return {
00481:         draw: last,
00482:         ymd: pickDrawYMD(last) || day,
00483:         hour: toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(last)),
00484:         source: "prev_day",
00485:       };
00486:     }
00487:   }
00488: 
00489:   return { draw: null, ymd: "", hour: "", source: "none" };
00490: }

00496: export function getNextSlotForLottery({

00498:   ymd,
00499:   hourBucket,
00500:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
00501:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,

00529:     lotteryKey: key,
00530:     ymd: y0,
00531:     hourBucket: h0,

00533:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,

00538:     (x) => toHourBucket(x) === h0

00543:   }

00547:     const sch = getScheduleForLottery({
00548:       lotteryKey: key,
00549:       ymd: day,
00550:       PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
00551:       PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
00552:       FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
00553:     });
00554:     if (Array.isArray(sch) && sch.length) {
00555:       return { ymd: day, hour: toHourBucket(sch[0]) };
00556:     }
00557:   }

00560: }

00565: 
00566: export function findNextExistingDrawFromSlot({
00567:   lotteryKey,
00568:   startSlot,
00569:   drawsIndex,
00570:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
00571:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
00572:   FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
00573:   maxSteps = TOP3_NEXTDRAW_SCAN_MAX_STEPS,
00574:   maxDays = TOP3_NEXTDRAW_SCAN_MAX_DAYS,
00575: }) {
00576:   const key = safeStr(lotteryKey).toUpperCase();
00577:   const y0 = safeStr(startSlot?.ymd);
00578:   const h0 = toHourBucket(startSlot?.hour);
00579: 
00580:   if (!isYMD(y0) || !h0 || !(drawsIndex instanceof Map)) {
00581:     return { slot: null, draw: null };
00582:   }
00583: 
00584:   if (key === "FEDERAL") {
00585:     let curY = y0;
00586:     let steps = 0;
00587:     let daysWalked = 0;
00588: 
00589:     while (steps < maxSteps && daysWalked <= maxDays) {
00590:       const sch = getScheduleForLottery({
00591:         lotteryKey: key,
00592:         ymd: curY,
00593:         PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
00594:         PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
00595:         FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
00596:       });
00597: 
00598:       if (Array.isArray(sch) && sch.length) {
00599:         const hh = toHourBucket(sch[0]);
00600:         const d = drawsIndex.get(`${curY}|${hh}`) || null;
00601:         if (d) return { slot: { ymd: curY, hour: hh }, draw: d };
00602:       }
00603: 
00604:       curY = addDaysYMD(curY, 1);
00605:       daysWalked += 1;
00606:       steps += 1;
00607:     }
00608: 
00609:     return { slot: null, draw: null };
00610:   }
00611: 
00612:   let curY = y0;
00613:   let curH = h0;
00614:   let steps = 0;
00615:   let daysWalked = 0;
00616: 
00617:   while (steps < maxSteps && daysWalked <= maxDays) {
00618:     const drawKey = `${curY}|${toHourBucket(curH)}`;
00619:     let d = drawsIndex.get(drawKey) || null;
00620: 
00621:     if (!d) {
00622:       const curTs = ymdHourToTs(curY, curH);
00623:       const curYLocked = curY;
00624:       d = Array.from(drawsIndex.entries())
00625:         .map(([k, v]) => {
00626:           const [ky, kh] = String(k).split("|");
00627:           return { ymd: ky, hour: kh, draw: v, ts: ymdHourToTs(ky, kh) };
00628:         })
00629:         .filter((x) => x.ymd === curYLocked)
00630:         .filter((x) => Number.isFinite(x.ts) && x.ts >= curTs)
00631:         .sort((a, b) => a.ts - b.ts)[0]?.draw || null;
00632:     }
00633: 
00634:     if (d) return { slot: { ymd: curY, hour: toHourBucket(curH) }, draw: d };
00635: 
00636:     const sch = getScheduleForLottery({
00637:       lotteryKey: key,
00638:       ymd: curY,
00639:       PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
00640:       PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
00641:       FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
00642:     });
00643: 
00644:     const arr = Array.isArray(sch) ? sch.map(toHourBucket) : [];
00645:     const curBucket = toHourBucket(curH);
00646:     const idx = arr.indexOf(curBucket);
00647: 
00648:     if (idx >= 0 && idx < arr.length - 1) {
00649:       curH = arr[idx + 1];
00650:     } else {
00651:       curY = addDaysYMD(curY, 1);
00652:       daysWalked += 1;
00653: 
00654:       const sch2 = getScheduleForLottery({
00655:         lotteryKey: key,
00656:         ymd: curY,
00657:         PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
00658:         PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
00659:         FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
00660:       });
00661: 
00662:       const arr2 = Array.isArray(sch2) ? sch2.map(toHourBucket) : [];
00663:       if (!arr2.length) break;
00664:       curH = arr2[0];
00665:     }
00666: 
00667:     steps += 1;
00668:   }
00669: 
00670:   return { slot: null, draw: null };
00671: }
00672: 
00673: /* =========================
00674:    Index + utilitários de grupo
00675: ========================= */

00677: export function indexDrawsByYmdHour(draws) {
00678:   const map = new Map();
00679:   const list = Array.isArray(draws) ? draws : [];
00680: 
00681:   for (const d of list) {
00682:     const y = pickDrawYMD(d);
00683:     const rawH = pickDrawHour(d);
00684:     const h = toHourBucket(rawH);
00685: 
00686:     if (!isYMD(y) || !h) continue;
00687: 
00688:     const drawKey = `${y}|${h}`;
00689:     const prev = map.get(drawKey);
00690: 
00691:     if (!prev) {
00692:       map.set(drawKey, d);
00693:       continue;
00694:     }
00695: 
00696:     if (drawQualityScore(d) >= drawQualityScore(prev)) {
00697:       map.set(drawKey, d);
00698:     }
00699:   }
00700: 
00701:   return map;
00702: }
00703: 
00704: export function computeLastSeenByGrupo(draws) {
00705:   const last = new Map();
00706:   const list = Array.isArray(draws) ? draws : [];
00707: 
00708:   for (const d of list) {
00709:     const y = pickDrawYMD(d);
00710:     const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));
00711:     if (!isYMD(y) || !h) continue;
00712: 
00713:     const ts = ymdHourToTs(y, h);
00714:     const ps = Array.isArray(d?.prizes) ? d.prizes : [];
00715: 
00716:     for (const p of ps) {
00717:       const pos = guessPrizePos(p);
00718:       if (!Number.isFinite(Number(pos)) || Number(pos) < 1 || Number(pos) > 5) {
00719:         continue;
00720:       }
00721: 
00722:       const g = guessPrizeGrupo(p);
00723:       if (!Number.isFinite(Number(g)) || Number(g) <= 0) continue;
00724: 
00725:       const gg = Number(g);
00726:       const prev = last.get(gg);
00727: 
00728:       if (!Number.isFinite(prev) || ts > prev) {
00729:         last.set(gg, ts);
00730:       }
00731:     }
00732:   }
00733: 
00734:   return last;
00735: }

00737: export function countAparicoesByGrupoInDraw(draw) {
00738:   const counts = new Map();
00739:   const ps = Array.isArray(draw?.prizes) ? draw.prizes : [];
00740:   for (const p of ps) {
00741:     const pos = guessPrizePos(p);
00742:     if (!Number.isFinite(Number(pos)) || Number(pos) < 1 || Number(pos) > 5) {
00743:       continue;
00744:     }
00745:     const g = guessPrizeGrupo(p);
00746:     if (!Number.isFinite(Number(g)) || Number(g) <= 0) continue;
00747:     const gg = Number(g);
00748:     counts.set(gg, (counts.get(gg) || 0) + 1);
00749:   }
00750:   return counts;
00751: }
00752: 
00753: function getFirstGrupoFromDraw(draw) {
00754:   const ps = Array.isArray(draw?.prizes) ? draw.prizes : [];
00755:   const p1 = ps.find((p) => guessPrizePos(p) === 1) || null;
00756:   if (p1) {
00757:     const g = guessPrizeGrupo(p1);
00758:     return Number.isFinite(Number(g)) ? Number(g) : null;
00759:   }
00760:   return pickPrize1GrupoFromDraw(draw);
00761: }
00762: 
00763: function getPrizeGroupsByPosition(draw, maxPosition = 3) {
00764:   const limit = Math.max(1, Number(maxPosition || 3));
00765:   const prizes = Array.isArray(draw?.prizes) ? draw.prizes : [];
00766: 
00767:   return Array.from({ length: limit }, (_, index) => {
00768:     const position = index + 1;
00769: 
00770:     const prize =
00771:       prizes.find(
00772:         (item) => Number(guessPrizePos(item)) === position
00773:       ) || null;
00774: 
00775:     if (!prize) return null;
00776: 
00777:     const grupo = Number(guessPrizeGrupo(prize));
00778: 
00779:     return Number.isFinite(grupo) &&
00780:       grupo >= 1 &&
00781:       grupo <= TOP3_GROUPS_K
00782:       ? grupo
00783:       : null;
00784:   });
00785: }
00786: 
00787: 
00788: function getAllPrizePresenceGroups(draw) {
00789:   const out = [];
00790:   const ps = Array.isArray(draw?.prizes) ? draw.prizes : [];
00791:   for (const p of ps) {
00792:     const pos = guessPrizePos(p);
00793:     if (!Number.isFinite(Number(pos)) || Number(pos) < 1 || Number(pos) > 5) {
00794:       continue;
00795:     }
00796:     const g = guessPrizeGrupo(p);
00797:     if (!Number.isFinite(Number(g)) || Number(g) <= 0) continue;
00798:     out.push({ pos: Number(pos), grupo: Number(g) });
00799:   }
00800:   return out.sort((a, b) => a.pos - b.pos);
00801: }
00802: 
00803: function buildFreqAndFirstMaps(nextDraw) {
00804:   const freq = new Map();
00805:   const firstMap = new Map();
00806:   const items = getAllPrizePresenceGroups(nextDraw);
00807: 
00808:   for (const it of items) {
00809:     freq.set(it.grupo, (freq.get(it.grupo) || 0) + 1);
00810:     if (it.pos === 1) {
00811:       firstMap.set(it.grupo, (firstMap.get(it.grupo) || 0) + 1);
00812:     }
00813:   }
00814: 
00815:   return { freq, firstMap };
00816: }
00817: 
00818: function mergeMapsAdd(target, source) {
00819:   for (const [k, v] of source.entries()) {
00820:     target.set(Number(k), Number(target.get(Number(k)) || 0) + Number(v || 0));
00821:   }
00822: }
00823: 
00824: function probFromFreq(freq, samples, groupsK = TOP3_GROUPS_K) {
00825:   const k = safeInt(groupsK, 25);
00826:   const denom = Math.max(1, Number(samples || 0) * 5);
00827:   const out = new Map();
00828: 
00829:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) {
00830:     const n = Number(freq.get(g) || 0);
00831:     out.set(g, n / denom);
00832:   }
00833: 
00834:   return out;
00835: }
00836: 
00837: function computeStructuralBaseDistribution(
00838:   draws,
00839:   lotteryKey,
00840:   targetHour,
00841:   targetDow,
00842:   groupsK = TOP3_GROUPS_K
00843: ) {
00844:   const list = Array.isArray(draws) ? draws : [];
00845:   const key = safeStr(lotteryKey).toUpperCase();
00846:   const target = toHourBucket(targetHour);
00847:   const k = safeInt(groupsK, 25);
00848: 
00849:   const freq = new Map();
00850:   let totalSamples = 0;
00851: 
00852:   for (const d of list) {
00853:     const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));
00854:     const y = pickDrawYMD(d);
00855:     if (!h || !y) continue;
00856: 
00857:     if (key === "FEDERAL") {
00858:       if (!isFederalDrawDay(y)) continue;
00859:       if (Number(getDowKey(y)) !== Number(targetDow)) continue;
00860:     } else {
00861:       if (h !== target) continue;
00862:     }
00863: 
00864:     const items = getAllPrizePresenceGroups(d);
00865:     if (!items.length) continue;
00866: 
00867:     totalSamples += 1;
00868: 
00869:     for (const it of items) {
00870:       const g = Number(it.grupo);
00871:       if (!Number.isFinite(g) || g < 1 || g > k) continue;
00872:       freq.set(g, Number(freq.get(g) || 0) + 1);
00873:     }
00874:   }
00875: 
00876:   const out = new Map();
00877:   const denom = Math.max(1, totalSamples * 5);
00878: 
00879:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) {
00880:     const n = Number(freq.get(g) || 0);
00881:     out.set(g, n / denom);
00882:   }
00883: 
00884:   return { scoreProb: out, totalSamples };
00885: }

00887: /* =========================
00888:    Recência / pressão estrutural
00889: ========================= */
00890: 
00891: function sortDrawsAsc(draws) {
00892:   const list = Array.isArray(draws) ? draws : [];
00893:   return [...list].sort((a, b) => {
00894:     const ya = pickDrawYMD(a);
00895:     const yb = pickDrawYMD(b);
00896:     const ha = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(a));
00897:     const hb = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(b));
00898:     const tsa = ymdHourToTs(ya, ha);
00899:     const tsb = ymdHourToTs(yb, hb);
00900:     return tsa - tsb;
00901:   });
00902: }
00903: 
00904: function getRecentDrawsBefore(draws, drawLast, count = 6) {
00905:   const list = sortDrawsAsc(draws);
00906:   const lastY = pickDrawYMD(drawLast);
00907:   const lastH = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(drawLast));
00908:   const lastTs = ymdHourToTs(lastY, lastH);
00909: 
00910:   const prev = list.filter((d) => {
00911:     const y = pickDrawYMD(d);
00912:     const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));
00913:     const ts = ymdHourToTs(y, h);
00914:     return Number.isFinite(ts) && ts < lastTs;
00915:   });
00916: 
00917:   return prev.slice(-Math.max(1, Number(count || 6)));
00918: }
00919: 
00920: function computeRecentPressureMetrics(recentDraws, groupsK = TOP3_GROUPS_K) {
00921:   const k = safeInt(groupsK, 25);
00922:   const out = new Map();
00923: 
00924:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) {
00925:     out.set(g, {
00926:       recentPrizePresence: 0,
00927:       recentIndirect: 0,
00928:       recentFirst: 0,
00929:       recentLast1First: 0,
00930:       recentLast2PrizePresence: 0,
00931:     });
00932:   }
00933: 
00934:   const list = Array.isArray(recentDraws) ? recentDraws : [];
00935:   const last1 = list.length ? [list[list.length - 1]] : [];
00936:   const last2 = list.slice(-2);
00937: 
00938:   for (const d of list) {
00939:     const items = getAllPrizePresenceGroups(d);
00940: 
00941:     for (const it of items) {
00942:       const g = Number(it.grupo);
00943:       const pos = Number(it.pos);
00944:       if (!Number.isFinite(g) || g < 1 || g > k) continue;
00945: 
00946:       const row = out.get(g);
00947:       row.recentPrizePresence += 1;
00948: 
00949:       if (pos >= 2 && pos <= 4) row.recentIndirect += 1;
00950:       if (pos === 1) row.recentFirst += 1;
00951:     }
00952:   }
00953: 
00954:   for (const d of last1) {
00955:     const items = getAllPrizePresenceGroups(d);
00956:     for (const it of items) {
00957:       const g = Number(it.grupo);
00958:       const pos = Number(it.pos);
00959:       if (!Number.isFinite(g) || g < 1 || g > k) continue;
00960:       if (pos === 1) {
00961:         out.get(g).recentLast1First += 1;
00962:       }
00963:     }
00964:   }
00965: 
00966:   for (const d of last2) {
00967:     const items = getAllPrizePresenceGroups(d);
00968:     const seen = new Set();
00969:     for (const it of items) {
00970:       const g = Number(it.grupo);
00971:       if (!Number.isFinite(g) || g < 1 || g > k) continue;
00972:       if (seen.has(g)) continue;
00973:       seen.add(g);
00974:       out.get(g).recentLast2PrizePresence += 1;
00975:     }
00976:   }
00977: 
00978:   return out;
00979: }
00980: 
00981: function normalizeMetric(value, maxValue) {
00982:   const v = Number(value || 0);
00983:   const m = Number(maxValue || 0);
00984:   if (!Number.isFinite(v) || !Number.isFinite(m) || m <= 0) return 0;
00985:   return Math.max(0, Math.min(1, v / m));
00986: }
00987: 
00988: function clamp01(v) {
00989:   const n = Number(v);
00990:   if (!Number.isFinite(n)) return 0;
00991:   return Math.max(0, Math.min(1, n));
00992: }
00993: 
00994: function buildZeroProbMap(groupsK = TOP3_GROUPS_K) {
00995:   const k = safeInt(groupsK, 25);
00996:   return new Map(Array.from({ length: k }, (_, i) => [i + 1, 0]));
00997: }
00998: 
00999: function confidenceFromSamples(samples, fullConfidenceAt = 8) {
01000:   const n = Number(samples || 0);
01001:   const lim = Math.max(1, Number(fullConfidenceAt || 8));
01002:   return clamp01(n / lim);
01003: }

01009: const LAYER_MIN_SAMPLES = {
01010:   DOM_DOW_PREVH_PREVG_TO_TARGET: 3,
01011:   DOW_PREVH_PREVG_TO_TARGET: 5,
01012:   PREVH_PREVG_TO_TARGET: 8,
01013:   DOM_TARGET: 8,
01014:   DOW_TARGET: 12,
01015:   TARGET_ONLY: 20,
01016: 
01017:   FED_DOW_PREVG: 3,
01018:   FED_PREVG: 4,
01019:   FED_DOW_ONLY: 6,
01020:   FED_ONLY: 10,

01023: function buildLayerConfigs({
01024:   lotteryKey,
01025:   targetDayOfMonth,
01026:   targetDow,
01027:   prevHour,
01028:   prevGrupo,
01029:   targetHour,
01030: }) {
01031:   const key = safeStr(lotteryKey).toUpperCase();
01032: 
01033:   if (key === "FEDERAL") {
01034:     return [
01035:       {
01036:         key: "FED_DOW_PREVG",
01037:         label: "Federal: dia da semana + grupo anterior",
01038:         minSamples: LAYER_MIN_SAMPLES.FED_DOW_PREVG,
01039:         match: (ctx) =>
01040:           ctx.targetDow === targetDow &&
01041:           ctx.prevGrupo === prevGrupo,
01042:       },
01043:       {
01044:         key: "FED_PREVG",
01045:         label: "Federal: grupo anterior",
01046:         minSamples: LAYER_MIN_SAMPLES.FED_PREVG,
01047:         match: (ctx) => ctx.prevGrupo === prevGrupo,
01048:       },
01049:       {
01050:         key: "FED_DOW_ONLY",
01051:         label: "Federal: dia da semana",
01052:         minSamples: LAYER_MIN_SAMPLES.FED_DOW_ONLY,
01053:         match: (ctx) => ctx.targetDow === targetDow,
01054:       },
01055:       {
01056:         key: "FED_ONLY",
01057:         label: "Federal: histórico geral",
01058:         minSamples: LAYER_MIN_SAMPLES.FED_ONLY,
01059:         match: () => true,
01060:       },
01061:     ];
01062:   }
01063: 
01064:   return [
01065:     {
01066:       key: "DOM_DOW_PREVH_PREVG_TO_TARGET",
01067:       label: "dia do mês + dia da semana + hora anterior + grupo anterior -> alvo",
01068:       minSamples: LAYER_MIN_SAMPLES.DOM_DOW_PREVH_PREVG_TO_TARGET,
01069:       match: (ctx) =>
01070:         ctx.targetDayOfMonth === targetDayOfMonth &&
01071:         ctx.targetDow === targetDow &&
01072:         ctx.prevHour === prevHour &&
01073:         ctx.prevGrupo === prevGrupo &&
01074:         ctx.targetHour === targetHour,
01075:     },
01076:     {
01077:       key: "DOW_PREVH_PREVG_TO_TARGET",
01078:       label: "dia da semana + hora anterior + grupo anterior -> alvo",
01079:       minSamples: LAYER_MIN_SAMPLES.DOW_PREVH_PREVG_TO_TARGET,
01080:       match: (ctx) =>
01081:         ctx.targetDow === targetDow &&
01082:         ctx.prevHour === prevHour &&
01083:         ctx.prevGrupo === prevGrupo &&
01084:         ctx.targetHour === targetHour,
01085:     },
01086:     {
01087:       key: "PREVH_PREVG_TO_TARGET",
01088:       label: "hora anterior + grupo anterior -> alvo",
01089:       minSamples: LAYER_MIN_SAMPLES.PREVH_PREVG_TO_TARGET,
01090:       match: (ctx) =>
01091:         ctx.prevHour === prevHour &&
01092:         ctx.prevGrupo === prevGrupo &&
01093:         ctx.targetHour === targetHour,
01094:     },
01095:     {
01096:       key: "DOM_TARGET",
01097:       label: "dia do mês + alvo",
01098:       minSamples: LAYER_MIN_SAMPLES.DOM_TARGET,
01099:       match: (ctx) =>
01100:         ctx.targetDayOfMonth === targetDayOfMonth &&
01101:         ctx.targetHour === targetHour,
01102:     },
01103:     {
01104:       key: "DOW_TARGET",
01105:       label: "dia da semana + alvo",
01106:       minSamples: LAYER_MIN_SAMPLES.DOW_TARGET,
01107:       match: (ctx) =>
01108:         ctx.targetDow === targetDow &&
01109:         ctx.targetHour === targetHour,
01110:     },
01111:     {
01112:       key: "TARGET_ONLY",
01113:       label: "alvo geral",
01114:       minSamples: LAYER_MIN_SAMPLES.TARGET_ONLY,
01115:       match: (ctx) => ctx.targetHour === targetHour,
01116:     },
01117:   ];
01118: }

01120: function buildConditionalLayerDistribution({
01121:   lotteryKey,
01122:   drawsRange,
01123:   targetHour,
01124:   targetDow,
01125:   targetDayOfMonth,
01126:   prevHour,
01127:   prevGrupo,
01128:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
01129:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
01130:   FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
01131: }) {
01132:   const list = Array.isArray(drawsRange) ? drawsRange : [];
01133:   const drawsIndex = indexDrawsByYmdHour(list);
01134: 
01135:   const layers = buildLayerConfigs({
01136:     lotteryKey,
01137:     targetDayOfMonth,
01138:     targetDow,
01139:     prevHour,
01140:     prevGrupo,
01141:     targetHour,
01142:   });
01143: 
01144:   const layerResults = layers.map((layer) => ({
01145:     key: layer.key,
01146:     label: layer.label,
01147:     minSamples: layer.minSamples,
01148:     samples: 0,
01149:     freq: new Map(),
01150:     firstFreq: new Map(),
01151:     matchingDraws: [],
01152:   }));
01153: 
01154:   for (const d of list) {
01155:     const prevY = pickDrawYMD(d);
01156:     const prevH = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));
01157:     if (!isYMD(prevY) || !prevH) continue;
01158: 
01159:     const prevG = getFirstGrupoFromDraw(d);
01160:     if (!Number.isFinite(Number(prevG)) || Number(prevG) <= 0) continue;
01161: 
01162:     const ns = getNextSlotForLottery({
01163:       lotteryKey,
01164:       ymd: prevY,
01165:       hourBucket: prevH,
01166:       PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
01167:       PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
01168:       FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
01169:     });
01170: 
01171:     if (!ns?.ymd || !ns?.hour) continue;
01172: 
01173:     const found = findNextExistingDrawFromSlot({
01174:       lotteryKey,
01175:       startSlot: { ymd: ns.ymd, hour: ns.hour },
01176:       drawsIndex,
01177:       PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
01178:       PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
01179:       FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
01180:     });
01181: 
01182:     const nextDraw = found?.draw || null;
01183:     const nextSlot = found?.slot || null;
01184:     if (!nextDraw || !nextSlot?.ymd || !nextSlot?.hour) continue;
01185: 
01186:     const ctx = {
01187:       prevYmd: prevY,
01188:       prevHour: prevH,
01189:       prevGrupo: Number(prevG),
01190:       targetYmd: safeStr(nextSlot.ymd),
01191:       targetHour: toHourBucket(nextSlot.hour),
01192:       targetDow: getDowKey(nextSlot.ymd),
01193:       targetDayOfMonth: getDayOfMonth(nextSlot.ymd),
01194:     };
01195: 
01196:     const nextMaps = buildFreqAndFirstMaps(nextDraw);
01197: 
01198:     for (let i = 0; i < layers.length; i += 1) {
01199:       const layer = layers[i];
01200:       if (!layer.match(ctx)) continue;
01201: 
01202:       layerResults[i].samples += 1;
01203:       layerResults[i].matchingDraws.push(nextDraw);
01204:       mergeMapsAdd(layerResults[i].freq, nextMaps.freq);
01205:       mergeMapsAdd(layerResults[i].firstFreq, nextMaps.firstMap);
01206:     }
01207:   }
01208: 
01209:   let chosen =
01210:     layerResults.find((x) => x.samples >= x.minSamples && x.freq.size > 0) ||
01211:     layerResults.find((x) => x.samples > 0 && x.freq.size > 0) ||
01212:     null;
01213: 
01214:   if (!chosen) {
01215:     chosen = {
01216:       key: "NONE",
01217:       label: "sem amostra",
01218:       minSamples: 0,
01219:       samples: 0,
01220:       freq: new Map(),
01221:       firstFreq: new Map(),
01222:       matchingDraws: [],
01223:     };
01224:   }
01225: 
01226:   return {
01227:     chosen,
01228:     layers: layerResults,
01229:   };
01230: }
01231: 
01232: /* =========================
01233:    Motor principal V1
01234: ========================= */
01235: 
01236: export function computeConditionalNextTop3({
01237:   lotteryKey,
01238:   drawsRange,
01239:   drawLast,
01240:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
01241:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
01242:   FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
01243:   topN = 3,
01244:   targetYmdOverride = "",
01245:   targetHourOverride = "",
01246: }) {
01247:   const list = Array.isArray(drawsRange) ? drawsRange : [];
01248:   if (!list.length || !drawLast) {
01249:     return { top: [], meta: null };
01250:   }
01251: 
01252:   const key = safeStr(lotteryKey).toUpperCase();
01253: 
01254:   const lastY = pickDrawYMD(drawLast);
01255:   const lastH = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(drawLast));
01256:   const prevGrupo = getFirstGrupoFromDraw(drawLast);
01257: 
01258:   if (
01259:     !isYMD(lastY) ||
01260:     !lastH ||
01261:     !Number.isFinite(Number(prevGrupo)) ||
01262:     Number(prevGrupo) <= 0
01263:   ) {
01264:     return { top: [], meta: null };
01265:   }
01266: 
01267:   const nextSlot = getNextSlotForLottery({
01268:     lotteryKey,
01269:     ymd: lastY,
01270:     hourBucket: lastH,
01271:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
01272:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
01273:     FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
01274:   });
01275: 
01276:   const forcedTargetY = safeStr(targetYmdOverride);
01277:   const forcedTargetH = toHourBucket(targetHourOverride);
01278: 
01279:   const targetY = isYMD(forcedTargetY) && forcedTargetH
01280:     ? forcedTargetY
01281:     : safeStr(nextSlot?.ymd);
01282: 
01283:   const targetH = isYMD(forcedTargetY) && forcedTargetH
01284:     ? forcedTargetH
01285:     : toHourBucket(nextSlot?.hour);
01286:   const targetDow = getDowKey(targetY);
01287:   const targetDayOfMonth = getDayOfMonth(targetY);
01288:   const transition = `${lastH}->${targetH}`;
01289: 
01290:   const useFirstFocusedRanking =
01291:     key !== "FEDERAL" &&
01292:     (transition === "11h->14h" || transition === "14h->16h");
01293: 
01294:   if (!isYMD(targetY) || !targetH || !Number.isFinite(targetDow)) {
01295:     return { top: [], meta: null };
01296:   }
01297: 
01298:   const layerOut = buildConditionalLayerDistribution({
01299:     lotteryKey,
01300:     drawsRange: list,
01301:     targetHour: targetH,
01302:     targetDow,
01303:     targetDayOfMonth,
01304:     prevHour: lastH,
01305:     prevGrupo: Number(prevGrupo),
01306:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
01307:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
01308:     FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
01309:   });
01310: 
01311:   const chosen = layerOut.chosen;
01312:   const samples = Number(chosen?.samples || 0);
01313:   const freq = chosen?.freq || new Map();
01314:   const firstFreq = chosen?.firstFreq || new Map();
01315:   const probCond = probFromFreq(freq, samples, TOP3_GROUPS_K);
01316: 
01317:   const structural = computeStructuralBaseDistribution(
01318:     list,
01319:     key,
01320:     targetH,
01321:     targetDow,
01322:     TOP3_GROUPS_K
01323:   );
01324:   const probBase = structural?.scoreProb || new Map();
01325: 
01326:   const condWeight =
01327:     key === "FEDERAL"
01328:       ? samples >= 8
01329:         ? 0.55
01330:         : samples >= 4
01331:           ? 0.4
01332:           : 0.25
01333:       : samples >= 12
01334:         ? 0.65
01335:         : samples >= 6
01336:           ? 0.55
01337:           : 0.45;
01338: 
01339:   const baseWeight = 1 - condWeight;
01340: 
01341:   const lastSeen = computeLastSeenByGrupo(list);
01342:   const nowTs = ymdHourToTs(lastY, lastH);
01343: 
01344:   const finiteLastSeen = Array.from(lastSeen.values()).filter((v) =>
01345:     Number.isFinite(Number(v))
01346:   );
01347:   const minLastSeenTs = finiteLastSeen.length ? Math.min(...finiteLastSeen) : nowTs;
01348:   const maxGapMsBase =
01349:     Number.isFinite(nowTs) && Number.isFinite(minLastSeenTs)
01350:       ? Math.max(1, nowTs - minLastSeenTs)
01351:       : 1;
01352: 
01353:   const recentDraws = getRecentDrawsBefore(list, drawLast, 6);
01354:   const recentMetrics = computeRecentPressureMetrics(recentDraws, TOP3_GROUPS_K);
01355: 
01356:   const recentMaxPrizePresence = Math.max(
01357:     1,
01358:     ...Array.from(recentMetrics.values()).map((x) => Number(x.recentPrizePresence || 0))
01359:   );
01360:   const recentMaxIndirect = Math.max(
01361:     1,
01362:     ...Array.from(recentMetrics.values()).map((x) => Number(x.recentIndirect || 0))
01363:   );
01364:   const recentMaxFirst = Math.max(
01365:     1,
01366:     ...Array.from(recentMetrics.values()).map((x) => Number(x.recentFirst || 0))
01367:   );
01368: 
01369:   const ranked = Array.from({ length: safeInt(TOP3_GROUPS_K, 25) }, (_, idx) => {
01370:     const grupo = idx + 1;
01371:     const aparicoes = Number(freq.get(grupo) || 0);
01372:     const primeiros = Number(firstFreq.get(grupo) || 0);
01373:     const pCond = Number(probCond.get(grupo) || 0);
01374:     const pBase = Number(probBase.get(grupo) || 0);
01375:     const pFinal = (pCond * condWeight) + (pBase * baseWeight);
01376: 
01377:     const ls = lastSeen.get(grupo);
01378:     const lastSeenTs = Number.isFinite(ls) ? ls : Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY;
01379: 
01380:     const gapMs =
01381:       Number.isFinite(nowTs) &&
01382:       Number.isFinite(lastSeenTs) &&
01383:       lastSeenTs !== Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY
01384:         ? Math.max(0, nowTs - lastSeenTs)
01385:         : maxGapMsBase;
01386: 
01387:     const taxaPrimeiro = samples > 0 ? primeiros / samples : 0;
01388:     const lateBonusRaw = Math.max(0, gapMs) / Math.max(1, maxGapMsBase);
01389:     const lateBonus = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, lateBonusRaw));
01390: 
01391:     const rm = recentMetrics.get(grupo) || {
01392:       recentPrizePresence: 0,
01393:       recentIndirect: 0,
01394:       recentFirst: 0,
01395:       recentLast1First: 0,
01396:       recentLast2PrizePresence: 0,
01397:     };
01398: 
01399:     const recentPrizePresenceNorm = normalizeMetric(rm.recentPrizePresence, recentMaxPrizePresence);
01400:     const recentIndirectNorm = normalizeMetric(rm.recentIndirect, recentMaxIndirect);
01401:     const recentFirstNorm = normalizeMetric(rm.recentFirst, recentMaxFirst);
01402:     const recentLast1FirstNorm = normalizeMetric(rm.recentLast1First, 1);
01403:     const recentLast2PrizePresenceNorm = normalizeMetric(rm.recentLast2PrizePresence, 2);
01404: 
01405:     const recencyCoolingFactor =
01406:       rm.recentLast1First > 0
01407:         ? 0.72
01408:         : rm.recentFirst >= 2
01409:           ? 0.84
01410:           : 1.0;
01411: 
01412:     const smartLateBoost =
01413:       lateBonus *
01414:       (0.45 + (recentIndirectNorm * 0.35) + (recentLast2PrizePresenceNorm * 0.2)) *
01415:       recencyCoolingFactor;
01416: 
01417:     const recentComposite =
01418:       (recentPrizePresenceNorm * 0.45) +
01419:       (recentIndirectNorm * 0.35) +
01420:       (recentFirstNorm * 0.2);
01421: 
01422:     const baseScore =
01423:       key === "FEDERAL"
01424:         ? (pFinal * 145) +
01425:           (pBase * 85) +
01426:           (primeiros * 42) +
01427:           (aparicoes * 18) +
01428:           (recentComposite * 26) +
01429:           (smartLateBoost * 10)
01430:         : useFirstFocusedRanking
01431:           ? (pFinal * 125) +
01432:             (aparicoes * 155) +
01433:             (primeiros * 70) +
01434:             (taxaPrimeiro * 34) +
01435:             (recentComposite * 18) +
01436:             (recentLast1FirstNorm * 10) +
01437:             (smartLateBoost * 16)
01438:           : (pFinal * 125) +
01439:             (aparicoes * 135) +
01440:             (primeiros * 62) +
01441:             (recentComposite * 24) +
01442:             (recentLast1FirstNorm * 8) +
01443:             (smartLateBoost * 18);
01444: 
01445:     return {
01446:       grupo,
01447:       aparicoes,
01448:       primeiros,
01449:       taxaPrimeiro,
01450:       scoreProb: pFinal,
01451:       probCond: pCond,
01452:       probBase: pBase,
01453:       score: baseScore,
01454:       lateBonus,
01455:       smartLateBoost,
01456:       recentPrizePresence: rm.recentPrizePresence,
01457:       recentIndirect: rm.recentIndirect,
01458:       recentFirst: rm.recentFirst,
01459:       recentLast1First: rm.recentLast1First,
01460:       recentLast2PrizePresence: rm.recentLast2PrizePresence,
01461:       recentComposite,
01462:       lastSeenTs,
01463:       gapMs,
01464:     };
01465:   })
01466:     .sort((a, b) => {
01467:       if (b.score !== a.score) return b.score - a.score;
01468:       if (b.scoreProb !== a.scoreProb) return b.scoreProb - a.scoreProb;
01469:       if (b.primeiros !== a.primeiros) return b.primeiros - a.primeiros;
01470:       if (b.aparicoes !== a.aparicoes) return b.aparicoes - a.aparicoes;
01471:       return a.grupo - b.grupo;
01472:     })
01473:     .slice(0, Math.max(1, Number(topN || 3)));
01474: 
01475:   const reasonsBase = [
01476:     `Camada usada: ${chosen?.label || "—"}`,
01477:     `Amostra histórica condicional: ${samples}`,
01478:     `Amostra estrutural: ${Number(structural?.totalSamples || 0)}`,
01479:     `Pesos: condicional=${(condWeight * 100).toFixed(0)}% | estrutural=${(baseWeight * 100).toFixed(0)}%`,
01480:     `Estado atual: prev=${String(prevGrupo).padStart(2, "0")} @ ${lastH} → alvo ${targetH}`,
01481:     `Data alvo: ${targetY} | DOW=${targetDow} | dia=${String(targetDayOfMonth).padStart(2, "0")}`,
01482:   ];
01483: 
01484:   const top = ranked.map((x, idx) => {
01485:     const g2 = String(x.grupo).padStart(2, "0");
01486:     const pct = (x.scoreProb * 100).toFixed(2);
01487:     const pctCond = (x.probCond * 100).toFixed(2);
01488:     const pctBase = (x.probBase * 100).toFixed(2);
01489: 
01490:     return {
01491:       rank: idx + 1,
01492:       title:
01493:         idx === 0
01494:           ? "Mais provável"
01495:           : idx === 1
01496:             ? "2º mais provável"
01497:             : "3º mais provável",
01498:       grupo: x.grupo,
01499:       scoreProb: Number(x.scoreProb || 0),
01500:       probCond: Number(x.probCond || 0),
01501:       probBase: Number(x.probBase || 0),
01502:       lateBonus: Number(x.lateBonus || 0),
01503:       freq: x.aparicoes,
01504:       freqCond: x.aparicoes,
01505:       freqBase: 0,
01506:       freqZeroWhy:
01507:         x.aparicoes <= 0
01508:           ? `Sem ocorrência nesta camada (${chosen?.label || "—"}).`
01509:           : "",
01510:       reasons: [
01511:         ...reasonsBase,
01512:         `Grupo G${g2}: aparições=${x.aparicoes} em ${samples} amostras do próximo sorteio`,
01513:         `Grupo G${g2}: primeiros lugares=${x.primeiros}`,
01514:         `Probabilidade final estimada no TOP3: ${pct}%`,
01515:         `Composição da probabilidade: condicional=${pctCond}% | estrutural=${pctBase}%`,
01516:         `Presença indireta recente (2º-4º): ${x.recentIndirect}`,
01517:         `Persistência curta recente: ${x.recentPrizePresence}`,
01518:         `Bônus de atraso ajustado: ${(Number(x.smartLateBoost || 0) * 100).toFixed(2)}%`,
01519:       ],
01520:       meta: {
01521:         trigger: {
01522:           ymd: lastY,
01523:           hour: lastH,
01524:           grupo: Number(prevGrupo),
01525:         },
01526:         next: {
01527:           ymd: safeStr(targetY),
01528:           hour: safeStr(targetH),
01529:         },
01530:         samples,
01531:         scenario: chosen?.key || "NONE",
01532:         explain: {
01533:           layerKey: chosen?.key || "NONE",
01534:           layerLabel: chosen?.label || "—",
01535:           layerSamples: samples,
01536:           baseSamples: Number(structural?.totalSamples || 0),
01537:           condWeight,
01538:           baseWeight,
01539:           targetDow,
01540:           targetDayOfMonth,
01541:           prevHour: lastH,
01542:           prevGrupo: Number(prevGrupo),
01543:           recentPrizePresence: x.recentPrizePresence,
01544:           recentIndirect: x.recentIndirect,
01545:           recentFirst: x.recentFirst,
01546:           recentLast1First: x.recentLast1First,
01547:           recentLast2PrizePresence: x.recentLast2PrizePresence,
01548:           allLayers: (layerOut?.layers || []).map((layer) => ({
01549:             key: layer.key,
01550:             label: layer.label,
01551:             samples: layer.samples,
01552:             minSamples: layer.minSamples,
01553:           })),
01554:         },
01555:       },
01556:     };
01557:   });
01558: 
01559:   return {
01560:     top,
01561:     meta: {
01562:       trigger: {
01563:         ymd: lastY,
01564:         hour: lastH,
01565:         grupo: Number(prevGrupo),
01566:       },
01567:       next: {
01568:         ymd: safeStr(targetY),
01569:         hour: safeStr(targetH),
01570:       },
01571:       samples,
01572:       scenario: chosen?.key || "NONE",
01573:       explain: {
01574:         layerKey: chosen?.key || "NONE",
01575:         layerLabel: chosen?.label || "—",
01576:         layerSamples: samples,
01577:         baseSamples: Number(structural?.totalSamples || 0),
01578:         condWeight,
01579:         baseWeight,
01580:         targetDow,
01581:         targetDayOfMonth,
01582:         prevHour: lastH,
01583:         prevGrupo: Number(prevGrupo),
01584:         allLayers: (layerOut?.layers || []).map((layer) => ({
01585:           key: layer.key,
01586:           label: layer.label,
01587:           samples: layer.samples,
01588:           minSamples: layer.minSamples,
01589:         })),
01590:       },
01591:     },
01592:   };
01593: }
01594: 
01595: /* =========================
01596:    V2 — helpers focados em BICHO
01597: ========================= */
01598: 
01599: function computeStructuralFirstDistribution(
01600:   draws,
01601:   lotteryKey,
01602:   targetHour,
01603:   targetDow,
01604:   groupsK = TOP3_GROUPS_K
01605: ) {
01606:   const list = Array.isArray(draws) ? draws : [];
01607:   const key = safeStr(lotteryKey).toUpperCase();
01608:   const target = toHourBucket(targetHour);
01609:   const k = safeInt(groupsK, 25);
01610: 
01611:   const firstFreq = new Map();
01612:   let totalSamples = 0;
01613: 
01614:   for (const d of list) {
01615:     const y = pickDrawYMD(d);
01616:     const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));
01617:     if (!isYMD(y) || !h) continue;
01618: 
01619:     if (key === "FEDERAL") {
01620:       if (!isFederalDrawDay(y)) continue;
01621:       if (Number(getDowKey(y)) !== Number(targetDow)) continue;
01622:     } else {
01623:       if (h !== target) continue;
01624:     }
01625: 
01626:     const g = getFirstGrupoFromDraw(d);
01627:     if (!Number.isFinite(Number(g)) || Number(g) < 1 || Number(g) > k) continue;
01628: 
01629:     totalSamples += 1;
01630:     firstFreq.set(Number(g), Number(firstFreq.get(Number(g)) || 0) + 1);
01631:   }
01632: 
01633:   const prob = new Map();
01634:   const denom = Math.max(1, totalSamples);
01635: 
01636:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) {
01637:     prob.set(g, Number(firstFreq.get(g) || 0) / denom);
01638:   }
01639: 
01640:   return { prob, firstFreq, totalSamples };
01641: }
01642: 
01643: function getComparableDrawsForTargetContext(
01644:   draws,
01645:   lotteryKey,
01646:   targetHour,
01647:   targetDow
01648: ) {
01649:   const list = sortDrawsAsc(draws);
01650:   const key = safeStr(lotteryKey).toUpperCase();
01651:   const target = toHourBucket(targetHour);
01652: 
01653:   return list.filter((d) => {
01654:     const y = pickDrawYMD(d);
01655:     const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));
01656:     if (!isYMD(y) || !h) return false;
01657: 
01658:     if (key === "FEDERAL") {
01659:       return isFederalDrawDay(y) && Number(getDowKey(y)) === Number(targetDow);
01660:     }
01661: 
01662:     return h === target;
01663:   });
01664: }
01665: 
01666: function buildComparableFirstSequence(
01667:   draws,
01668:   lotteryKey,
01669:   targetHour,
01670:   targetDow
01671: ) {
01672:   return getComparableDrawsForTargetContext(
01673:     draws,
01674:     lotteryKey,
01675:     targetHour,
01676:     targetDow
01677:   )
01678:     .map((d) => {
01679:       const y = pickDrawYMD(d);
01680:       const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));
01681:       const grupo = getFirstGrupoFromDraw(d);
01682:       return {
01683:         draw: d,
01684:         ymd: y,
01685:         hour: h,
01686:         ts: ymdHourToTs(y, h),
01687:         grupo: Number(grupo),
01688:       };
01689:     })
01690:     .filter((x) =>
01691:       isYMD(x.ymd) &&
01692:       x.hour &&
01693:       Number.isFinite(Number(x.ts)) &&
01694:       Number.isFinite(Number(x.grupo)) &&
01695:       Number(x.grupo) >= 1 &&
01696:       Number(x.grupo) <= 25
01697:     );
01698: }
01699: 
01700: function getStateFromComparableSequenceBeforeTs(seq, beforeTs, count = 3) {
01701:   const list = Array.isArray(seq) ? seq : [];
01702:   const n = Math.max(1, Number(count || 3));
01703: 
01704:   return list
01705:     .filter((x) => Number.isFinite(Number(x.ts)) && Number(x.ts) < Number(beforeTs))
01706:     .slice(-n)
01707:     .reverse()
01708:     .map((x) => Number(x.grupo))
01709:     .filter((g) => Number.isFinite(g) && g >= 1 && g <= 25);
01710: }
01711: 
01712: function getComparableRecentDrawsBeforeTs(seq, beforeTs, count = 6) {
01713:   const list = Array.isArray(seq) ? seq : [];
01714:   return list
01715:     .filter((x) => Number.isFinite(Number(x.ts)) && Number(x.ts) < Number(beforeTs))
01716:     .slice(-Math.max(1, Number(count || 6)));
01717: }
01718: 
01719: function countLeadingStateMatches(currentState, histState) {
01720:   const a = Array.isArray(currentState) ? currentState : [];
01721:   const b = Array.isArray(histState) ? histState : [];
01722:   const len = Math.min(a.length, b.length);
01723: 
01724:   let matched = 0;
01725:   for (let i = 0; i < len; i += 1) {
01726:     if (Number(a[i]) !== Number(b[i])) break;
01727:     matched += 1;
01728:   }
01729:   return matched;
01730: }
01731: 
01732: function buildMemoryStateDistribution({
01733:   drawsRange,
01734:   lotteryKey,
01735:   targetHour,
01736:   targetDow,
01737:   currentState,
01738:   groupsK = TOP3_GROUPS_K,
01739: }) {
01740:   const seq = buildComparableFirstSequence(
01741:     drawsRange,
01742:     lotteryKey,
01743:     targetHour,
01744:     targetDow
01745:   );
01746: 
01747:   const k = safeInt(groupsK, 25);
01748:   const freq = new Map();
01749: 
01750:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) {
01751:     freq.set(g, 0);
01752:   }
01753: 
01754:   let totalWeight = 0;
01755:   let matchedSamples = 0;
01756: 
01757:   for (let i = 0; i < seq.length; i += 1) {
01758:     const target = seq[i];
01759:     const histState = [];
01760: 
01761:     for (let j = i - 1; j >= 0 && histState.length < 3; j -= 1) {
01762:       histState.push(Number(seq[j].grupo));
01763:     }
01764: 
01765:     const matchDepth = countLeadingStateMatches(currentState, histState);
01766:     if (matchDepth <= 0) continue;
01767: 
01768:     const weight =
01769:       matchDepth >= 3 ? 4.0 :
01770:       matchDepth === 2 ? 2.5 :
01771:       1.0;
01772: 
01773:     const g = Number(target.grupo);
01774:     if (!Number.isFinite(g) || g < 1 || g > k) continue;
01775: 
01776:     freq.set(g, Number(freq.get(g) || 0) + weight);
01777:     totalWeight += weight;
01778:     matchedSamples += 1;
01779:   }
01780: 
01781:   const prob = new Map();
01782:   const denom = Math.max(1, totalWeight);
01783: 
01784:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) {
01785:     prob.set(g, Number(freq.get(g) || 0) / denom);
01786:   }
01787: 
01788:   return {
01789:     prob,
01790:     freq,
01791:     totalWeight,
01792:     matchedSamples,
01793:     state: Array.isArray(currentState) ? currentState : [],
01794:     sequenceSamples: seq.length,
01795:   };
01796: }
01797: 
01798: function detectRegimeFromComparableSequence(seqRecent) {
01799:   const list = Array.isArray(seqRecent) ? seqRecent : [];
01800:   const groups = list
01801:     .map((x) => Number(x?.grupo))
01802:     .filter((g) => Number.isFinite(g) && g >= 1 && g <= 25);
01803: 
01804:   if (groups.length <= 1) {
01805:     return {
01806:       regime: "neutral",
01807:       repeatRate: 0,
01808:       uniqueRate: groups.length ? 1 : 0,
01809:       samples: groups.length,
01810:     };
01811:   }
01812: 
01813:   let repeats = 0;
01814:   for (let i = 1; i < groups.length; i += 1) {
01815:     if (groups[i] === groups[i - 1]) repeats += 1;
01816:   }
01817: 
01818:   const repeatRate = repeats / Math.max(1, groups.length - 1);
01819:   const uniqueRate = new Set(groups).size / Math.max(1, groups.length);
01820: 
01821:   let regime = "neutral";
01822: 
01823:   if (repeatRate >= 0.34 || uniqueRate <= 0.5) {
01824:     regime = "repeat";
01825:   } else if (repeatRate === 0 && uniqueRate >= 0.84) {
01826:     regime = "spread";
01827:   }
01828: 
01829:   return {
01830:     regime,
01831:     repeatRate,
01832:     uniqueRate,
01833:     samples: groups.length,
01834:   };
01835: }

01837: function computeComparablePrizePresenceDistribution(
01838:   draws,
01839:   lotteryKey,
01840:   targetHour,
01841:   targetDow,
01842:   groupsK = TOP3_GROUPS_K
01843: ) {
01844:   const list = getComparableDrawsForTargetContext(
01845:     draws,
01846:     lotteryKey,
01847:     targetHour,
01848:     targetDow
01849:   );
01850: 
01851:   const k = safeInt(groupsK, 25);
01852:   const freq = new Map();
01853:   let totalSamples = 0;
01854: 
01855:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) {
01856:     freq.set(g, 0);
01857:   }
01858: 
01859:   for (const d of list) {
01860:     const items = getAllPrizePresenceGroups(d);
01861:     if (!items.length) continue;
01862: 
01863:     totalSamples += 1;
01864: 
01865:     for (const it of items) {
01866:       const g = Number(it.grupo);
01867:       if (!Number.isFinite(g) || g < 1 || g > k) continue;
01868:       freq.set(g, Number(freq.get(g) || 0) + 1);
01869:     }
01870:   }
01871: 
01872:   const prob = new Map();
01873:   const denom = Math.max(1, totalSamples * 5);
01874: 
01875:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) {
01876:     prob.set(g, Number(freq.get(g) || 0) / denom);
01877:   }
01878: 
01879:   return { prob, freq, totalSamples };
01880: }

01882: function computeComparableDuplicationDistribution(
01883:   draws,
01884:   lotteryKey,
01885:   targetHour,
01886:   targetDow,
01887:   groupsK = TOP3_GROUPS_K
01888: ) {
01889:   const list = getComparableDrawsForTargetContext(
01890:     draws,
01891:     lotteryKey,
01892:     targetHour,
01893:     targetDow
01894:   );
01895: 
01896:   const k = safeInt(groupsK, 25);
01897:   const freq = new Map();
01898:   let totalSamples = 0;
01899: 
01900:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) {
01901:     freq.set(g, 0);
01902:   }
01903: 
01904:   for (const d of list) {
01905:     const counts = countAparicoesByGrupoInDraw(d);
01906:     if (!counts.size) continue;
01907: 
01908:     totalSamples += 1;
01909: 
01910:     for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) {
01911:       const c = Number(counts.get(g) || 0);
01912:       if (c >= 2) {
01913:         freq.set(g, Number(freq.get(g) || 0) + 1);
01914:       }
01915:     }
01916:   }
01917: 
01918:   const prob = new Map();
01919:   const denom = Math.max(1, totalSamples);
01920: 
01921:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) {
01922:     prob.set(g, Number(freq.get(g) || 0) / denom);
01923:   }
01924: 
01925:   return { prob, freq, totalSamples };
01926: }

01928: function getComparableContextDrawsBeforeTs(
01929:   draws,
01930:   lotteryKey,
01931:   targetHour,
01932:   targetDow,
01933:   beforeTs,
01934:   count = 8
01935: ) {
01936:   return getComparableDrawsForTargetContext(draws, lotteryKey, targetHour, targetDow)
01937:     .filter((d) => {
01938:       const y = pickDrawYMD(d);
01939:       const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));
01940:       const ts = ymdHourToTs(y, h);
01941:       return Number.isFinite(Number(ts)) && ts < Number(beforeTs);
01942:     })
01943:     .slice(-Math.max(1, Number(count || 8)));
01944: }

01946: function computeRecentBichoMetrics(draws, groupsK = TOP3_GROUPS_K) {
01947:   const k = safeInt(groupsK, 25);
01948:   const out = new Map();
01949: 
01950:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) {
01951:     out.set(g, {
01952:       recentPrizePresence: 0,
01953:       recentFirst: 0,
01954:       recentDupDraws: 0,
01955:       recentLast1PrizePresence: 0,
01956:       recentLast2PrizePresence: 0,
01957:       recentLast3PrizePresence: 0,
01958:     });
01959:   }
01960: 
01961:   const list = Array.isArray(draws) ? draws : [];
01962:   const last1 = list.length ? [list[list.length - 1]] : [];
01963:   const last2 = list.slice(-2);
01964:   const last3 = list.slice(-3);
01965: 
01966:   for (const d of list) {
01967:     const items = getAllPrizePresenceGroups(d);
01968:     const perDrawCounts = new Map();
01969: 
01970:     for (const it of items) {
01971:       const g = Number(it.grupo);
01972:       if (!Number.isFinite(g) || g < 1 || g > k) continue;
01973: 
01974:       const row = out.get(g);
01975:       row.recentPrizePresence += 1;
01976: 
01977:       if (Number(it.pos) === 1) {
01978:         row.recentFirst += 1;
01979:       }
01980: 
01981:       perDrawCounts.set(g, Number(perDrawCounts.get(g) || 0) + 1);
01982:     }
01983: 
01984:     for (const [g, c] of perDrawCounts.entries()) {
01985:       if (Number(c) >= 2) {
01986:         out.get(Number(g)).recentDupDraws += 1;
01987:       }
01988:     }
01989:   }
01990: 
01991:   for (const d of last1) {
01992:     const seen = new Set();
01993:     for (const it of getAllPrizePresenceGroups(d)) {
01994:       const g = Number(it.grupo);
01995:       if (!Number.isFinite(g) || g < 1 || g > k || seen.has(g)) continue;
01996:       seen.add(g);
01997:       out.get(g).recentLast1PrizePresence += 1;
01998:     }
01999:   }
02000: 
02001:   for (const d of last2) {
02002:     const seen = new Set();
02003:     for (const it of getAllPrizePresenceGroups(d)) {
02004:       const g = Number(it.grupo);
02005:       if (!Number.isFinite(g) || g < 1 || g > k || seen.has(g)) continue;
02006:       seen.add(g);
02007:       out.get(g).recentLast2PrizePresence += 1;
02008:     }
02009:   }
02010: 
02011:   for (const d of last3) {
02012:     const seen = new Set();
02013:     for (const it of getAllPrizePresenceGroups(d)) {
02014:       const g = Number(it.grupo);
02015:       if (!Number.isFinite(g) || g < 1 || g > k || seen.has(g)) continue;
02016:       seen.add(g);
02017:       out.get(g).recentLast3PrizePresence += 1;
02018:     }
02019:   }
02020: 
02021:   return out;
02022: }

02024: function getDayDrivenWeights(mode) {
02025:   if (mode === "REPEAT") {
02026:     return {
02027:       transition: 0.25,
02028:       pair: 0.25,
02029:       memory: 0.2,
02030:       recent: 0.15,
02031:       structural: 0.1,
02032:       late: 0.05,
02033:     };
02034:   }
02035: 
02036:   if (mode === "SPREAD") {
02037:     return {
02038:       transition: 0.2,
02039:       pair: 0.1,
02040:       memory: 0.1,
02041:       recent: 0.2,
02042:       structural: 0.3,
02043:       late: 0.1,
02044:     };
02045:   }
02046: 
02047:   return {
02048:     transition: 0.3,
02049:     pair: 0.15,
02050:     memory: 0.15,
02051:     recent: 0.15,
02052:     structural: 0.2,
02053:     late: 0.05,
02054:   };
02055: }
02056: 
02057: function normalizeWeights(weights) {
02058:   const safe = {
02059:     transition: Number(weights?.transition || 0),
02060:     pair: Number(weights?.pair || 0),
02061:     memory: Number(weights?.memory || 0),
02062:     recent: Number(weights?.recent || 0),
02063:     structural: Number(weights?.structural || 0),
02064:     late: Number(weights?.late || 0),
02065:   };
02066: 
02067:   const total = Object.values(safe).reduce((acc, n) => acc + n, 0);
02068:   if (!Number.isFinite(total) || total <= 0) {
02069:     return {
02070:       transition: 0.3,
02071:       pair: 0.15,
02072:       memory: 0.15,
02073:       recent: 0.15,
02074:       structural: 0.2,
02075:       late: 0.05,
02076:     };
02077:   }
02078: 
02079:   return {
02080:     transition: safe.transition / total,
02081:     pair: safe.pair / total,
02082:     memory: safe.memory / total,
02083:     recent: safe.recent / total,
02084:     structural: safe.structural / total,
02085:     late: safe.late / total,
02086:   };
02087: }
