04368:   if (!normalizedSchedule.length) return [];
04369: 
04370:   const daySorted = day
04371:     .filter((d) => isYMD(pickDrawYMD(d)) && toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d)))
04372:     .sort((a, b) => {
04373:       const ta = ymdHourToTs(pickDrawYMD(a), toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(a)));
04374:       const tb = ymdHourToTs(pickDrawYMD(b), toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(b)));
04375:       return ta - tb;
04376:     });
04377: 
04378:   const rangeSorted = range
04379:     .filter((d) => isYMD(pickDrawYMD(d)) && toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d)))
04380:     .sort((a, b) => {
04381:       const ta = ymdHourToTs(pickDrawYMD(a), toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(a)));
04382:       const tb = ymdHourToTs(pickDrawYMD(b), toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(b)));
04383:       return ta - tb;
04384:     });
04385: 
04386:   const realizedTodayMap = new Map();
04387: 
04388:   for (const d of daySorted) {
04389:     const y = pickDrawYMD(d);
04390:     const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));
04391:     if (!isYMD(y) || !h) continue;
04392:     if (y !== targetYmd) continue;
04393:     realizedTodayMap.set(`${y}|${h}`, d);
04394:   }
04395: 
04396: 
04397:   const chainContextDraws = [];
04398:   const timeline = [];
04399:   let pendingPredictionAlreadyShown = false;
04400: 
04401:   for (const slotHour of normalizedSchedule) {
04402:     const currentDraw = realizedTodayMap.get(`${targetYmd}|${slotHour}`) || null;
04403:     const slotTs = ymdHourToTs(targetYmd, slotHour);
04404: 
04405: 
04406:     if (!currentDraw && pendingPredictionAlreadyShown) {
04407:       timeline.push({
04408:         targetYmd,
04409:         targetHour: slotHour,
04410:         baseYmd: "",
04411:         baseHour: "",
04412:         top3: [],
04413:         resultGrupo: null,
04414:         hit: null,
04415:         status: "pending",
04416:       });
04417:       continue;
04418:     }
04419: 
04420:     let baseDraw = null;
04421: 
04422:     const priorChainDraw = [...chainContextDraws]
04423:       .filter((d) => {
04424:         const y = pickDrawYMD(d);
04425:         const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));
04426:         const ts = ymdHourToTs(y, h);
04427:         return isYMD(y) && h && Number.isFinite(ts) && ts < slotTs;
04428:       })
04429:       .sort((a, b) => {
04430:         const ta = ymdHourToTs(pickDrawYMD(a), toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(a)));
04431:         const tb = ymdHourToTs(pickDrawYMD(b), toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(b)));
04432:         return tb - ta;
04433:       })[0] || null;
04434: 
04435:     if (priorChainDraw) baseDraw = priorChainDraw;
04436: 
04437:     if (!baseDraw) {
04438:       const prevSameDay = daySorted
04439:         .filter((d) => {
04440:           const y = pickDrawYMD(d);
04441:           const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));
04442:           const ts = ymdHourToTs(y, h);
04443:           return y === targetYmd && h && Number.isFinite(ts) && ts < slotTs;
04444:         })
04445:         .sort((a, b) => {
04446:           const ta = ymdHourToTs(pickDrawYMD(a), toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(a)));
04447:           const tb = ymdHourToTs(pickDrawYMD(b), toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(b)));
04448:           return tb - ta;
04449:         })[0] || null;
04450: 
04451:       if (prevSameDay) baseDraw = prevSameDay;
04452:     }
04453: 
04454:     if (!baseDraw) {
04455:       for (let i = rangeSorted.length - 1; i >= 0; i -= 1) {
04456:         const d = rangeSorted[i];
04457:         const y = pickDrawYMD(d);
04458:         const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));
04459:         const ts = ymdHourToTs(y, h);
04460: 
04461:         if (!Number.isFinite(ts) || ts >= slotTs) continue;
04462: 
04463:         baseDraw = d;
04464:         break;
04465:       }
04466:     }
04467: 
04468:     if (!baseDraw) {
04469:       baseDraw = findPreviousValidDraw(rangeSorted, targetYmd, slotHour);
04470:     }
04471: 
04472:     if (!baseDraw) {
04473:       timeline.push({
04474:         targetYmd,
04475:         targetHour: slotHour,
04476:         baseYmd: "",
04477:         baseHour: "",
04478:         top3: [],
04479:         resultGrupo: null,
04480:         hit: null,
04481:         status: "pending",
04482:       });
04483:       continue;
04484:     }
04485: 
04486:     const baseTs = ymdHourToTs(
04487:       pickDrawYMD(baseDraw),
04488:       toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(baseDraw))
04489:     );
04490: 
04491:     const usableHistory = rangeSorted.filter((d) => {
04492:       const ts = ymdHourToTs(
04493:         pickDrawYMD(d),
04494:         toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d))
04495:       );
04496:       return Number.isFinite(ts) && ts <= baseTs;
04497:     });
04498: 
04499:     const historyStats = (() => {
04500:       const MS_DAY = 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000;
04501: 
04502:       let d30 = 0;
04503:       let d90 = 0;
04504:       let d180 = 0;
04505:       let d365 = 0;
04506: 
04507:       for (const historicalDraw of usableHistory) {
04508:         const historicalTs = ymdHourToTs(
04509:           pickDrawYMD(historicalDraw),
04510:           toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(historicalDraw))
04511:         );
04512: 
04513:         if (!Number.isFinite(historicalTs)) continue;
04514: 
04515:         const diffDays = (baseTs - historicalTs) / MS_DAY;
04516: 
04517:         if (diffDays >= 0 && diffDays <= 30) d30 += 1;
04518:         if (diffDays >= 0 && diffDays <= 90) d90 += 1;
04519:         if (diffDays >= 0 && diffDays <= 180) d180 += 1;
04520:         if (diffDays >= 0 && diffDays <= 365) d365 += 1;
04521:       }
04522: 
04523:       return {
04524:         total: usableHistory.length,
04525:         d30,
04526:         d90,
04527:         d180,
04528:         d365,
04529:       };
04530:     })();
04531: 
04532:     const usableTodayContext = [...chainContextDraws]
04533:       .filter((d) => {
04534:         const ts = ymdHourToTs(
04535:           pickDrawYMD(d),
04536:           toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d))
04537:         );
04538:         return Number.isFinite(ts) && ts <= baseTs;
04539:       })
04540:       .sort((a, b) => {
04541:         const ta = ymdHourToTs(pickDrawYMD(a), toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(a)));
04542:         const tb = ymdHourToTs(pickDrawYMD(b), toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(b)));
04543:         return ta - tb;
04544:       });
04545: 
04546:     const computed = computeStatisticalTop3V3({
04547:       lotteryKey,
04548:       drawsRange: [...usableHistory, ...usableTodayContext],
04549:       drawLast: baseDraw,
04550:       drawsToday: usableTodayContext,
04551:       PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
04552:       PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
04553:       FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
04554:       topN: 3,
04555:       targetYmdOverride: targetYmd,
04556:       targetHourOverride: slotHour,
04557:     });
04558: 
04559: 
04560:     const computedNextYmd = safeStr(computed?.meta?.next?.ymd || "");
04561:     const computedNextHour = toHourBucket(computed?.meta?.next?.hour || "");
04562:     const computedForSlot =
04563:       computedNextYmd === targetYmd && computedNextHour === slotHour;
04564: 
04565:     const top3 =
04566:       computedForSlot && Array.isArray(computed?.top)
04567:         ? computed.top.slice(0, 3)
04568:         : [];
04569: 
04570:     const resultTop3Groups = currentDraw
04571:       ? getPrizeGroupsByPosition(currentDraw, 3)
04572:       : [null, null, null];
04573: 
04574:     const resultGrupo = Number(resultTop3Groups[0]);
04575: 
04576:     const normalizedResultGrupo =
04577:       Number.isFinite(resultGrupo) &&
04578:       resultGrupo >= 1 &&
04579:       resultGrupo <= TOP3_GROUPS_K
04580:         ? resultGrupo
04581:         : null;
04582: 
04583:     const hitPrizePositions = resultTop3Groups.map(
04584:       (resultGroup) =>
04585:         Number.isFinite(Number(resultGroup)) &&
04586:         top3.some(
04587:           (item) =>
04588:             Number(item?.grupo) ===
04589:             Number(resultGroup)
04590:         )
04591:     );
04592: 
04593:     const matchedPrizePositions =
04594:       hitPrizePositions.filter(Boolean).length;
04595: 
04596:     const hit =
04597:       top3.length > 0 &&
04598:       resultTop3Groups.some(
04599:         (resultGroup) =>
04600:           Number.isFinite(Number(resultGroup))
04601:       )
04602:         ? matchedPrizePositions > 0
04603:         : null;
04604: 
04605:     timeline.push({
04606:       targetYmd,
04607:       targetHour: slotHour,
04608: 
04609:       baseYmd: pickDrawYMD(baseDraw) || "",
04610:       baseHour: toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(baseDraw)) || "",
04611: 
04612:       top3,
04613: 
04614:       resultGrupo: normalizedResultGrupo,
04615:       resultTop3Groups,
04616: 
04617:       hit,
04618:       top3PrizeHit: hit,
04619:       hitPrizePositions,
04620:       matchedPrizePositions,
04621: 
04622:       status: Number.isFinite(
04623:         Number(normalizedResultGrupo)
04624:       )
04625:         ? "validated"
04626:         : "pending",
04627: 
04628:       historyStats,
04629:     });
04630: 
04631:     if (currentDraw) {
04632:       chainContextDraws.push(currentDraw);
04633:     } else {
04634:       pendingPredictionAlreadyShown = true;
04635:     }
04636:   }
04637: 
04638:   return timeline;
04639: 
04640: }
04641: 
04642: export function buildTimelineTop3(args) {
04643:   return buildTimelineForDate(args || {});
04644: }
04645: 
04646: export function auditTop3Backtest({
04647:   drawsRange,
04648:   lotteryKey,
04649:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
04650:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
04651:   FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
04652: }) {
04653:   const range = Array.isArray(drawsRange) ? drawsRange : [];
04654:   const dates = Array.from(
04655:     new Set(
04656:       range
04657:         .map((d) => pickDrawYMD(d))
04658:         .filter((ymd) => isYMD(ymd))
04659:     )
04660:   ).sort();
04661: 
04662:   const allTimeline = [];
04663: 
04664:   for (const ymd of dates) {
04665:     const drawsToday = range.filter((d) => pickDrawYMD(d) === ymd);
04666: 
04667:     const dayTimeline = buildTimelineForDate({
04668:       ymd,
04669:       drawsToday,
04670:       drawsRange: range,
04671:       lotteryKey,
04672:       PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
04673:       PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
04674:       FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
04675:     });
04676: 
04677:     allTimeline.push(...(Array.isArray(dayTimeline) ? dayTimeline : []));
04678:   }
04679: 
04680:   return auditTop3Timeline({
04681:     timeline: allTimeline,
04682:     lotteryKey,
04683:   });
04684: }

00028: }

00031:   return toHourBucket(value) || "";

00035:   return normalizeHour(value).replace(/\D/g, "").padStart(2, "0");

00038: function makePredictionId({ lotteryKey, targetYmd, targetHour }) {
00039:   const lottery = normalizeLotteryKey(lotteryKey);
00040:   const ymd = safeStr(targetYmd);
00041:   const hour = hourCode(targetHour);
00042:

00044: 
00045:   return `${lottery}__${ymd}__${hour}`;
00046: }

00050:   return id ? doc(db, COLLECTION, id) : null;

00053: function cleanFirestoreValue(value) {
00054:   try {
00055:     return JSON.parse(
00056:       JSON.stringify(value, (_key, current) => {
00057:         if (current === undefined) return null;
00058:         if (typeof current === "number" && !Number.isFinite(current)) {
00059:           return null;
00060:         }
00061:         return current;
00062:       })
00063:     );
00064:   } catch {
00065:     return null;
00066:   }

00070:   const digits = String(value || "").replace(/\D/g, "");
00071:   if (!digits) return "";
00072:   return digits.slice(-4).padStart(4, "0");
00073: }

00075: function extractPrize1(draw) {
00076:   const prizes = Array.isArray(draw?.prizes) ? draw.prizes : [];
00077: 
00078:   return (
00079:     prizes.find((item) => Number(item?.position) === 1) ||
00080:     prizes[0] ||
00081:     null
00082:   );
00083: }
00084: 
00085: function extractPrize1Milhar(draw) {
00086:   const prize = extractPrize1(draw);
00087: 
00088:   return normalizeMilhar(
00089:     prize?.milhar ??
00090:       prize?.numero ??
00091:       prize?.number ??
00092:       prize?.valor ??
00093:       draw?.prize_1 ??
00094:       ""
00095:   );

00098: function findDrawForTarget({
00099:   draws,
00100:   targetYmd,
00101:   targetHour,
00102: }) {
00103:   const ymd = safeStr(targetYmd);
00104:   const hour = normalizeHour(targetHour);
00105: 
00106:   if (!isYMD(ymd) || !hour) return null;
00107: 
00108:   return (
00109:     (Array.isArray(draws) ? draws : []).find((draw) => {
00110:       return (
00111:         pickDrawYMD(draw) === ymd &&
00112:         normalizeHour(pickDrawHour(draw)) === hour
00113:       );
00114:     }) || null
00115:   );
00116: }
00117: function normalizeSnapshot(snapshot) {
00118:   return (Array.isArray(snapshot) ? snapshot : [])
00119:     .slice(0, 3)
00120:     .map((item, index) => ({
00121:       rank: Number(item?.rank || index + 1),
00122:       grupo: Number(item?.grupo),
00123:       animal: safeStr(item?.animal || ""),
00124:       prob: Number(item?.prob || 0),
00125:       probPct: Number(item?.probPct || 0),
00126:       milhares20: Array.isArray(item?.milhares20)
00127:         ? item.milhares20.map(normalizeMilhar).filter(Boolean).slice(0, 20)
00128:         : [],
00129:       milharesCols: Array.isArray(item?.milharesCols)
00130:         ? cleanFirestoreValue(item.milharesCols)
00131:         : [],
00132:       meta: cleanFirestoreValue(item?.meta || null),
00133:     }))
00134:     .filter((item) => {
00135:       return (
00136:         Number.isFinite(item.grupo) &&
00137:         item.grupo >= 1 &&
00138:         item.grupo <= 25
00139:       );
00140:     });
00141: }
00142: 
00143: function extractPrizeGrupo(prize) {
00144:   const direct = Number(
00145:     prize?.grupo ??
00146:       prize?.group ??
00147:       prize?.animal_grupo ??
00148:       prize?.grupo2
00149:   );
00150: 
00151:   if (
00152:     Number.isFinite(direct) &&
00153:     direct >= 1 &&
00154:     direct <= 25
00155:   ) {
00156:     return direct;
00157:   }
00158: 
00159:   const milhar = normalizeMilhar(
00160:     prize?.milhar ??
00161:       prize?.numero ??
00162:       prize?.number ??
00163:       prize?.valor ??
00164:       ""
00165:   );
00166: 
00167:   if (!milhar) return null;
00168: 
00169:   const dezena = Number(milhar.slice(-2));
00170:   const normalizedDezena = dezena === 0 ? 100 : dezena;
00171:   const grupo = Math.ceil(normalizedDezena / 4);
00172: 
00173:   return grupo >= 1 && grupo <= 25 ? grupo : null;
00174: }
00175: 
00176: function extractOfficialPodium(draw) {
00177:   const prizes = Array.isArray(draw?.prizes)
00178:     ? draw.prizes
00179:     : [];
00180: 
00181:   return [1, 2, 3].map((position) => {
00182:     const prize =
00183:       prizes.find(
00184:         (item) => Number(item?.position) === position
00185:       ) ||
00186:       prizes[position - 1] ||
00187:       null;
00188: 
00189:     if (!prize) return null;
00190: 
00191:     const grupo = extractPrizeGrupo(prize);
00192:     const milhar = normalizeMilhar(
00193:       prize?.milhar ??
00194:         prize?.numero ??
00195:         prize?.number ??
00196:         prize?.valor ??
00197:         ""
00198:     );
00199: 
00200:     if (
00201:       !Number.isFinite(Number(grupo)) ||
00202:       Number(grupo) < 1 ||
00203:       Number(grupo) > 25
00204:     ) {
00205:       return null;
00206:     }
00207: 
00208:     return {
00209:       position,
00210:       grupo: Number(grupo),
00211:       milhar,
00212:       animal: safeStr(prize?.animal || ""),
00213:     };
00214:   });
00215: }
00216: 
00217: function podiumMedalFromPosition(position) {
00218:   if (Number(position) === 1) return "gold";
00219:   if (Number(position) === 2) return "silver";
00220:   if (Number(position) === 3) return "bronze";
00221:   return "";
00222: }
00223: 
00224: function analyzeSnapshotHit(snapshot, officialPodium) {
00225:   const top3 = Array.isArray(snapshot)
00226:     ? snapshot.slice(0, 3)
00227:     : [];
00228: 
00229:   const podium = Array.isArray(officialPodium)
00230:     ? officialPodium.filter(Boolean).slice(0, 3)
00231:     : [];
00232: 
00233:   if (!top3.length || !podium.length) {
00234:     return {
00235:       hitType: "miss",
00236:       hitScore: 0,
00237:       hitPosition: -1,
00238:       predictionPosition: -1,
00239:       resultPosition: -1,
00240:       podiumMedal: "",
00241:       matchedValue: "",
00242:       matchedGrupo: null,
00243:       matchedMilhar: "",
00244:       matchedAnimal: "",
00245:     };
00246:   }
00247: 
00248:   for (const officialPrize of podium) {
00249:     const resultGrupo = Number(officialPrize?.grupo);
00250:     const resultMilhar = normalizeMilhar(
00251:       officialPrize?.milhar
00252:     );
00253:     const resultCentena = resultMilhar
00254:       ? resultMilhar.slice(-3)
00255:       : "";
00256: 
00257:     for (
00258:       let predictionIndex = 0;
00259:       predictionIndex < top3.length;
00260:       predictionIndex += 1
00261:     ) {
00262:       const prediction = top3[predictionIndex];
00263:       const predictionGrupo = Number(prediction?.grupo);
00264: 
00265:       const milhares = (
00266:         Array.isArray(prediction?.milhares20)
00267:           ? prediction.milhares20
00268:           : Array.isArray(prediction?.milhares)
00269:             ? prediction.milhares
00270:             : []
00271:       )
00272:         .map(normalizeMilhar)
00273:         .filter(Boolean);
00274: 
00275:       const centenas = milhares.map(
00276:         (value) => value.slice(-3)
00277:       );
00278: 
00279:       let hitType = "miss";
00280:       let hitScore = 0;
00281:       let matchedValue = "";
00282: 
00283:       if (
00284:         resultMilhar &&
00285:         milhares.includes(resultMilhar)
00286:       ) {
00287:         hitType = "hit_exact";
00288:         hitScore = 100;
00289:         matchedValue = resultMilhar;
00290:       } else if (
00291:         resultCentena &&
00292:         centenas.includes(resultCentena)
00293:       ) {
00294:         hitType = "hit_centena";
00295:         hitScore = 66.67;
00296:         matchedValue = resultCentena;
00297:       } else if (
00298:         Number.isFinite(resultGrupo) &&
00299:         predictionGrupo === resultGrupo
00300:       ) {
00301:         hitType = "hit_grupo";
00302:         hitScore = 33.33;
00303:         matchedValue = resultMilhar
00304:           ? resultMilhar.slice(-2)
00305:           : String(resultGrupo).padStart(2, "0");
00306:       }
00307: 
00308:       if (hitType !== "miss") {
00309:         const resultPosition = Number(
00310:           officialPrize?.position
00311:         );
00312: 
00313:         return {
00314:           hitType,
00315:           hitScore,
00316:           hitPosition: predictionIndex + 1,
00317:           predictionPosition: predictionIndex + 1,
00318:           resultPosition,
00319:           podiumMedal:
00320:             podiumMedalFromPosition(resultPosition),
00321:           matchedValue,
00322:           matchedGrupo: resultGrupo,
00323:           matchedMilhar: resultMilhar,
00324:           matchedAnimal: safeStr(
00325:             officialPrize?.animal || ""
00326:           ),
00327:         };
00328:       }
00329:     }
00330:   }
00331: 
00332:   return {
00333:     hitType: "miss",
00334:     hitScore: 0,
00335:     hitPosition: -1,
00336:     predictionPosition: -1,
00337:     resultPosition: -1,
00338:     podiumMedal: "",
00339:     matchedValue: "",
00340:     matchedGrupo: null,
00341:     matchedMilhar: "",
00342:     matchedAnimal: "",
00343:   };
00344: }

00346: export async function saveTop3PredictionSnapshot({
00347:   lotteryKey,
00348:   targetYmd,
00349:   targetHour,
00350:   picks,
00351:   snapshot,
00352:   engineVersion,
00353: }) {
00354:   let user = null;
00355: 
00356:   try {
00357:     user = await loginAnonymous();
00358:   } catch (error) {
00359:     return {
00360:       ok: false,
00361:       skipped: true,
00362:       reason: "AUTH_FAILED",
00363:       error: String(error?.message || error || ""),
00364:     };
00365:   }
00366: 
00367:   if (!user?.uid) {
00368:     return {
00369:       ok: false,
00370:       skipped: true,
00371:       reason: "AUTH_REQUIRED",
00372:     };
00373:   }
00374: 
00375:   const lottery = normalizeLotteryKey(lotteryKey);
00376:   const ymd = safeStr(targetYmd);
00377:   const hour = normalizeHour(targetHour);
00378:   const ref = predictionRef({
00379:     lotteryKey: lottery,
00380:     targetYmd: ymd,
00381:     targetHour: hour,
00382:   });
00383: 
00384:   if (!ref || !isYMD(ymd) || !hour) {
00385:     return {
00386:       ok: false,
00387:       skipped: true,
00388:       reason: "INVALID_TARGET",
00389:     };
00390:   }
00391: 
00392:   const normalizedSnapshot = normalizeSnapshot(snapshot);
00393: 
00394:   if (!normalizedSnapshot.length) {
00395:     return {
00396:       ok: false,
00397:       skipped: true,
00398:       reason: "EMPTY_SNAPSHOT",
00399:     };
00400:   }
00401: 
00402:   const normalizedPicks = Array.from(
00403:     new Set(
00404:       (Array.isArray(picks) ? picks : [])
00405:         .map(Number)
00406:         .filter((value) => value >= 1 && value <= 25)
00407:     )
00408:   ).slice(0, 3);
00409: 
00410:   const now = Date.now();
00411: 
00412:   const payload = cleanFirestoreValue({
00413:     id: ref.id,
00414:     lotteryKey: lottery,
00415:     targetYmd: ymd,
00416:     targetHour: hour,
00417:     targetKey: `${ymd}_${hour}`,
00418:     picks: normalizedPicks,
00419:     snapshot: normalizedSnapshot,
00420:     engineVersion: safeStr(engineVersion || "V3_STATISTICAL"),
00421:     status: "predicted",
00422:     resultGrupo: null,
00423:     resultMilhar: "",
00424:     resultAnimal: "",
00425:     hitType: "",
00426:     hitScore: 0,
00427:     hitPosition: -1,
00428:     matchedValue: "",
00429:     createdAt: now,
00430:     updatedAt: now,
00431:     createdBy: user.uid,
00432:   });
00433: 
00434:   const result = await runTransaction(db, async (transaction) => {
00435:     const current = await transaction.get(ref);
00436: 
00437:     if (current.exists()) {
00438:       return {
00439:         ok: true,
00440:         created: false,
00441:         existing: true,
00442:       };
00443:     }
00444: 
00445:     transaction.set(ref, payload);
00446: 
00447:     return {
00448:       ok: true,
00449:       created: true,
00450:       existing: false,
00451:     };
00452:   });
00453: 
00454:   return result;
00455: }

00457: export async function loadTop3PredictionDay({
00458:   lotteryKey,
00459:   targetYmd,
00460:   schedule,
00461: }) {
00462:   const lottery = normalizeLotteryKey(lotteryKey);
00463:   const ymd = safeStr(targetYmd);
00464: 
00465:   if (!isYMD(ymd)) return [];
00466: 
00467:   const hours = Array.from(
00468:     new Set(
00469:       (Array.isArray(schedule) ? schedule : [])
00470:         .map(normalizeHour)
00471:         .filter(Boolean)
00472:     )
00473:   );
00474: 
00475:   const snapshots = await Promise.all(
00476:     hours.map(async (hour) => {
00477:       const ref = predictionRef({
00478:         lotteryKey: lottery,
00479:         targetYmd: ymd,
00480:         targetHour: hour,
00481:       });
00482: 
00483:       if (!ref) return null;
00484: 
00485:       try {
00486:         const snap = await getDoc(ref);
00487:         if (!snap.exists()) return null;
00488: 
00489:         return {
00490:           id: snap.id,
00491:           ...snap.data(),
00492:         };
00493:       } catch {
00494:         return null;
00495:       }
00496:     })
00497:   );
00498: 
00499:   return snapshots
00500:     .filter(Boolean)
00501:     .sort((a, b) => {
00502:       return normalizeHour(a?.targetHour).localeCompare(
00503:         normalizeHour(b?.targetHour)
00504:       );
00505:     });
00506: }
00507: 
00508: export async function reconcileTop3PredictionDay({
00509:   lotteryKey,
00510:   targetYmd,
00511:   schedule,
00512:   draws,
00513: }) {
00514:   let user = null;
00515: 
00516:   try {
00517:     user = await loginAnonymous();
00518:   } catch (error) {
00519:     return {
00520:       ok: false,
00521:       skipped: true,
00522:       reason: "AUTH_FAILED",
00523:       error: String(error?.message || error || ""),
00524:     };
00525:   }
00526: 
00527:   if (!user?.uid) {
00528:     return {
00529:       ok: false,
00530:       skipped: true,
00531:       reason: "AUTH_REQUIRED",
00532:     };
00533:   }
00534: 
00535:   const history = await loadTop3PredictionDay({
00536:     lotteryKey,
00537:     targetYmd,
00538:     schedule,
00539:   });
00540: 
00541:   const lottery = normalizeLotteryKey(lotteryKey);
00542:   let updated = 0;
00543:   const reconciledHistory = [];
00544: 
00545:   for (const entry of history) {
00546:     if (!entry) continue;
00547: 
00548:     const realDraw = findDrawForTarget({
00549:       draws,
00550:       targetYmd: entry?.targetYmd,
00551:       targetHour: entry?.targetHour,
00552:     });
00553: 
00554:     if (!realDraw) {
00555:       reconciledHistory.push(entry);
00556:       continue;
00557:     }
00558: 
00559:     const officialPodium =
00560:       extractOfficialPodium(realDraw);
00561: 
00562:     const firstOfficialPrize =
00563:       officialPodium.find(
00564:         (item) => Number(item?.position) === 1
00565:       ) || null;
00566: 
00567:     const resultGrupo = Number(
00568:       firstOfficialPrize?.grupo
00569:     );
00570: 
00571:     if (
00572:       !Number.isFinite(resultGrupo) ||
00573:       resultGrupo < 1 ||
00574:       resultGrupo > 25
00575:     ) {
00576:       reconciledHistory.push(entry);
00577:       continue;
00578:     }
00579: 
00580:     const resultMilhar = normalizeMilhar(
00581:       firstOfficialPrize?.milhar
00582:     );
00583:     const savedLottery = safeStr(
00584:       entry?.resultLotteryKey
00585:     ).toUpperCase();
00586:     const savedGrupo = Number(entry?.resultGrupo);
00587:     const savedMilhar = normalizeMilhar(entry?.resultMilhar);
00588: 
00589:     const analysis = analyzeSnapshotHit(
00590:       entry?.snapshot,
00591:       officialPodium
00592:     );
00593: 
00594:     const alreadyMatchesRealResult =
00595:       entry?.status === "validated" &&
00596:       savedLottery === lottery &&
00597:       savedGrupo === resultGrupo &&
00598:       savedMilhar === resultMilhar &&
00599:       safeStr(entry?.hitType) === analysis.hitType &&
00600:       Number(entry?.hitScore) === analysis.hitScore &&
00601:       Number(entry?.hitPosition) === analysis.hitPosition &&
00602:       Number(entry?.resultPosition ?? -1) ===
00603:         Number(analysis.resultPosition ?? -1) &&
00604:       safeStr(entry?.podiumMedal) ===
00605:         safeStr(analysis.podiumMedal) &&
00606:       safeStr(entry?.matchedValue) === analysis.matchedValue;
00607: 
00608:     if (alreadyMatchesRealResult) {
00609:       reconciledHistory.push(entry);
00610:       continue;
00611:     }
00612: 
00613:     const ref = doc(db, COLLECTION, entry.id);
00614:     const now = Date.now();
00615: 
00616:     const validationPayload = {
00617:       resultGrupo,
00618:       resultMilhar,
00619:       resultLotteryKey: lottery,
00620:       resultAnimal: safeStr(
00621:         firstOfficialPrize?.animal ||
00622:           extractPrize1(realDraw)?.animal ||
00623:           ""
00624:       ),
00625:       resultTop3Groups: officialPodium.map(
00626:         (item) => Number(item?.grupo) || null
00627:       ),
00628:       resultTop3Milhares: officialPodium.map(
00629:         (item) => normalizeMilhar(item?.milhar)
00630:       ),
00631:       hitType: analysis.hitType,
00632:       hitScore: analysis.hitScore,
00633:       hitPosition: analysis.hitPosition,
00634:       predictionPosition: analysis.predictionPosition,
00635:       resultPosition: analysis.resultPosition,
00636:       podiumMedal: analysis.podiumMedal,
00637:       matchedGrupo: analysis.matchedGrupo,
00638:       matchedMilhar: analysis.matchedMilhar,
00639:       matchedAnimal: analysis.matchedAnimal,
00640:       matchedValue: analysis.matchedValue,
00641:       validatedAt: now,
00642:       validatedBy: user.uid,
00643:       updatedAt: now,
00644:       status: "validated",
00645:     };
00646: 
00647:     await setDoc(
00648:       ref,
00649:       validationPayload,
00650:       { merge: true }
00651:     );
00652: 
00653:     reconciledHistory.push({
00654:       ...entry,
00655:       ...validationPayload,
00656:     });
00657: 
00658:     updated += 1;
00659:   }
00660: 
00661:   return {
00662:     ok: true,
00663:     updated,
00664:     history: reconciledHistory,
00665:   };
00666: }

00093: function drawTs(draw) {
00094:   const y = pickDrawYMD(draw);
00095:   const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(draw));
00096: 
00097:   if (!isYMD(y) || !h) return Number.NEGATIVE_INFINITY;
00098: 
00099:   const [Y, M, D] = String(y).split("-").map(Number);
00100:   const mins = hourToInt(h);
00101: 
00102:   if (!Number.isFinite(mins) || mins < 0) {
00103:     return Number.NEGATIVE_INFINITY;
00104:   }
00105: 
00106:   return Date.UTC(Y, M - 1, D) + mins * 60 * 1000;
00107: }
00108: 
00109: function drawKey(draw) {
00110:   const y = pickDrawYMD(draw);
00111:   const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(draw));
00112:   return isYMD(y) && h ? `${y}|${h}` : "";
00113: }

00115: function hasDrawAtHour(draws, hourBucket) {
00116:   const target = toHourBucket(hourBucket);
00117:   if (!target) return false;
00118: 
00119:   return (Array.isArray(draws) ? draws : []).some((d) => {
00120:     const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));
00121:     return h === target;
00122:   });
00123: }

00125: function mergeBaseIntoRange(rangeDraws, baseDraw) {
00126:   const list = Array.isArray(rangeDraws) ? rangeDraws : [];
00127:   const key = drawKey(baseDraw);
00128: 
00129:   if (!key) return list;
00130: 
00131:   const map = new Map();
00132: 
00133:   for (const d of list) {
00134:     const k = drawKey(d);
00135:     if (k) map.set(k, d);
00136:   }
00137: 
00138:   if (!map.has(key)) {
00139:     map.set(key, baseDraw);
00140:   }
00141: 
00142:   return Array.from(map.values()).sort((a, b) => drawTs(a) - drawTs(b));
00143: }
00144: 
00145: function isDrawValidForLotterySchedule(draw, lotteryKey) {
00146:   const y = pickDrawYMD(draw);
00147:   const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(draw));
00148: 
00149:   if (!isYMD(y) || !h) return false;
00150: 
00151:   const schedule = getScheduleForLottery({
00152:     lotteryKey,
00153:     ymd: y,
00154:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
00155:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
00156:     FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
00157:   });
00158: 
00159:   const normalizedSchedule = Array.isArray(schedule)
00160:     ? schedule.map(toHourBucket).filter(Boolean)
00161:     : [];
00162: 
00163:   return normalizedSchedule.includes(h);
00164: }
00165: 
00166: function findLatestHistoricalBaseDraw({
00167:   draws,
00168:   lotteryKey,
00169:   targetYmd,
00170:   targetHourBucket,
00171: }) {
00172:   const list = Array.isArray(draws) ? draws : [];
00173:   const targetHour = toHourBucket(targetHourBucket);
00174: 
00175:   if (!isYMD(targetYmd) || !targetHour) {
00176:     return { draw: null, ymd: "", hour: "", source: "none" };
00177:   }
00178: 
00179:   const [Y, M, D] = String(targetYmd).split("-").map(Number);
00180:   const mins = hourToInt(targetHour);
00181:   const targetTs = Date.UTC(Y, M - 1, D) + mins * 60 * 1000;
00182: 
00183:   let best = null;
00184:   let bestTs = Number.NEGATIVE_INFINITY;
00185: 
00186:   for (const d of list) {
00187:     if (!isDrawValidForLotterySchedule(d, lotteryKey)) continue;
00188: 
00189:     const ts = drawTs(d);
00190:     if (!Number.isFinite(ts)) continue;
00191:     if (ts >= targetTs) continue;
00192: 
00193:     if (ts > bestTs) {
00194:       best = d;
00195:       bestTs = ts;
00196:     }
00197:   }
00198: 
00199:   if (!best) {
00200:     return { draw: null, ymd: "", hour: "", source: "none" };
00201:   }
00202: 
00203:   return {
00204:     draw: best,
00205:     ymd: pickDrawYMD(best) || "",
00206:     hour: toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(best)) || "",
00207:     source: "history_range",
00208:   };
00209: }
00210: 
00211: function sanitizeHistoricalDraws({ draws, lotteryKey, baseDraw }) {
00212:   const list = Array.isArray(draws) ? draws : [];
00213:   const baseTs = drawTs(baseDraw);
00214: 
00215:   if (!baseDraw || !Number.isFinite(baseTs)) return [];
00216: 
00217:   return list
00218:     .filter((d) => isDrawValidForLotterySchedule(d, lotteryKey))
00219:     .filter((d) => {
00220:       const ts = drawTs(d);
00221:       return Number.isFinite(ts) && ts <= baseTs;
00222:     })
00223:     .sort((a, b) => drawTs(a) - drawTs(b));
00224: }
00225: 
00226: function parseTargetDate(ymd, hour) {
00227:   if (!isYMD(ymd)) return null;
00228: 
00229:   const h = toHourBucket(hour);
00230:   const m = String(h || "").match(/^(\d{2})h$/);
00231: 
00232:   if (!m) return null;
00233: 
00234:   const [Y, M, D] = String(ymd).split("-").map(Number);
00235:   const hh = Number(m[1]);
00236: 
00237:   const dt = new Date(Y, M - 1, D, hh, 0, 0, 0);
00238:   return Number.isNaN(dt.getTime()) ? null : dt;
00239: }
00240: 
00241: function isFutureTarget(ymd, hour) {
00242:   const target = parseTargetDate(ymd, hour);
00243:   if (!target) return false;
00244:   return target.getTime() > Date.now();
00245: }
00246: 
00247: function makeTargetKey(ymd, hour) {
00248:   const y = safeStr(ymd);
00249:   const h = toHourBucket(hour);
00250:   return isYMD(y) && h ? `${y}_${h}` : "";
00251: }
00252: 
00253: function hydratePersistedTop3(entry) {
00254:   const snapshot = Array.isArray(entry?.snapshot)
00255:     ? entry.snapshot.slice(0, 3)
00256:     : [];
00257: 
00258:   return snapshot
00259:     .map((item, index) => {
00260:       const grupo = Number(item?.grupo);
00261: 
00262:       if (
00263:         !Number.isFinite(grupo) ||
00264:         grupo < 1 ||
00265:         grupo > 25
00266:       ) {
00267:         return null;
00268:       }
00269: 
00270:       const imgBg =
00271:         Array.isArray(item?.imgBg) && item.imgBg.length
00272:           ? item.imgBg.filter(Boolean)
00273:           : [getGrupoImgSrc(grupo, 512)].filter(Boolean);
00274: 
00275:       const imgIcon =
00276:         Array.isArray(item?.imgIcon) && item.imgIcon.length
00277:           ? item.imgIcon.filter(Boolean)
00278:           : buildResultStyleImgVariants(grupo, 96);
00279: 
00280:       return {
00281:         ...item,
00282:         rank: Number(item?.rank || index + 1),
00283:         grupo,
00284:         animal:
00285:           safeStr(item?.animal) ||
00286:           safeStr(getAnimalLabel(grupo)),
00287:         prob: Number(item?.prob || 0),
00288:         probPct: Number(item?.probPct || 0),
00289:         milhares20: Array.isArray(item?.milhares20)
00290:           ? item.milhares20.slice(0, 20)
00291:           : [],
00292:         milharesCols: Array.isArray(item?.milharesCols)
00293:           ? item.milharesCols
00294:           : [],
00295:         imgBg,
00296:         imgIcon,
00297:         persistedSnapshot: true,
00298:       };
00299:     })
00300:     .filter(Boolean)
00301:     .slice(0, 3);
00302: }

00304: function resolveLayerMetaText(analytics) {
00305:   const meta = analytics?.meta || null;
00306:   const explain = meta?.explain || null;
00307: 
00308:   const candidates = [
00309:     meta?.label,
00310:     explain?.layerLabel,
00311:     explain?.layerKey,
00312:     meta?.scenario,
00313:   ]
00314:     .map((v) => safeStr(v))
00315:     .filter(Boolean);
00316: 
00317:   return candidates[0] || "";
00318: }

00320: function debugTop3Effect(name, details = {}) {
00321:   if (typeof window === "undefined") return;
00322: 
00323:   const enabled =
00324:     new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get(
00325:       "debugTop3Effects"
00326:     ) === "1";
00327: 
00328:   if (!enabled) return;
00329: 
00330:   const counters =
00331:     window.__TOP3_EFFECT_COUNTS__ ||
00332:     (window.__TOP3_EFFECT_COUNTS__ = {});
00333: 
00334:   counters[name] = Number(counters[name] || 0) + 1;
00335: 
00336:   console.info(
00337:     "[TOP3 EFFECT]",
00338:     name,
00339:     `#${counters[name]}`,
00340:     details
00341:   );
00342: }

01474:           window.localStorage.setItem(
01475:             "top3_firestore_last_save",
01476:             JSON.stringify(diagnostic)
01477:           );
01478: 
01479:           window.__TOP3_FIRESTORE_LAST_SAVE__ = diagnostic;
01480:         } catch {}
01481: 
01482:         if (!result?.ok) {
01483:           top3SaveRunKeys.delete(saveRunKey);
01484: 
01485:           console.error(
01486:             "[TOP3 FIRESTORE SAVE FAILED]",
01487:             diagnostic
01488:           );
01489:         } else {
01490:           const persistedEntry = {
01491:             lotteryKey: lotteryKeySafe,
01492:             targetYmd: analysisYmd,
01493:             targetHour: analysisHourBucket,
01494:             targetKey,
01495:             picks,
01496:             snapshot,
01497:             engineVersion,
01498:             status: "predicted",
01499:           };
01500: 
01501:           setCurrentPersistedPrediction(persistedEntry);
01502:           setCurrentPersistedResolved(true);
01503: 
01504:           console.info(
01505:             "[TOP3 FIRESTORE SAVE OK]",
01506:             diagnostic
01507:           );
01508:         }
01509:       })
01510:       .catch((error) => {
01511:         top3SaveRunKeys.delete(saveRunKey);
01512: 
01513:         const diagnostic = {
01514:           at: new Date().toISOString(),
01515:           lotteryKey: lotteryKeySafe,
01516:           targetYmd: analysisYmd,
01517:           targetHour: analysisHourBucket,
01518:           picks,
01519:           error: String(error?.message || error || ""),
01520:         };
01521: 
01522:         try {
01523:           window.localStorage.setItem(
01524:             "top3_firestore_last_save",
01525:             JSON.stringify(diagnostic)
01526:           );
01527: 
01528:           window.__TOP3_FIRESTORE_LAST_SAVE__ = diagnostic;
01529:         } catch {}
01530: 
01531:         console.error(
01532:           "[TOP3 FIRESTORE SAVE EXCEPTION]",
01533:           diagnostic
01534:         );
01535:       });
01536:   }, [
01537:     analysisYmd,
01538:     analysisHourBucket,
01539:     top3,
01540:     lotteryKeySafe,
01541:     debugTop3,
01542:     currentPersistedResolved,
01543:     currentPersistedPrediction,
01544:   ]);
01545: 
01546:   useEffect(() => {
01547:     const persistedSchedule = scheduleKey
01548:       ? scheduleKey.split("|").filter(Boolean)
01549:       : [];
01550: 
01551:     debugTop3Effect("04_load_persisted_history", {
01552:       lotteryKey: lotteryKeySafe,
01553:       timelineYmd,
01554:       scheduleLength: persistedSchedule.length,
01555:       secondaryReady,
01556:     });
01557: 
01558:     if (!secondaryReady) {
01559:       setPersistedTop3History([]);
01560:       return undefined;
01561:     }
01562: 
01563:     let alive = true;
01564: 
01565:     async function loadPersistedHistory() {
01566:       if (!isYMD(timelineYmd)) {
01567:         if (alive) setPersistedTop3History([]);
01568:         return;
01569:       }
01570: 
01571:       try {
01572:         const history = await loadTop3PredictionDay({
01573:           lotteryKey: lotteryKeySafe,
01574:           targetYmd: timelineYmd,
01575:           schedule: persistedSchedule,
01576:         });
01577: 
01578:         if (alive) {
01579:           setPersistedTop3History(
01580:             Array.isArray(history) ? history : []
01581:           );
01582:         }
01583:       } catch {
01584:         if (alive) setPersistedTop3History([]);
01585:       }
01586:     }
01587:

01589:

01591:       alive = false;
01592:     };
01593:   }, [
01594:     lotteryKeySafe,
01595:     timelineYmd,
01596:     scheduleKey,
01597:     secondaryReady,
01598:   ]);
01599: 
01600:   useEffect(() => {
01601:     const persistedSchedule = scheduleKey
01602:       ? scheduleKey.split("|").filter(Boolean)
01603:       : [];
01604: 
01605:     debugTop3Effect("05_reconcile_history", {
01606:       lotteryKey: lotteryKeySafe,
01607:       timelineYmd,
01608:       scheduleLength: persistedSchedule.length,
01609:       todayDrawsLength: Array.isArray(todayDraws)
01610:         ? todayDraws.length
01611:         : -1,
01612:       rangeDrawsLength: Array.isArray(rangeDraws)
01613:         ? rangeDraws.length
01614:         : -1,
01615:       loading,
01616:       secondaryReady,
01617:     });
01618: 
01619:     if (!secondaryReady) return;
01620:     if (loading) return;
01621:     if (!isYMD(timelineYmd)) return;
01622:     if (!(todayDraws?.length || rangeDraws?.length)) return;
01623: 
01624:     const drawMap = new Map();
01625: 
01626:     for (const draw of [
01627:       ...(Array.isArray(rangeDraws) ? rangeDraws : []),
01628:       ...(Array.isArray(todayDraws) ? todayDraws : []),
01629:     ]) {
01630:       if (pickDrawYMD(draw) !== timelineYmd) continue;
01631: 
01632:       const key = drawKey(draw);
01633:       if (!key) continue;
01634: 
01635:       drawMap.set(key, draw);
01636:     }
01637: 
01638:     const targetDraws = Array.from(drawMap.values()).sort(
01639:       (a, b) => drawTs(a) - drawTs(b)
01640:     );
01641: 
01642:     if (!targetDraws.length) return;
01643: 
01644:     const drawSignature = targetDraws
01645:       .map((draw) => {
01646:         const key = drawKey(draw);
01647:         const grupo = Number(
01648:           pickPrize1GrupoFromDraw(draw) || 0
01649:         );
01650:

01652:           ? draw.prizes.find(
01653:               (item) => Number(item?.position) === 1
01654:             ) || draw.prizes[0]
01655:           : null;
01656: 
01657:         const milhar = safeStr(
01658:           prize1?.milhar ??
01659:             prize1?.numero ??
01660:             prize1?.number ??
01661:             prize1?.valor ??
01662:             draw?.prize_1 ??
01663:             ""
01664:         );
01665: 
01666:         return `${key}:${grupo}:${milhar}`;
01667:       })
01668:       .join(",");
01669: 
01670:     const reconcileRunKey = [
01671:       lotteryKeySafe,
01672:       timelineYmd,
01673:       scheduleKey,
01674:       drawSignature,
01675:     ].join("|");
01676: 
01677:     if (top3ReconcileRunKeys.has(reconcileRunKey)) return;
01678: 
01679:     top3ReconcileRunKeys.add(reconcileRunKey);
01680: 
01681:     let alive = true;
01682: 
01683:     reconcilePendingTop3Log({
01684:       todayDraws: Array.isArray(todayDraws) ? todayDraws : [],
01685:       rangeDraws: Array.isArray(rangeDraws) ? rangeDraws : [],
01686:     });
01687: 
01688:     reconcileTop3PredictionDay({
01689:       lotteryKey: lotteryKeySafe,
01690:       targetYmd: timelineYmd,
01691:       schedule: persistedSchedule,
01692:       draws: targetDraws,
01693:     })
01694:       .then((result) => {
01695:         if (!result?.ok) {
01696:           top3ReconcileRunKeys.delete(reconcileRunKey);
01697:           top3ReconcileRetryCounts.delete(reconcileRunKey);
01698:           return;
01699:         }
01700: 
01701:         const reconciledHistory = Array.isArray(result?.history)
01702:           ? result.history
01703:           : [];
01704: 
01705:         if (alive) {
01706:           setPersistedTop3History(reconciledHistory);
01707:         }
01708: 
01709:         const targetDrawKeys = new Set(
01710:           targetDraws
01711:             .map((draw) => drawKey(draw))
01712:             .filter(Boolean)
01713:         );
01714: 
01715:         const hasPendingResult = reconciledHistory.some((entry) => {
01716:           const y = safeStr(entry?.targetYmd);
01717:           const h = toHourBucket(entry?.targetHour);
01718:           const key = isYMD(y) && h ? `${y}|${h}` : "";
01719: 
01720:           return (
01721:             key &&
01722:             targetDrawKeys.has(key) &&
01723:             safeStr(entry?.status).toLowerCase() !== "validated"
01724:           );
01725:         });
01726: 
01727:         const updated = Number(result?.updated || 0);
01728: 
01729:         if (updated > 0 || !hasPendingResult) {
01730:           top3ReconcileRetryCounts.delete(reconcileRunKey);
01731:           return;
01732:         }
01733: 
01734:         const retryCount =
01735:           Number(top3ReconcileRetryCounts.get(reconcileRunKey) || 0) + 1;
01736: 
01737:         if (retryCount > TOP3_RECONCILE_MAX_RETRIES) {
01738:           top3ReconcileRunKeys.delete(reconcileRunKey);
01739:           top3ReconcileRetryCounts.delete(reconcileRunKey);
01740: 
01741:           if (debugTop3) {
01742:             console.warn(
01743:               "[TOP3 FIRESTORE RECONCILE RETRIES EXHAUSTED]",
01744:               {
01745:                 reconcileRunKey,
01746:                 retryCount: retryCount - 1,
01747:                 historyLength: reconciledHistory.length,
01748:               }
01749:             );
01750:           }
01751: 
01752:           return;
01753:         }
01754: 
01755:         top3ReconcileRetryCounts.set(
01756:           reconcileRunKey,
01757:           retryCount
01758:         );
01759: 
01760:         window.setTimeout(() => {
01761:           top3ReconcileRunKeys.delete(reconcileRunKey);
01762: 
01763:           if (alive) {
01764:             setReconcileRetryNonce((value) => value + 1);
01765:           }
01766:         }, TOP3_RECONCILE_RETRY_DELAY_MS);
01767:       })
01768:       .catch((error) => {
01769:         top3ReconcileRunKeys.delete(reconcileRunKey);
01770:         top3ReconcileRetryCounts.delete(reconcileRunKey);
01771: 
01772:         if (debugTop3) {
01773:           console.warn("[TOP3 FIRESTORE RECONCILE]", error);
01774:         }
01775:       });
01776: 
01777:     return () => {
01778:       alive = false;
01779:     };
01780:   }, [
01781:     todayDraws,
01782:     rangeDraws,
01783:     lotteryKeySafe,
01784:     timelineYmd,
01785:     scheduleKey,
01786:     debugTop3,
01787:     loading,
01788:     secondaryReady,
01789:     reconcileRetryNonce,
01790:   ]);
01791: 
01792:   const availableHistoryDates = useMemo(() => {
01793:     const key = safeStr(lotteryKeySafe).toUpperCase();
01794: 
01795:     return Array.isArray(
01796:       availableHistoryDatesByLottery?.[key]
01797:     )
01798:       ? availableHistoryDatesByLottery[key]
01799:       : [];
01800:   }, [
01801:     availableHistoryDatesByLottery,
01802:     lotteryKeySafe,
01803:   ]);
01804: 
01805:   return {
01806:     LOOKBACK_ALL,
01807:     LOOKBACK_OPTIONS,
01808:     LOTTERY_OPTIONS,
01809: 
01810:     lotteryKeySafe,
01811:     ymdSafe,

00001: import { safeStr, isYMD, toHourBucket } from "./top3.formatters";
00002: 
00003: import {
00004:   pickDrawYMD,
00005:   pickDrawHour,
00006:   pickPrize1GrupoFromDraw,
00007:   getScheduleForLottery,
00008: } from "./top3.engine";
00009: 
00010: import {
00011:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
00012:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
00013:   FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
00014: } from "./top3.constants";
00015: 
00016: // ===============================
00017: // TOP3 LOG SYSTEM (VALIDAÇÃO REAL)
00018: // ===============================
00019: 
00020: function normHour(h) {
00021:   return toHourBucket(h) || "";
00022: }
00023: 
00024: function makeKey(ymd, hour) {
00025:   const y = safeStr(ymd);
00026:   const h = normHour(hour);
00027:   return isYMD(y) && h ? `${y}_${h}` : "";
00028: }
00029: 
00030: function parseTargetDate(ymd, hour) {
00031:   if (!isYMD(ymd)) return null;
00032: 
00033:   const hourBucket = normHour(hour);
00034:   const m = String(hourBucket || "").match(/^(\d{2})h$/);
00035:   if (!m) return null;
00036: 
00037:   const [Y, M, D] = String(ymd).split("-").map(Number);
00038:   const hh = Number(m[1]);
00039: 
00040:   const dt = new Date(Y, M - 1, D, hh, 0, 0, 0);
00041:   return Number.isNaN(dt.getTime()) ? null : dt;
00042: }
00043: 
00044: function isFutureTarget(ymd, hour) {
00045:   const target = parseTargetDate(ymd, hour);
00046:   if (!target) return false;
00047:   return target.getTime() > Date.now();
00048: }
00049: 
00050: function resolvePendingStatus(ymd, hour, picks) {
00051:   const hasPicks = Array.isArray(picks) && picks.length > 0;
00052:   if (!hasPicks) return "empty";
00053:   return isFutureTarget(ymd, hour) ? "future" : "pending_result";
00054: }
00055: 
00056: function normalizePicks(picks) {
00057:   return Array.from(
00058:     new Set(
00059:       (Array.isArray(picks) ? picks : [])
00060:         .map(Number)
00061:         .filter((n) => Number.isFinite(n) && n >= 1 && n <= 25)
00062:     )
00063:   ).slice(0, 3);
00064: }
00065: 
00066: function normalizeResult(resultValue) {
00067:   const n = Number(resultValue);
00068:   return Number.isFinite(n) && n >= 1 && n <= 25 ? n : null;
00069: }
00070: 
00071: function normalizeLogEntry(entry) {
00072:   if (!entry || typeof entry !== "object") return null;
00073: 
00074:   const ymd = safeStr(entry?.target?.ymd || "");
00075:   const hour = normHour(entry?.target?.hour || "");
00076:   const targetKey = makeKey(ymd, hour);
00077: 
00078:   if (!targetKey) return null;
00079: 
00080:   const picks = normalizePicks(entry?.picks);
00081:   const result = normalizeResult(entry?.result);
00082: 
00083:   const hit =
00084:     result != null && picks.length
00085:       ? picks.includes(result)
00086:       : typeof entry?.hit === "boolean"
00087:         ? entry.hit
00088:         : null;
00089: 
00090:   const status =
00091:     result != null ? "validated" : resolvePendingStatus(ymd, hour, picks);
00092: 
00093:   return {
00094:     ...entry,
00095:     targetKey,
00096:     target: { ymd, hour },
00097:     picks,
00098:     result,
00099:     hit,
00100:     createdAt: Number(entry?.createdAt) || Date.now(),
00101:     validatedAt:
00102:       result != null && Number(entry?.validatedAt)
00103:         ? Number(entry.validatedAt)
00104:         : undefined,
00105:     status,
00106:   };
00107: }
00108: 
00109: function scoreLogEntry(entry) {
00110:   if (!entry) return -1;
00111: 
00112:   let score = 0;
00113:   if (Array.isArray(entry?.picks) && entry.picks.length) score += 20;
00114:   if (entry?.result != null) score += 10;
00115:   if (typeof entry?.hit === "boolean") score += 5;
00116:   if (entry?.validatedAt) score += 2;
00117:   if (entry?.createdAt) score += 1;
00118: 
00119:   return score;
00120: }
00121: 
00122: function normalizeAndDedupeLog(log) {
00123:   const list = Array.isArray(log) ? log : [];
00124:   const byKey = new Map();
00125: 
00126:   for (const raw of list) {
00127:     const entry = normalizeLogEntry(raw);
00128:     if (!entry) continue;
00129: 
00130:     const prev = byKey.get(entry.targetKey);
00131: 
00132:     if (!prev || scoreLogEntry(entry) >= scoreLogEntry(prev)) {
00133:       byKey.set(entry.targetKey, entry);
00134:     }
00135:   }
00136: 
00137:   return [...byKey.values()].sort((a, b) => {
00138:     const aKey = `${a?.target?.ymd || ""}_${a?.target?.hour || ""}`;
00139:     const bKey = `${b?.target?.ymd || ""}_${b?.target?.hour || ""}`;
00140:     return aKey < bKey ? -1 : aKey > bKey ? 1 : 0;
00141:   });
00142: }
00143: 
00144: function getTop3Log() {
00145:   try {
00146:     const raw = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem("top3_log") || "[]");
00147:     return normalizeAndDedupeLog(raw);
00148:   } catch {
00149:     return [];
00150:   }
00151: }
00152: 
00153: function saveTop3Log(log) {
00154:   try {
00155:     const normalized = normalizeAndDedupeLog(log).slice(-200);
00156:     localStorage.setItem("top3_log", JSON.stringify(normalized));
00157:   } catch {}
00158: }
00159: 
00160: function registerPrediction({
00161:   targetKey,
00162:   targetYmd,
00163:   targetHour,
00164:   picks,
00165:   snapshot = [],
00166:   engineVersion = "",
00167: }) {
00168:   const ymd = safeStr(targetYmd);
00169:   const hour = normHour(targetHour);
00170:   const normalizedKey = targetKey || makeKey(ymd, hour);
00171: 
00172:   if (!isYMD(ymd) || !hour || !normalizedKey) return;
00173: 
00174:   const normalizedPicks = normalizePicks(picks);
00175:   if (!normalizedPicks.length) return;
00176: 
00177:   const log = getTop3Log();
00178: 
00179:   const idx = log.findIndex(
00180:     (l) => String(l?.targetKey || "") === String(normalizedKey)
00181:   );
00182: 
00183:   const status = resolvePendingStatus(ymd, hour, normalizedPicks);
00184: 
00185:   if (idx >= 0) {
00186:     const prev = log[idx] || {};
00187: 
00188:     if (prev.result != null || prev.status === "validated") return;
00189: 
00190:     log[idx] = {
00191:       ...prev,
00192:       targetKey: normalizedKey,
00193:       target: { ymd, hour },
00194:       picks: normalizedPicks,
00195:       result: null,
00196:       hit: null,
00197:       snapshot:
00198:         Array.isArray(prev?.snapshot) && prev.snapshot.length
00199:           ? prev.snapshot
00200:           : Array.isArray(snapshot)
00201:             ? snapshot
00202:             : [],
00203:       engineVersion:
00204:         safeStr(prev?.engineVersion || engineVersion || "V3_STATISTICAL"),
00205:       createdAt: prev.createdAt || Date.now(),
00206:       status,
00207:     };
00208:   } else {
00209:     log.push({
00210:       targetKey: normalizedKey,
00211:       target: { ymd, hour },
00212:       picks: normalizedPicks,
00213:       result: null,
00214:       hit: null,
00215:       snapshot: Array.isArray(snapshot) ? snapshot : [],
00216:       engineVersion: safeStr(engineVersion || "V3_STATISTICAL"),
00217:       createdAt: Date.now(),
00218:       status,
00219:     });
00220:   }
00221: 
00222:   saveTop3Log(log);
00223: }
00224: 
00225: function registerResult({ targetKey, resultGrupo }) {
00226:   const log = getTop3Log();
00227:   const normalizedTargetKey = safeStr(targetKey);
00228:   const result = normalizeResult(resultGrupo);
00229: 
00230:   if (!normalizedTargetKey || result == null) return;
00231: 
00232:   const idx = log.findIndex(
00233:     (l) => String(l?.targetKey || "") === String(normalizedTargetKey)
00234:   );
00235: 
00236:   if (idx === -1) return;
00237: 
00238:   const picks = normalizePicks(log[idx]?.picks);
00239: 
00240:   log[idx] = {
00241:     ...log[idx],
00242:     picks,
00243:     result,
00244:     hit: picks.includes(result),
00245:     validatedAt: Date.now(),
00246:     status: "validated",
00247:   };
00248: 
00249:   saveTop3Log(log);
00250: }
00251: 
00252: function findRealDrawByTarget({ targetYmd, targetHour, todayDraws, rangeDraws }) {
00253:   const ymd = safeStr(targetYmd);
00254:   const hour = normHour(targetHour);
00255: 
00256:   if (!isYMD(ymd) || !hour) return null;
00257: 
00258:   const sameTarget = (d) =>
00259:     pickDrawYMD(d) === ymd && normHour(pickDrawHour(d)) === hour;
00260: 
00261:   const realToday =
00262:     (Array.isArray(todayDraws) ? todayDraws : []).find(sameTarget) || null;
00263: 
00264:   const realRange =
00265:     (Array.isArray(rangeDraws) ? rangeDraws : []).find(sameTarget) || null;
00266: 
00267:   return realToday || realRange || null;
00268: }
00269: 
00270: function reconcilePendingTop3Log({ todayDraws, rangeDraws }) {
00271:   const log = getTop3Log();
00272:   if (!Array.isArray(log) || !log.length) return;
00273: 
00274:   let changed = false;
00275: 
00276:   const nextLog = log.map((entry) => {
00277:     if (!entry || entry.result != null) return entry;
00278: 
00279:     const targetYmd = safeStr(entry?.target?.ymd || "");
00280:     const targetHour = normHour(entry?.target?.hour || "");
00281: 
00282:     if (!isYMD(targetYmd) || !targetHour) return entry;
00283:     if (isFutureTarget(targetYmd, targetHour)) return entry;
00284: 
00285:     const real = findRealDrawByTarget({
00286:       targetYmd,
00287:       targetHour,
00288:       todayDraws,
00289:       rangeDraws,
00290:     });
00291: 
00292:     if (!real) return entry;
00293: 
00294:     const result = normalizeResult(pickPrize1GrupoFromDraw(real));
00295:     if (result == null) return entry;
00296: 
00297:     const picks = normalizePicks(entry?.picks);
00298: 
00299:     changed = true;
00300: 
00301:     return {
00302:       ...entry,
00303:       picks,
00304:       result,
00305:       hit: picks.includes(result),
00306:       validatedAt: Date.now(),
00307:       status: "validated",
00308:     };
00309:   });
00310: 
00311:   if (changed) saveTop3Log(nextLog);
00312: }
00313: 
00314: function ensureDayTimeline({ ymd, lotteryKey }) {
00315:   if (!isYMD(ymd)) return;
00316: 
00317:   const schedule = getScheduleForLottery({
00318:     lotteryKey,
00319:     ymd,
00320:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
00321:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
00322:     FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
00323:   });
00324: 
00325:   if (!Array.isArray(schedule) || !schedule.length) return;
00326: 
00327:   const log = getTop3Log();
00328: 
00329:   for (const h of schedule) {
00330:     const hour = normHour(h);
00331:     const targetKey = makeKey(ymd, hour);
00332: 
00333:     if (!targetKey) continue;
00334: 
00335:     const idx = log.findIndex(
00336:       (l) => String(l?.targetKey || "") === String(targetKey)
00337:     );
00338: 
00339:     if (idx >= 0) {
00340:       const prev = log[idx] || {};
00341:       const picks = normalizePicks(prev?.picks);
00342:       const result = normalizeResult(prev?.result);
00343:       const status =
00344:         result != null ? "validated" : resolvePendingStatus(ymd, hour, picks);
00345: 
00346:       log[idx] = {
00347:         ...prev,
00348:         targetKey,
00349:         target: { ymd, hour },
00350:         picks,
00351:         result,
00352:         hit: result != null ? picks.includes(result) : prev?.hit ?? null,
00353:         createdAt: prev?.createdAt || Date.now(),
00354:         validatedAt: result != null ? prev?.validatedAt : undefined,
00355:         status,
00356:       };
00357:     } else {
00358:       log.push({
00359:         targetKey,
00360:         target: { ymd, hour },
00361:         picks: [],
00362:         result: null,
00363:         hit: null,
00364:         createdAt: Date.now(),
00365:         status: "empty",
00366:       });
00367:     }
00368:   }
00369: 
00370:   saveTop3Log(log);
00371: }
00372: 
00373: export {
00374:   getTop3Log,
00375:   saveTop3Log,
00376:   registerPrediction,
00377:   registerResult,
00378:   reconcilePendingTop3Log,
00379:   ensureDayTimeline,
00380:   isFutureTarget,
00381:   makeKey,
00382: };

00274: function clampColsFromItemMilharesCols(milharesCols, expectedCols = 4, perCol = 5) {
00275:   const colsArr = Array.isArray(milharesCols) ? milharesCols : [];
00276:   const out = [];

00285:       .slice(0, perCol);

00289:   }

00292:     out.push({ dezena: "", items: Array(perCol).fill("") });
00293:   }

00298: function build20ByDezena({ grupo, baseMilhares, perCol = 5 }) {
00299:   const g = Number(grupo);
00300:   const dezenas = getDezenasFixasFromGrupo(g);
00301: 
00302:   if (!dezenas.length) {
00303:     return { dezenas: [], rows: [], flat20: [] };
00304:   }
00305: 
00306:   const input = Array.isArray(baseMilhares) ? baseMilhares : [];
00307:   const normalized = input
00308:     .map(normalizeMilharStr)
00309:     .filter((x) => /^\d{4}$/.test(x));
00310: 
00311:   const byDz = new Map();
00312:   for (const dz of dezenas) byDz.set(dz, []);
00313: 
00314:   for (const m4 of normalized) {
00315:     const dz = dezenaFromMilhar(m4);
00316:     if (byDz.has(dz)) byDz.get(dz).push(m4);
00317:   }
00318: 
00319:   const seenCent = new Set();
00320:   const seenMilhar = new Set();
00321: 
00322:   const cols = {};
00323:   for (const dz of dezenas) cols[dz] = [];
00324: 
00325:   const tryPush = (dz, m4) => {
00326:     const mm = normalizeMilharStr(m4);
00327:     if (!mm) return false;
00328: 
00329:     const c3 = centenaFromMilhar(mm);
00330:     if (!c3) return false;
00331: 
00332:     if (seenCent.has(c3)) return false;
00333:     if (seenMilhar.has(mm)) return false;
00334: 
00335:     cols[dz].push(mm);
00336:     seenCent.add(c3);
00337:     seenMilhar.add(mm);
00338:     return true;
00339:   };
00340: 
00341:   for (const dz of dezenas) {
00342:     const arr = byDz.get(dz) || [];
00343:     for (const m4 of arr) {
00344:       if (cols[dz].length >= perCol) break;
00345:       tryPush(dz, m4);
00346:     }
00347:   }
00348: 
00349:   for (const dz of dezenas) {
00350:     while (cols[dz].length < perCol) cols[dz].push("");
00351:     if (cols[dz].length > perCol) cols[dz] = cols[dz].slice(0, perCol);
00352:   }
00353: 
00354:   const rows = [];
00355:   for (let r = 0; r < perCol; r += 1) {
00356:     rows.push(dezenas.map((dz) => cols[dz][r] || ""));
00357:   }
00358: 
00359:   const flat20 = rows.flat().filter(Boolean);
00360:   return { dezenas, rows, flat20 };
00361: }
