00412: function analyzeTop3Hit(top3, resultSource, resultMilhar) {
00413:   if (!Array.isArray(top3) || !top3.length) {
00414:     return {
00415:       type: "none",
00416:       score: 0,
00417:       position: -1,
00418:       predictionPosition: -1,
00419:       resultPosition: -1,
00420:       podiumMedal: "",
00421:       matchedValue: "",
00422:       matchedGrupo: null,
00423:       matchedMilhar: "",
00424:     };
00425:   }
00426: 
00427:   const officialPodium = Array.isArray(resultSource)
00428:     ? resultSource.filter(Boolean).slice(0, 3)
00429:     : [
00430:         {
00431:           position: 1,
00432:           grupo: Number(resultSource),
00433:           milhar: normalizeMilharStr(resultMilhar),
00434:         },
00435:       ].filter(
00436:         (item) =>
00437:           Number.isFinite(item.grupo) &&
00438:           item.grupo >= 1 &&
00439:           item.grupo <= 25
00440:       );
00441: 
00442:   if (!officialPodium.length) {
00443:     return {
00444:       type: "none",
00445:       score: 0,
00446:       position: -1,
00447:       predictionPosition: -1,
00448:       resultPosition: -1,
00449:       podiumMedal: "",
00450:       matchedValue: "",
00451:       matchedGrupo: null,
00452:       matchedMilhar: "",
00453:     };
00454:   }
00455: 
00456:   for (const officialPrize of officialPodium) {
00457:     const grupoNum = Number(officialPrize?.grupo);
00458:     const milhar = normalizeMilharStr(
00459:       officialPrize?.milhar
00460:     );
00461:     const centena = milhar
00462:       ? milhar.slice(-3)
00463:       : "";
00464: 
00465:     for (
00466:       let predictionIndex = 0;
00467:       predictionIndex < top3.length;
00468:       predictionIndex += 1
00469:     ) {
00470:       const item = top3[predictionIndex];
00471:       const grupo = Number(item?.grupo);
00472: 
00473:       const milhares = (
00474:         Array.isArray(item?.milhares20)
00475:           ? item.milhares20
00476:           : Array.isArray(item?.milhares)
00477:             ? item.milhares
00478:             : []
00479:       )
00480:         .map(normalizeMilharStr)
00481:         .filter((value) => /^\d{4}$/.test(value));
00482: 
00483:       const centenas = milhares
00484:         .map((value) => centenaFromMilhar(value))
00485:         .filter((value) => /^\d{3}$/.test(value));
00486: 
00487:       let type = "miss";
00488:       let score = 0;
00489:       let matchedValue = "";
00490: 
00491:       if (milhar && milhares.includes(milhar)) {
00492:         type = "hit_exact";
00493:         score = 100;
00494:         matchedValue = milhar;
00495:       } else if (centena && centenas.includes(centena)) {
00496:         type = "hit_centena";
00497:         score = 66.67;
00498:         matchedValue = centena;
00499:       } else if (
00500:         Number.isFinite(grupoNum) &&
00501:         grupo === grupoNum
00502:       ) {
00503:         type = "hit_grupo";
00504:         score = 33.33;
00505:         matchedValue = milhar
00506:           ? milhar.slice(-2)
00507:           : formatGrupo(grupoNum);
00508:       }
00509: 
00510:       if (type !== "miss") {
00511:         const resultPosition = Number(
00512:           officialPrize?.position
00513:         );
00514: 
00515:         return {
00516:           type,
00517:           score,
00518:           position: predictionIndex + 1,
00519:           predictionPosition: predictionIndex + 1,
00520:           resultPosition,
00521:           podiumMedal:
00522:             podiumMedalFromPosition(resultPosition),
00523:           matchedValue,
00524:           matchedGrupo: grupoNum,
00525:           matchedMilhar: milhar,
00526:         };
00527:       }
00528:     }
00529:   }
00530: 
00531:   return {
00532:     type: "miss",
00533:     score: 0,
00534:     position: -1,
00535:     predictionPosition: -1,
00536:     resultPosition: -1,
00537:     podiumMedal: "",
00538:     matchedValue: "",
00539:     matchedGrupo: null,
00540:     matchedMilhar: "",
00541:   };
00542: }
00543: 
00544: function ImgWithFallback({ srcs, alt, size = 84, style }) {
00545:   const list = useMemo(
00546:     () => (Array.isArray(srcs) ? srcs.filter(Boolean) : []),
00547:     [srcs]
00548:   );
00549: 
00550:   const [i, setI] = useState(0);
00551:   const listKey = useMemo(() => list.join("|"), [list]);
00552: 
00553:   useEffect(() => {
00554:     setI(0);
00555:   }, [listKey]);
00556: 
00557:   const src = list[i] || "";
00558: 
00559:   const onError = () => {
00560:     setI((prev) => (prev < list.length - 1 ? prev + 1 : prev));
00561:   };
00562: 
00563:   return (
00564:     <div
00565:       style={{
00566:         width: size,
00567:         height: size,
00568:         borderRadius: 16,
00569:         border: "1px solid rgba(201,168,62,0.36)",
00570:         background:
00571:           "linear-gradient(180deg, rgba(255,255,255,0.04), rgba(0,0,0,0.28))",
00572:         display: "grid",
00573:         placeItems: "center",
00574:         overflow: "hidden",
00575:         boxShadow: "inset 0 0 0 1px rgba(255,255,255,0.04)",
00576:         ...style,
00577:       }}
00578:     >
00579:       {src ? (
00580:         <img
00581:           src={src}
00582:           alt={alt || ""}
00583:           width={size}
00584:           height={size}
00585:           onError={onError}
00586:           style={{
00587:             width: "100%",
00588:             height: "100%",
00589:             objectFit: "cover",
00590:             display: "block",
00591:             imageRendering: "auto",
00592:           }}
00593:         />
00594:       ) : (
00595:         <div style={{ color: "rgba(255,255,255,0.55)", fontSize: 12 }}>
00596:           sem imagem
00597:         </div>
00598:       )}
00599:     </div>
00600:   );
00601: }
00602: 
00603: 
00604: function getWeekdayNameBR(ymd) {
00605:   const s = String(ymd || "").trim();
00606:   const m = s.match(/^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})$/);
00607:   if (!m) return "Dia da semana";
00608: 
00609:   const d = new Date(Date.UTC(Number(m[1]), Number(m[2]) - 1, Number(m[3])));
00610:   const names = [
00611:     "Domingo",
00612:     "Segunda-feira",
00613:     "Terça-feira",
00614:     "Quarta-feira",
00615:     "Quinta-feira",
00616:     "Sexta-feira",
00617:     "Sábado",
00618:   ];
00619: 
00620:   return names[d.getUTCDay()] || "Dia da semana";
00621: }
00622: 
00623: function Top3Card({
00624:   item,
00625:   idx,
00626:   theme,
00627:   copiedAllKey,
00628:   copiedCellKey,
00629:   setCopiedAllKey,
00630:   setCopiedCellKey,
00631:   copyText,
00632:   build16,
00633:   buildMilhares,
00634:   build20,
00635: }) {
00636:   const t = theme;
00637: 
00638:   const grupoTxt = formatGrupo(item?.grupo);
00639:   const animal = String(item?.animal || "").trim();
00640: 
00641:   const samplesRaw = Number(item?.meta?.samples ?? item?.samples ?? 0);
00642:   const samples = Number.isFinite(samplesRaw)
00643:     ? Math.max(0, Math.trunc(samplesRaw))
00644:     : 0;
00645: 
00646:   const freqRaw = Number(item?.freq ?? 0);
00647:   const freq = Number.isFinite(freqRaw)
00648:     ? Math.max(0, Math.trunc(freqRaw))
00649:     : 0;
00650: 
00651:   const denom = samples > 0 ? samples * 5 : 0;
00652:   const derivedScore = denom > 0 ? freq / denom : 0;
00653: 
00654:   const pct = toPercent(
00655:     item?.probPct ??
00656:       item?.prob ??
00657:       item?.probCond ??
00658:       item?.score ??
00659:       derivedScore
00660:   );
00661: 
00662:   const iconSrcs =
00663:     Array.isArray(item?.imgIcon) && item.imgIcon.length
00664:       ? item.imgIcon
00665:       : Array.isArray(item?.imgBg)
00666:         ? item.imgBg
00667:         : [];
00668: 
00669:   const hasCols =
00670:     Array.isArray(item?.milharesCols) &&
00671:     item.milharesCols.length >= 4 &&
00672:     Array.isArray(item.milharesCols[0]?.items);
00673: 
00674:   let dezenasHeader = [];
00675:   let gridRows = Array.from({ length: 5 }, () => Array(4).fill(""));
00676:   let flat20 = [];
00677: 
00678:   if (hasCols) {
00679:     const cols4 = clampColsFromItemMilharesCols(item.milharesCols, 4, 5);
00680:     dezenasHeader = cols4.map((c) => String(c.dezena || ""));
00681:     gridRows = Array.from({ length: 5 }, (_, r) =>
00682:       cols4.map((c) => c.items[r] || "")
00683:     );
00684:     flat20 = gridRows.flat().filter(Boolean);
00685:   } else {
00686:     let milharesBase = [];
00687: 
00688:     const m20 = Array.isArray(item?.milhares20) ? item.milhares20 : null;
00689:     const mAny = Array.isArray(item?.milhares) ? item.milhares : null;
00690: 
00691:     if (m20 && m20.length) milharesBase = m20.slice(0);
00692:     else if (mAny && mAny.length) milharesBase = mAny.slice(0);
00693: 
00694:     if (!milharesBase.length) {
00695:       const g = Number(item?.grupo);
00696: 
00697:       if (Number.isFinite(g) && g > 0) {
00698:         if (typeof build20 === "function") {
00699:           const out20 = build20(g);
00700:           const slots20 = Array.isArray(out20?.slots) ? out20.slots : [];
00701:           milharesBase = slots20.map((x) => x?.milhar).filter(Boolean);
00702:         } else if (typeof buildMilhares === "function") {
00703:           const out = buildMilhares(g, 20);
00704:           if (Array.isArray(out)) {
00705:             milharesBase = out.slice(0);
00706:           } else if (out && Array.isArray(out.slots)) {
00707:             milharesBase = out.slots.map((x) => x?.milhar).filter(Boolean);
00708:           }
00709:         } else if (typeof build16 === "function") {
00710:           const out16 = build16(g);
00711:           const slots16 = Array.isArray(out16?.slots) ? out16.slots : [];
00712:           milharesBase = slots16.map((x) => x?.milhar).filter(Boolean);
00713:         }
00714:       }
00715:     }
00716: 
00717:     const grupoNum = Number(item?.grupo);
00718:     const grid = build20ByDezena({
00719:       grupo: grupoNum,
00720:       baseMilhares: milharesBase,
00721:       perCol: 5,
00722:     });
00723: 
00724:     dezenasHeader = grid.dezenas;
00725:     gridRows = grid.rows;
00726:     flat20 = grid.flat20;
00727:   }
00728: 
00729:   const key = `${String(item?.grupo ?? "g")}__${animal || "x"}__${idx}`;
00730:   const rank = idx + 1;
00731:   // TOP3 EQUAL CARD LAYOUT
00732:   // A hierarquia permanece no ranking, mas os três cards usam a mesma estrutura.
00733:   const isHero = false;
00734: 
00735:   const title =
00736:     idx === 0 ? "1º MAIS FORTE" : idx === 1 ? "2º MAIS FORTE" : "3º MAIS FORTE";
00737: 
00738:   const engineScore = Number(item?.score ?? 0);
00739:   const evidenceCount = Number(item?.evidenceCount ?? 0);
00740:   const evidenceModules = Array.isArray(item?.evidenceModules)
00741:     ? item.evidenceModules.filter(Boolean)
00742:     : [];
00743: 
00744:   const explain = item?.meta?.explain || {};
00745:   const detailMap =
00746:     explain?.details && typeof explain.details === "object"
00747:       ? explain.details
00748:       : {};
00749: 
00750:   const transitionDetail = detailMap.transition || null;
00751:   const hourDetail = detailMap.hour || null;
00752:   const dowDetail = detailMap.dow || detailMap.weekday || null;
00753:   const sceneDetail = detailMap.scene || null;
00754: 
00755:   const periodFrom = String(item?.meta?.period?.from || "").trim();
00756:   const periodTo = String(item?.meta?.period?.to || "").trim();
00757: 
00758:   const periodLabel =
00759:     periodFrom && periodTo
00760:       ? `Período analisado: ${ymdToBR(periodFrom)} até ${ymdToBR(periodTo)}`
00761:       : samples > 0
00762:         ? `Amostra analisada: ${samples} sorteios`
00763:         : "";
00764: 
00765:   const baseGrupo = formatGrupo(item?.meta?.trigger?.grupo);
00766:   const baseHour = String(item?.meta?.trigger?.hour || "").trim();
00767:   const targetHour = String(item?.meta?.next?.hour || "").trim();
00768: 
00769:   const smartReasons = [];
00770: 
00771:   if (periodLabel) {
00772:     smartReasons.push(periodLabel);
00773:   }
00774: 
00775:   if (transitionDetail) {
00776:     const first = Number(transitionDetail.firstCount || 0);
00777:     const top5 = Number(transitionDetail.top3Count || 0);
00778:     const amostras = Number(transitionDetail.samples || 0);
00779: 
00780:     smartReasons.push(
00781:       `Após G${baseGrupo} às ${baseHour}, G${grupoTxt} apareceu ${first}x em 1º e ${top5}x no TOP3 para o alvo ${targetHour}.`
00782:     );
00783: 
00784:     if (amostras > 0) {
00785:       smartReasons.push(`Cenários de transição avaliados: ${amostras}.`);
00786:     }
00787:   }
00788: 
00789:   if (hourDetail) {
00790:     const first = Number(hourDetail.firstCount || 0);
00791:     const top5 = Number(hourDetail.top3Count || 0);
00792: 
00793:     smartReasons.push(
00794:       `No horário ${targetHour}, G${grupoTxt} apareceu ${first}x em 1º e ${top5}x no TOP3.`
00795:     );
00796:   }
00797: 
00798:   if (dowDetail) {
00799:     const first = Number(dowDetail.firstCount || 0);
00800:     const top5 = Number(dowDetail.top3Count || 0);
00801: 
00802:     smartReasons.push(
00803:       `No mesmo dia da semana, G${grupoTxt} apareceu ${first}x em 1º e ${top5}x no TOP3.`
00804:     );
00805:   }
00806: 
00807:   if (sceneDetail && Number(sceneDetail.samples || 0) > 0) {
00808:     smartReasons.push(
00809:       `Cenários semelhantes encontrados: ${Number(sceneDetail.samples || 0)}.`
00810:     );
00811:   }
00812: 
00813:   smartReasons.push(`Probabilidade final do G${grupoTxt}: ${pct.toFixed(2)}%.`);
00814: 
00815:   const evidenceReasons = smartReasons
00816:     .concat(Array.isArray(item?.reasons) ? item.reasons.filter(Boolean) : [])
00817:     .filter(Boolean)
00818:     .slice(0, 7);
00819: 
00820:   const visibleEvidenceCount = Math.max(
00821:     evidenceCount || 0,
00822:     evidenceModules.length || 0,
00823:     evidenceReasons.length || 0
00824:   );
00825: 
00826:   const trendLabel =
00827:     engineScore >= pct
00828:       ? "TENDÊNCIA POSITIVA"
00829:       : "TENDÊNCIA NEUTRA";
00830: 
00831:   const displayScore = Math.round(
00832:     Math.max(
00833:       1,
00834:       Math.min(
00835:         99,
00836:         Number.isFinite(engineScore) && engineScore > 0
00837:           ? engineScore
00838:           : pct * 10
00839:       )
00840:     )
00841:   );
00842: 
00843:   const scoreBarPct = Math.max(4, Math.min(100, displayScore));
00844: 
00845:   const baseAnimalName =
00846:     typeof getAnimalLabel === "function"
00847:       ? String(getAnimalLabel(Number(item?.meta?.trigger?.grupo)) || "").trim()
00848:       : "";
00849: 
00850:   const baseLabel = baseAnimalName
00851:     ? `${baseAnimalName} (G${baseGrupo})`
00852:     : `G${baseGrupo}`;
00853: 
00854:   const targetLabel = animal
00855:     ? `${animal} (G${grupoTxt})`
00856:     : `G${grupoTxt}`;
00857: 
00858:   const targetWeekdayName = getWeekdayNameBR(item?.meta?.next?.ymd || item?.targetYmd || "");
00859: 
00860:   const transitionFirst = Number(transitionDetail?.firstCount || 0);
00861:   const transitionTop3 = Number(transitionDetail?.top3Count || transitionDetail?.top5Count || 0);
00862:   const transitionSamples = Number(transitionDetail?.samples || 0);
00863: 
00864:   const hourFirst = Number(hourDetail?.firstCount || 0);
00865:   const dowFirst = Number(dowDetail?.firstCount || 0);
00866:   const sceneSamples = Number(sceneDetail?.samples || 0);
00867: 
00868:   const premiumForceLabel =
00869:     displayScore >= 90
00870:       ? "MUITO FORTE"
00871:       : displayScore >= 75
00872:         ? "FORTE"
00873:         : displayScore >= 60
00874:           ? "MODERADA"
00875:           : "BAIXA";
00876: 
00877:   const premiumReliabilityLabel =
00878:     displayScore >= 85
00879:       ? "ALTA"
00880:       : displayScore >= 70
00881:         ? "MÉDIA"
00882:         : visibleEvidenceCount > 0
00883:           ? "EM FORMAÇÃO"
00884:           : "SEM EVIDÊNCIAS";
00885: 
00886:   const doCopyAll = async () => {
00887:     const payload = flat20.join(" ").trim();
00888:     if (!payload) return;
00889: 
00890:     const ok = await copyText(payload);
00891:     if (ok) setCopiedAllKey(key);
00892:   };
00893: 
00894:   const doCopyOne = async (m, rIdx, cIdx) => {
00895:     const mm = normalizeMilharStr(m);
00896:     if (!mm) return;
00897: 
00898:     const ok = await copyText(mm);
00899:     if (ok) setCopiedCellKey(`${key}__${rIdx}__${cIdx}`);
00900:   };
00901: 
00902:   return (
00903:     <article
00904:       className={`top3-card ${isHero ? "top3-card--hero" : "top3-card--secondary"}`}
00905:     >
00906:       <div className="top3-card__topline">
00907:         <div className="top3-rankBadge">{rank}</div>
00908:         <div className="top3-card__label">🏅 {title}</div>
00909:       </div>
00910: 
00911:       <div className="top3-card__summary">
00912:         <div className="top3-card__identity">
00913:           <ImgWithFallback
00914:             srcs={iconSrcs}
00915:             alt={animal ? animal : `G${grupoTxt}`}
00916:             size={isHero ? 92 : 84}
00917:           />
00918: 
00919:           <div className="top3-card__identityText">
00920:             <div className="top3-card__group">GRUPO {grupoTxt}</div>
00921:             <div className="top3-card__animal">
00922:               {animal ? animal.toUpperCase() : "—"}
00923:             </div>
00924:           </div>
00925:         </div>
00926: 
00927:         <div className="top3-card__confidence">
00928:           <div className="top3-card__confidenceLabel">CONFIANÇA</div>
00929:           <div className="top3-card__confidenceValue">{pct.toFixed(2)}%</div>
00930:           <div className="top3-card__confidenceBar">
00931:             <div
00932:               className="top3-card__confidenceBarFill"
00933:               style={{ width: `${pct}%`, background: t.accent }}
00934:             />
00935:           </div>
00936:         </div>
00937:       </div>
00938: 
00939:       <div
00940:         className="top3-card__engine top3-card__engine--premium"
00941:         style={{
00942:           borderTop: "1px solid rgba(212,175,55,0.22)",
00943:           paddingTop: 14,
00944:           display: "grid",
00945:           gridTemplateColumns: isHero ? "300px minmax(0, 1fr)" : "1fr",
00946:           gap: 14,
00947:           alignItems: "stretch",
00948:         }}
00949:       >
00950:         {isHero ? (
00951:           <div
00952:             style={{
00953:               border: "1px solid rgba(212,175,55,0.30)",
00954:               borderRadius: 18,
00955:               padding: 14,
00956:               background:
00957:                 "radial-gradient(circle at 50% 0%, rgba(212,175,55,0.18), rgba(0,0,0,0.12) 48%, rgba(0,0,0,0.28))",
00958:               boxShadow: "0 18px 45px rgba(0,0,0,0.32)",
00959:             }}
00960:           >
00961:             <div style={{ display: "grid", gap: 12 }}>
00962:               <div style={{ display: "flex", alignItems: "center", gap: 10 }}>
00963:                 <div className="top3-rankBadge">{rank}</div>
00964:                 <div className="top3-card__label">⭐ {title}</div>

00966:

00990:                 </div>
00991:               </div>
00992:

00994:                 style={{

00996:                   borderRadius: 14,
00997:                   padding: 14,
00998:                   background: "rgba(0,0,0,0.22)",
00999:                 }}
01000:               >

01002:                 <div

01004:                     fontSize: 42,

01044:         ) : null}
01045:

01053:           }}

01057:               display: "grid",
01058:               gridTemplateColumns: isHero
01059:                 ? "repeat(3, minmax(0, 1fr))"
01060:                 : "1fr",
01061:               gap: 10,
01062:             }}
01063:           >
01064:             <div className="top3-metaItem">

01070:               </div>

01072:

01079:                 <br />

01081:               </div>

01087:                 Às {targetHour || "—"}
01088:                 <br />
01089:                 {targetLabel} em 1º: {hourFirst}x
01090:               </div>
01091:             </div>
01092: 
01093:             <div className="top3-metaItem">
01094:               <div className="top3-metaItem__label">📅 Dia da semana</div>
01095:               <div className="top3-metaItem__value">
01096:                 {targetWeekdayName}
01097:                 <br />
01098:                 {targetLabel} em 1º: {dowFirst}x
01099:               </div>
01100:             </div>
01101: 
01102:             <div className="top3-metaItem">
01103:               <div className="top3-metaItem__label">🧠 Cenários semelhantes</div>
01104:               <div className="top3-metaItem__value">
01105:                 {
01106:       sceneSamples > 0
01107:         ? `${sceneSamples} cenários semelhantes`
01108:         : transitionSamples > 0
01109:           ? `${transitionSamples} transições históricas`
01110:           : `${samples} registros históricos`
01111:     }
01112:               </div>
01113:             </div>
01114: 
01115:             <div className="top3-metaItem">
01116:               <div className="top3-metaItem__label">🎯 Frequência histórica da transição</div>
01117:               <div className="top3-metaItem__value">
01118:                 {transitionFirst > 0 && transitionSamples > 0
01119:                   ? `${transitionFirst} ocorrências em ${transitionSamples} transições`
01120:                   : "Em apuração"}
01121:               </div>
01122:             </div>
01123:           </div>
01124: 
01125:           <div
01126:             style={{
01127:               marginTop: 12,
01128:               border: "1px solid rgba(212,175,55,0.20)",
01129:               borderRadius: 14,
01130:               padding: 12,
01131:               display: "flex",
01132:               gap: 10,
01133:               alignItems: "center",
01134:               background: "rgba(212,175,55,0.055)",
01135:             }}
01136:           >
01137:             <div style={{ fontSize: 26 }}>⭐</div>
01138:             <div>
01139:               <div
01140:                 style={{
01141:                   color: t.accent,
01142:                   fontWeight: 900,
01143:                   textTransform: "uppercase",
01144:                 }}
01145:               >
01146:                 Score — confiança da análise
01147:               </div>
01148:               <div style={{ color: "rgba(255,255,255,0.82)", fontSize: 13 }}>
01149:                 {displayScore}/100 · {visibleEvidenceCount} evidências estatísticas · {trendLabel}
01150:               </div>
01151:             </div>
01152:           </div>
01153:         </div>
01154:       </div>
01155: 
01156:       <div className="top3-card__body">
01157:         <div
01158:           className="top3-card__actions"
01159:           style={{
01160:             display: "flex",
01161:             justifyContent: "flex-end",
01162:             alignItems: "center",
01163:           }}
01164:         >
01165:           <button
01166:             type="button"
01167:             onClick={doCopyAll}
01168:             className="pp-btn"
01169:             title="Copiar as 20 milhares"
01170:           >
01171:             {copiedAllKey === key ? "✅ Copiado" : "📋 Copiar"}
01172:           </button>
01173:         </div>
01174: 
01175:         {dezenasHeader.length ? (
01176:           <div className="pp-chipRow">
01177:             {dezenasHeader.map((dz, i) => (
01178:               <div key={`${dz}-${i}`} className="pp-chip">
01179:                 {dz || "—"}
01180:               </div>
01181:             ))}
01182:           </div>
01183:         ) : null}
01184: 
01185:         <div className="pp-gridBox">
01186:           {gridRows.map((row, rIdx) => (
01187:             <div key={rIdx} className="pp-row">
01188:               {row.map((m, cIdx) => {
01189:                 const mm = normalizeMilharStr(m);
01190:                 const empty = !mm ? "1" : "0";
01191:                 const cKey = `${key}__${rIdx}__${cIdx}`;
01192:                 const isCopied = copiedCellKey === cKey;
01193: 
01194:                 return (
01195:                   <div
01196:                     key={`${rIdx}-${cIdx}`}
01197:                     className="pp-pill"
01198:                     data-empty={empty}
01199:                     title={mm ? "Clique para copiar" : ""}
01200:                     onClick={() => doCopyOne(mm, rIdx, cIdx)}
01201:                   >
01202:                     {mm || "—"}
01203:                     {isCopied ? <div className="pp-copiedBadge">COPIADO</div> : null}
01204:                   </div>
01205:                 );
01206:               })}
01207:             </div>
01208:           ))}
01209:         </div>
01210:       </div>
01211:     </article>
01212:   );
01213: }
01214: 
01215: function CompactTop3Result({
01216:   slot,
01217:   status,
01218:   statusLabel,
01219:   statusDetail,
01220:   analysis,
01221: }) {
01222:   const slotTop3 = Array.isArray(slot?.top3)
01223:     ? slot.top3.slice(0, 3)
01224:     : [];
01225: 
01226:   const resultGrupo = getSlotResultGrupo(slot);
01227: 
01228:   const hasResult =
01229:     Number.isFinite(resultGrupo) &&
01230:     resultGrupo >= 1 &&
01231:     resultGrupo <= 25;
01232: 
01233:   const resultAnimal = hasResult
01234:     ? String(getAnimalLabel(resultGrupo) || "")
01235:     : "";
01236: 
01237:   const resultImg = hasResult
01238:     ? getImgFromGrupo(resultGrupo, 64)
01239:     : "";
01240: 
01241:   const resultMilhar = extractResultMilhar(slot);
01242: 
01243:   const matchedPredictionIndex =
01244:     Number.isFinite(Number(analysis?.position)) &&
01245:     Number(analysis.position) >= 1
01246:       ? Number(analysis.position) - 1
01247:       : -1;
01248: 
01249:   const resultPosition = Number(
01250:     analysis?.resultPosition
01251:   );
01252: 
01253:   const medal =
01254:     status !== "hit"
01255:       ? ""
01256:       : resultPosition === 1
01257:         ? "🥇"
01258:         : resultPosition === 2
01259:           ? "🥈"
01260:           : resultPosition === 3
01261:             ? "🥉"
01262:             : "";
01263: 
01264:   const dateLabel = formatYmdHour(
01265:     slot?.targetYmd,
01266:     slot?.targetHour
01267:   );
01268: 
01269:   return (
01270:     <div
01271:       className="timeline-compact"
01272:       title={statusDetail || statusLabel || ""}
01273:       style={{
01274:         width: "100%",
01275:         overflowX: "auto",
01276:       }}
01277:     >
01278:       <div
01279:         className="timeline-compact__twoLine"
01280:         style={{
01281:           display: "grid",
01282:           gridTemplateColumns:
01283:             "minmax(112px, 1.35fr) repeat(3, minmax(54px, 0.72fr)) minmax(82px, 0.95fr)",
01284:           alignItems: "center",
01285:           gap: "6px",
01286:           minWidth: "410px",
01287:           padding: "8px 6px",
01288:         }}
01289:       >
01290:         <div
01291:           className="timeline-compact__date"
01292:           style={{
01293:             gridRow: "1 / span 2",
01294:             alignSelf: "center",
01295:             fontSize: "12px",
01296:             fontWeight: 800,
01297:             lineHeight: 1.2,
01298:             whiteSpace: "nowrap",
01299:           }}
01300:         >
01301:           {dateLabel}
01302:         </div>
01303: 
01304:         {slotTop3.map((item, idx) => {
01305:           const grupo = Number(item?.grupo);
01306: 
01307:           const animal = String(
01308:             item?.animal ||
01309:               getAnimalLabel(grupo) ||
01310:               ""
01311:           ).trim();
01312: 
01313:           const img = Number.isFinite(grupo)
01314:             ? getImgFromGrupo(grupo, 64)
01315:             : "";
01316: 
01317:           const isMatched =
01318:             status === "hit" &&
01319:             matchedPredictionIndex === idx;
01320: 
01321:           return (
01322:             <div
01323:               key={`${String(
01324:                 slot?.targetYmd || "y"
01325:               )}_${String(
01326:                 slot?.targetHour || "h"
01327:               )}_${idx}_${grupo}`}
01328:               className={`timeline-compact__pick ${
01329:                 isMatched
01330:                   ? "timeline-compact__pick--matched"
01331:                   : ""
01332:               }`}
01333:               style={{
01334:                 display: "grid",
01335:                 gridTemplateRows: "34px 18px",
01336:                 justifyItems: "center",
01337:                 alignItems: "center",
01338:                 minWidth: 0,
01339:                 padding: 0,
01340:                 border: "none",
01341:                 background: "transparent",
01342:               }}
01343:               title={`${animal || "Animal"} — G${formatGrupo(
01344:                 grupo
01345:               )}`}
01346:             >
01347:               <div
01348:                 style={{
01349:                   position: "relative",
01350:                   display: "flex",
01351:                   alignItems: "center",
01352:                   justifyContent: "center",
01353:                 }}
01354:               >
01355:                 <ImgWithFallback
01356:                   srcs={img ? [img] : []}
01357:                   alt={
01358:                     animal ||
01359:                     `G${formatGrupo(grupo)}`
01360:                   }
01361:                   size={34}
01362:                   style={{
01363:                     borderRadius: 8,
01364:                     outline: isMatched
01365:                       ? "2px solid rgba(201,168,62,0.95)"
01366:                       : "none",
01367:                     outlineOffset: 1,
01368:                   }}
01369:                 />
01370: 
01371:                 {isMatched && medal ? (
01372:                   <span
01373:                     aria-label="Palpite acertado"
01374:                     style={{
01375:                       position: "absolute",
01376:                       top: -8,
01377:                       right: -13,
01378:                       fontSize: "17px",
01379:                       lineHeight: 1,
01380:                       filter:
01381:                         "drop-shadow(0 1px 2px rgba(0,0,0,0.75))",
01382:                     }}
01383:                   >
01384:                     {medal}
01385:                   </span>
01386:                 ) : null}
01387:               </div>
01388: 
01389:               <div
01390:                 style={{
01391:                   fontSize: "12px",
01392:                   fontWeight: 900,
01393:                   lineHeight: 1,
01394:                   whiteSpace: "nowrap",
01395:                 }}
01396:               >
01397:                 G{formatGrupo(grupo)}
01398:               </div>
01399:             </div>
01400:           );
01401:         })}
01402: 
01403:         <div
01404:           className="timeline-compact__official"
01405:           style={{
01406:             display: "grid",
01407:             gridTemplateRows: "34px 18px",
01408:             justifyItems: "center",
01409:             alignItems: "center",
01410:             minWidth: 0,
01411:             paddingLeft: "6px",
01412:             borderLeft:
01413:               "1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.14)",
01414:           }}
01415:           title={
01416:             hasResult
01417:               ? `${resultAnimal} — G${formatGrupo(
01418:                   resultGrupo
01419:                 )}${
01420:                   resultMilhar
01421:                     ? ` — ${resultMilhar}`
01422:                     : ""
01423:                 }`
01424:               : "Resultado pendente"
01425:           }
01426:         >
01427:           {hasResult ? (
01428:             <>
01429:               <ImgWithFallback
01430:                 srcs={resultImg ? [resultImg] : []}
01431:                 alt={resultAnimal}
01432:                 size={34}
01433:                 style={{ borderRadius: 8 }}
01434:               />
01435: 
01436:               <div
01437:                 style={{
01438:                   fontSize: "11px",
01439:                   fontWeight: 900,
01440:                   lineHeight: 1,
01441:                   whiteSpace: "nowrap",
01442:                 }}
01443:               >
01444:                 G{formatGrupo(resultGrupo)}
01445:                 {resultMilhar
01446:                   ? ` • ${resultMilhar}`
01447:                   : ""}
01448:               </div>
01449:             </>
01450:           ) : (
01451:             <div
01452:               style={{
01453:                 gridRow: "1 / span 2",
01454:                 alignSelf: "center",
01455:                 fontSize: "10px",
01456:                 fontWeight: 800,
01457:               }}
01458:             >
01459:               PENDENTE
01460:             </div>
01461:           )}
01462:         </div>
01463:       </div>
01464:     </div>
01465:   );
01466: }
01467: 
01468: function TimelineSlot({
01469:   slot,
01470:   theme,
01471:   copiedAllKey,
01472:   copiedCellKey,
01473:   setCopiedAllKey,
01474:   setCopiedCellKey,
01475:   copyText,
01476:   build16,
01477:   buildMilhares,
01478:   build20,
01479: }) {
01480:   const t = theme;
01481:   const slotTop3 = Array.isArray(slot?.top3) ? slot.top3.slice(0, 3) : [];
01482: 
01483:   const resultGrupo = getSlotResultGrupo(slot);
01484:   const hasResult = hasOfficialResult(slot);
01485:   const resultAnimal =
01486:     hasResult && Number.isFinite(resultGrupo)
01487:       ? String(getAnimalLabel(resultGrupo) || "")
01488:       : "";
01489: 
01490:   const officialPodium = getOfficialPodium(slot);
01491: 
01492:   const analysis = analyzeTop3Hit(
01493:     slotTop3,
01494:     officialPodium
01495:   );
01496: 
01497:   const status =
01498:     !hasResult
01499:       ? "pending"
01500:       : analysis.type === "miss"
01501:         ? "miss"
01502:         : "hit";
01503: 
01504:   const statusLabel =
01505:     !hasResult
01506:       ? "⏳ PENDENTE"
01507:       : analysis.type === "hit_exact"
01508:         ? "🎯 MILHAR (100%)"
01509:         : analysis.type === "hit_centena"
01510:           ? "🟡 CENTENA (66,67%)"
01511:           : analysis.type === "hit_grupo"
01512:             ? "✅ DEZENA/GRUPO (33,33%)"
01513:             : "❌ ERRO (0%)";
01514: 
01515:   const statusDetail =
01516:     !hasResult
01517:       ? "Aguardando resultado oficial."
01518:       : analysis.type === "hit_exact"
01519:         ? `Milhar exata acertada no palpite #${analysis.position}.`
01520:         : analysis.type === "hit_centena"
01521:           ? `Centena acertada no palpite #${analysis.position}.`
01522:           : analysis.type === "hit_grupo"
01523:             ? `Grupo acertado no palpite #${analysis.position}.`
01524:             : "Nenhum dos 3 palpites foi sorteado.";
01525: 
01526:   return (
01527:     <section className="top3-shell">
01528:       {status === "pending" ? (
01529:         <>
01530:           <div className="timeline-slot__header">
01531:             <div className="timeline-slot__title">
01532:               {formatYmdHour(
01533:                 slot?.targetYmd,
01534:                 slot?.targetHour
01535:               )}
01536:             </div>
01537: 
01538:             <div
01539:               className={`timeline-slot__badge timeline-slot__badge--${status}`}
01540:             >
01541:               {statusLabel}
01542:             </div>
01543:           </div>
01544: 
01545:           <div className="timeline-slot__metaGrid">
01546:             <div className="top3-metaItem">
01547:               <div className="top3-metaItem__label">
01548:                 Base usada
01549:               </div>
01550: 
01551:               <div className="top3-metaItem__value">
01552:                 {formatYmdHour(
01553:                   slot?.baseYmd,
01554:                   slot?.baseHour
01555:                 )}
01556:               </div>
01557:             </div>
01558: 
01559:             <div className="top3-metaItem">
01560:               <div className="top3-metaItem__label">
01561:                 Resultado real
01562:               </div>
01563: 
01564:               <div className="top3-metaItem__value">
01565:                 {hasResult
01566:                   ? `G${formatGrupo(
01567:                       resultGrupo
01568:                     )} • ${resultAnimal.toUpperCase()}`
01569:                   : "Pendente"}
01570:               </div>
01571:             </div>
01572: 
01573:             <div className="top3-metaItem">
01574:               <div className="top3-metaItem__label">
01575:                 Status
01576:               </div>
01577: 
01578:               <div className="top3-metaItem__value">
01579:                 {statusDetail}
01580:               </div>
01581:             </div>
01582:           </div>
01583:         </>
01584:       ) : null}
01585: 
01586:       {!slotTop3.length ? (
01587:         <div className="top3-empty">Sem Top3 calculado para este horário.</div>
01588:       ) : status === "pending" ? (
01589:         <div className="timeline-slot__cards">
01590:           {slotTop3.map((item, i) => (
01591:             <Top3Card
01592:               key={`${slot?.targetYmd || "y"}_${slot?.targetHour || "h"}_${String(item?.grupo ?? i)}`}
01593:               item={item}
01594:               idx={i}
01595:               theme={t}
01596:               copiedAllKey={copiedAllKey}
01597:               copiedCellKey={copiedCellKey}
01598:               setCopiedAllKey={setCopiedAllKey}
01599:               setCopiedCellKey={setCopiedCellKey}
01600:               copyText={copyText}
01601:               build16={build16}
01602:               buildMilhares={buildMilhares}
01603:               build20={build20}
01604:             />
01605:           ))}
01606:         </div>
01607:       ) : (
01608:         <CompactTop3Result
01609:           slot={slot}
01610:           status={status}
01611:           statusLabel={statusLabel}
01612:           statusDetail={statusDetail}
01613:           analysis={analysis}
01614:         />
01615:       )}
01616:     </section>
01617:   );
01618: }
01619: 
01620: export default function Top3View(props) {
01621:   const {
01622:     loading,
01623:     error,
01624:     timelineTop3,
01625:     persistedTop3History,
01626:     availableHistoryDates: controllerHistoryDates,
01627:     top3,
01628:     layerMetaText,
01629:     lastLabel,
01630:     prevLabel,
01631:     theme,
01632:     LOTTERY_OPTIONS,
01633:     lotteryKeySafe,
01634:     setLotteryKey,
01635:     setYmd,
01636:     build16,
01637:     buildMilhares,
01638:     build20,
01639:     analysisYmd,
01640:     analysisHourBucket,
01641:     loadedYmd,
01642:     ymdSafe,
01643:     lastHourBucket,
01644:   } = props || {};
01645: 
01646:   // TODO_BACKTEST_REAL
01647: // Os cards estatísticos deverão consumir auditTop3Backtest()
01648: // evitando cálculos paralelos na interface.
01649: //
01650: // Fase atual:
01651: // apenas corrigindo nomenclatura para refletir o cálculo existente.
01652: 
01653: const list = Array.isArray(top3)
01654:   ? top3.slice(0, 3)
01655:   : [];
01656:   const timeline = (Array.isArray(timelineTop3) ? timelineTop3 : [])
01657:   .slice()
01658:   .sort((a, b) => {
01659:     const ta = `${a?.targetYmd || ""} ${a?.targetHour || ""}`;
01660:     const tb = `${b?.targetYmd || ""} ${b?.targetHour || ""}`;
01661:     return ta.localeCompare(tb);
01662:   });
01663: 
01664:   const showDetailedTimeline = false;
01665: 
01666:   const meta = useMemo(() => {
01667:     const last = lastLabel || "—";
01668:     const prev = prevLabel || "—";
01669:     const layer = cleanLayerText(layerMetaText || "—");
01670:     return { last, prev, layer };
01671:   }, [lastLabel, prevLabel, layerMetaText]);
01672: 
01673:   const headerBase = useMemo(() => meta.last || "—", [meta.last]);
01674: 
01675:   const headerForecast = useMemo(() => {
01676:     return formatYmdHour(analysisYmd, analysisHourBucket);
01677:   }, [analysisYmd, analysisHourBucket]);
01678: 
01679:   const headerTransition = useMemo(() => {
01680:     const from = String(lastHourBucket || "").trim();
01681:     const to = String(analysisHourBucket || "").trim();
01682:     if (from && to) return `${from} → ${to}`;
01683:     return "—";
01684:   }, [lastHourBucket, analysisHourBucket]);
01685: 
01686:   const t = theme || {
01687:     bg: "#050505",
01688:     panel: "rgba(0,0,0,0.55)",
01689:     border: "rgba(255,255,255,0.18)",
01690:     text: "rgba(255,255,255,0.92)",
01691:     muted: "rgba(255,255,255,0.72)",
01692:     accent: "rgba(201,168,62,0.92)",
01693:   };
01694: 
01695:   const [copiedAllKey, setCopiedAllKey] = useState("");
01696:   const [copiedCellKey, setCopiedCellKey] = useState("");
01697: 
01698: 
01699:   useEffect(() => {
01700:     if (!copiedAllKey) return;
01701:     const id = setTimeout(() => setCopiedAllKey(""), 900);
01702:     return () => clearTimeout(id);
01703:   }, [copiedAllKey]);
01704: 
01705:   useEffect(() => {
01706:     if (!copiedCellKey) return;
01707:     const id = setTimeout(() => setCopiedCellKey(""), 750);
01708:     return () => clearTimeout(id);
01709:   }, [copiedCellKey]);
01710: 
01711:   const copyText = useCallback(async (txt) => {
01712:     const s = String(txt || "").trim();
01713:     if (!s) return false;
01714: 
01715:     try {
01716:       if (typeof navigator !== "undefined" && navigator?.clipboard?.writeText) {
01717:         await navigator.clipboard.writeText(s);
01718:         return true;
01719:       }
01720:     } catch {}
01721: 
01722:     try {
01723:       if (typeof document === "undefined") return false;
01724: 
01725:       const ta = document.createElement("textarea");
01726:       ta.value = s;
01727:       ta.style.position = "fixed";
01728:       ta.style.left = "-9999px";
01729:       ta.style.top = "0";
01730:       document.body.appendChild(ta);
01731:       ta.focus();
01732:       ta.select();
01733:       document.execCommand("copy");
01734:       document.body.removeChild(ta);
01735:       return true;
01736:     } catch {
01737:       return false;
01738:     }
01739:   }, []);
01740: 
01741:   const baseLots = Array.isArray(LOTTERY_OPTIONS) ? LOTTERY_OPTIONS : [];
01742:   const mustHave = [
01743:     { value: "PT_RIO", label: "RJ" },
01744:     { value: "FEDERAL", label: "Federal" },
01745:     { value: "LOOK", label: "LOOK" },
01746:     { value: "NACIONAL", label: "Nacional" },
01747:   ];
01748: 
01749:   const map = new Map();
01750: 
01751:   [...baseLots, ...mustHave].forEach((op) => {
01752:     const rawVal = String(op?.value ?? op?.key ?? "").toUpperCase();
01753:     if (!rawVal) return;
01754: 
01755:     if (!map.has(rawVal)) {
01756:       map.set(rawVal, {
01757:         value: rawVal,
01758:         label:
01759:           rawVal === "PT_RIO"
01760:             ? "RJ"
01761:             : op?.label || rawVal,
01762:       });
01763:     }
01764:   });
01765: 
01766:   const lotOptions = [
01767:     map.get("PT_RIO"),
01768:     map.get("FEDERAL"),
01769:     map.get("LOOK"),
01770:     map.get("NACIONAL"),
01771:     ...[...map.values()].filter(
01772:       (x) =>
01773:         ![
01774:           "PT_RIO",
01775:           "FEDERAL",
01776:           "LOOK",
01777:           "NACIONAL",
01778:         ].includes(
01779:           String(
01780:             x?.value || ""
01781:           ).toUpperCase()
01782:         )
01783:     ),
01784:   ].filter(Boolean);
01785: 
01786:   const curLot = String(lotteryKeySafe || "PT_RIO").toUpperCase();
01787:   const visibleTop3 = list.slice(0, 3);
01788: 
01789:   const historyAnchorYmd = useMemo(() => {
01790:   const loaded = String(loadedYmd || "").trim();
01791:   if (isYMD(loaded)) return loaded;
01792: 
01793:   const selected = String(ymdSafe || "").trim();
01794:   if (isYMD(selected)) return selected;
01795: 
01796:   const realized = (Array.isArray(timeline) ? timeline : [])
01797:     .filter((slot) => String(slot?.status || "").toLowerCase() === "validated")
01798:     .slice()
01799:     .sort((a, b) => {
01800:       const ta = `${a?.targetYmd || ""} ${a?.targetHour || ""}`;
01801:       const tb = `${b?.targetYmd || ""} ${b?.targetHour || ""}`;

02105:         item?.hitType === "none"

02109:       (sum, item) => sum + Number(item?.hitScore || 0),
02110:       0

02114:       validated.length > 0
02115:         ? totalScore / validated.length
02116:         : 0;

02119:       total: historyRows.length,
02120:       validated: validated.length,
02121:       pending: Math.max(0, historyRows.length - validated.length),
02122:       exact,
02123:       centena,
02124:       grupo,

02126:       average,

00005: function extractResultMilhar(slot) {
00006:   if (!slot || !Array.isArray(slot.prizes)) return "";
00007: 
00008:   const p1 = slot.prizes.find((p) => Number(p.position) === 1);
00009:   if (!p1) return "";
00010: 
00011:   const num = String(
00012:     p1.numero ||
00013:       p1.milhar ||
00014:       p1.number ||
00015:       p1.valor ||
00016:       ""
00017:   ).replace(/\D/g, "");
00018: 
00019:   return num.padStart(4, "0");
00020: }
00021: 
00022: function toPercent(score) {
00023:   const n = Number(score);
00024:   if (!Number.isFinite(n)) return 0;
00025:   const pct = n <= 1 ? n * 100 : n;
00026:   return Math.max(0, Math.min(100, pct));
00027: }
00028: 
00029: function formatGrupo(grupo) {
00030:   const g = Number(grupo);
00031:   if (!Number.isFinite(g) || g <= 0) return "—";
00032:   return String(Math.trunc(g)).padStart(2, "0");
00033: }
00034: 
00035: function normalizeMilharStr(v) {
00036:   const s = String(v || "").trim();
00037:   if (!s) return "";
00038:   const dig = s.replace(/\D+/g, "");
00039:   if (!dig) return "";
00040:   return dig.length >= 4 ? dig.slice(-4) : dig.padStart(4, "0");
00041: }
00042: 
00043: function centenaFromMilhar(m4) {
00044:   const s = normalizeMilharStr(m4);
00045:   return s ? s.slice(-3) : "";
00046: }
00047: 
00048: function dezenaFromMilhar(m4) {
00049:   const s = normalizeMilharStr(m4);
00050:   return s ? s.slice(-2) : "";
00051: }
00052: 
00053: function isYMD(s) {
00054:   return /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(String(s || "").trim());
00055: }
00056: 
00057: function ymdToBR(ymd) {
00058:   const s = String(ymd || "").trim();
00059:   if (!isYMD(s)) return s || "—";
00060:   const [y, m, d] = s.split("-");
00061:   return `${d}/${m}/${y}`;
00062: }
00063: 
00064: function formatYmdHour(ymd, hour) {
00065:   const y = String(ymd || "").trim();
00066:   const h = String(hour || "").trim();
00067:   if (y && h) return `${ymdToBR(y)} ${h}`;
00068:   if (y) return ymdToBR(y);
00069:   if (h) return h;
00070:   return "—";
00071: }
00072: 
00073: function hourBucketToSortValue(hour) {
00074:   const s = String(hour || "").trim().toLowerCase();
00075:   const m = s.match(/^(\d{1,2})(?::(\d{2}))?h?$/);
00076:   if (!m) return -1;
00077:   const hh = Number(m[1]);
00078:   const mm = Number(m[2] || 0);
00079:   if (!Number.isFinite(hh) || !Number.isFinite(mm)) return -1;
00080:   return hh * 60 + mm;
00081: }
00082: 
00083: function wrapToDezena2(n) {
00084:   const x = Number(n);
00085:   if (!Number.isFinite(x)) return "";
00086:   const d = ((x % 100) + 100) % 100;
00087:   return String(d).padStart(2, "0");
00088: }
00089: 
00090: function getDezenasFixasFromGrupo(grupo) {
00091:   const g = Number(grupo);
00092:   if (!Number.isFinite(g) || g < 1 || g > 25) return [];
00093: 
00094:   const start = (g - 1) * 4 + 1;
00095:   const out = [];
00096: 
00097:   for (let i = 0; i < 4; i += 1) {
00098:     out.push(wrapToDezena2(start + i));
00099:   }
00100: 
00101:   return out;
00102: }

00104: function clampColsFromItemMilharesCols(milharesCols, expectedCols = 4, perCol = 5) {
00105:   const colsArr = Array.isArray(milharesCols) ? milharesCols : [];
00106:   const out = [];
00107: 
00108:   for (let i = 0; i < Math.min(expectedCols, colsArr.length); i += 1) {
00109:     const dz = String(colsArr[i]?.dezena || "").trim();
00110:     const items0 = Array.isArray(colsArr[i]?.items) ? colsArr[i].items : [];
00111: 
00112:     const items = items0
00113:       .map(normalizeMilharStr)
00114:       .map((m) => (m && /^\d{4}$/.test(m) ? m : ""))
00115:       .slice(0, perCol);
00116: 
00117:     while (items.length < perCol) items.push("");
00118:     out.push({ dezena: dz, items });
00119:   }
00120: 
00121:   while (out.length < expectedCols) {
00122:     out.push({ dezena: "", items: Array(perCol).fill("") });
00123:   }
00124: 
00125:   return out.slice(0, expectedCols);
00126: }

00128: function build20ByDezena({ grupo, baseMilhares, perCol = 5 }) {
00129:   const g = Number(grupo);
00130:   const dezenas = getDezenasFixasFromGrupo(g);
00131: 
00132:   if (!dezenas.length) {
00133:     return { dezenas: [], rows: [], flat20: [] };
00134:   }
00135: 
00136:   const input = Array.isArray(baseMilhares) ? baseMilhares : [];
00137:   const normalized = input
00138:     .map(normalizeMilharStr)
00139:     .filter((x) => /^\d{4}$/.test(x));
00140: 
00141:   const byDz = new Map();
00142:   for (const dz of dezenas) byDz.set(dz, []);
00143: 
00144:   for (const m4 of normalized) {
00145:     const dz = dezenaFromMilhar(m4);
00146:     if (byDz.has(dz)) byDz.get(dz).push(m4);
00147:   }
00148: 
00149:   const seenCent = new Set();
00150:   const seenMilhar = new Set();
00151: 
00152:   const cols = {};
00153:   for (const dz of dezenas) cols[dz] = [];
00154: 
00155:   const tryPush = (dz, m4) => {
00156:     const mm = normalizeMilharStr(m4);
00157:     if (!mm) return false;
00158: 
00159:     const c3 = centenaFromMilhar(mm);
00160:     if (!c3) return false;
00161: 
00162:     if (seenCent.has(c3)) return false;
00163:     if (seenMilhar.has(mm)) return false;
00164: 
00165:     cols[dz].push(mm);
00166:     seenCent.add(c3);
00167:     seenMilhar.add(mm);
00168:     return true;
00169:   };
00170: 
00171:   for (const dz of dezenas) {
00172:     const arr = byDz.get(dz) || [];
00173:     for (const m4 of arr) {
00174:       if (cols[dz].length >= perCol) break;
00175:       tryPush(dz, m4);
00176:     }
00177:   }
00178: 
00179:   for (const dz of dezenas) {
00180:     while (cols[dz].length < perCol) cols[dz].push("");
00181:     if (cols[dz].length > perCol) cols[dz] = cols[dz].slice(0, perCol);
00182:   }
00183: 
00184:   const rows = [];
00185:   for (let r = 0; r < perCol; r += 1) {
00186:     rows.push(dezenas.map((dz) => cols[dz][r] || ""));
00187:   }
00188: 
00189:   const flat20 = rows.flat().filter(Boolean);
00190:   return { dezenas, rows, flat20 };
00191: }
00192: 
00193: function cleanLayerText(s) {
00194:   const raw = String(s || "").trim();
00195:   if (!raw) return "—";
00196: 
00197:   const noSamples = raw
00198:     .replace(/\s*[•\-|]\s*Amostras:\s*\d+\s*$/i, "")
00199:     .replace(/\s*Amostras:\s*\d+\s*$/i, "")
00200:     .trim();
00201: 
00202:   return noSamples || "—";
00203: }
00204: 
00205: function getSlotResultGrupo(slot) {
00206:   const direct = Number(
00207:     slot?.resultGrupo ??
00208:       slot?.grupo ??
00209:       slot?.result?.grupo ??
00210:       slot?.resultado?.grupo
00211:   );
00212: 
00213:   if (Number.isFinite(direct) && direct >= 1 && direct <= 25) {
00214:     return direct;
00215:   }
00216: 
00217:   if (Array.isArray(slot?.prizes)) {
00218:     const p1 = slot.prizes.find((p) => Number(p?.position) === 1) || slot.prizes[0];
00219:     const fromPrize = Number(
00220:       p1?.grupo ??
00221:         p1?.group ??
00222:         p1?.animal_grupo ??
00223:         p1?.grupo2
00224:     );
00225: 
00226:     if (Number.isFinite(fromPrize) && fromPrize >= 1 && fromPrize <= 25) {
00227:       return fromPrize;
00228:     }
00229:   }
00230: 
00231:   return NaN;
00232: }
00233: 
00234: function hasOfficialResult(slot) {
00235:   const grupo = getSlotResultGrupo(slot);
00236:   if (Number.isFinite(grupo) && grupo >= 1 && grupo <= 25) return true;
00237: 
00238:   const milhar = extractResultMilhar(slot);
00239:   return /^\d{4}$/.test(milhar);
00240: }
00241: 
00242: function analyzeTop3Hit(top3, resultGrupo, resultMilhar) {
00243:   if (!Array.isArray(top3) || !top3.length) {
00244:     return { type: "none", score: 0, position: -1 };
00245:   }
00246: 
00247:   const grupoNum = Number(resultGrupo);
00248:   const milhar = normalizeMilharStr(resultMilhar);
00249:   const centena = milhar ? milhar.slice(-3) : "";
00250: 
00251:   const hasGrupo = Number.isFinite(grupoNum) && grupoNum >= 1 && grupoNum <= 25;
00252:   const hasMilhar = /^\d{4}$/.test(milhar);
00253: 
00254:   if (!hasGrupo && !hasMilhar) {
00255:     return { type: "none", score: 0, position: -1 };
00256:   }
00257: 
00258:   let best = { type: "miss", score: 0, position: -1 };
00259: 
00260:   top3.forEach((item, idx) => {
00261:     const g = Number(item?.grupo);
00262: 
00263:     const milhares = Array.isArray(item?.milhares20)
00264:       ? item.milhares20
00265:       : Array.isArray(item?.milhares)
00266:         ? item.milhares
00267:         : [];
00268: 
00269:     const centenas = milhares.map((m) => centenaFromMilhar(m));
00270: 
00271:     let score = 0;
00272:     let type = "miss";
00273: 
00274:     if (hasMilhar && milhares.includes(milhar)) {
00275:       score = 100;
00276:       type = "hit_exact";
00277:     } else if (centena && centenas.includes(centena)) {
00278:       score = 66;
00279:       type = "hit_centena";
00280:     } else if (hasGrupo && g === grupoNum) {
00281:       score = 33;
00282:       type = "hit_grupo";
00283:     }
00284: 
00285:     if (score > best.score) {
00286:       best = {
00287:         type,
00288:         score,
00289:         position: idx + 1,
00290:       };
00291:     }
00292:   });
00293: 
00294:   return best;
00295: }
00296: 
00297: function ImgWithFallback({ srcs, alt, size = 84, style }) {
00298:   const list = useMemo(
00299:     () => (Array.isArray(srcs) ? srcs.filter(Boolean) : []),
00300:     [srcs]
00301:   );
00302: 
00303:   const [i, setI] = useState(0);
00304:   const listKey = useMemo(() => list.join("|"), [list]);
00305: 
00306:   useEffect(() => {
00307:     setI(0);
00308:   }, [listKey]);
00309: 
00310:   const src = list[i] || "";
00311: 
00312:   const onError = () => {
00313:     setI((prev) => (prev < list.length - 1 ? prev + 1 : prev));
00314:   };
00315: 
00316:   return (
00317:     <div
00318:       style={{
00319:         width: size,
00320:         height: size,
00321:         borderRadius: 16,
00322:         border: "1px solid rgba(201,168,62,0.36)",
00323:         background:
00324:           "linear-gradient(180deg, rgba(255,255,255,0.04), rgba(0,0,0,0.28))",
00325:         display: "grid",
00326:         placeItems: "center",
00327:         overflow: "hidden",
00328:         boxShadow: "inset 0 0 0 1px rgba(255,255,255,0.04)",
00329:         ...style,
00330:       }}
00331:     >
00332:       {src ? (
00333:         <img
00334:           src={src}
00335:           alt={alt || ""}
00336:           width={size}
00337:           height={size}
00338:           onError={onError}
00339:           style={{
00340:             width: "100%",
00341:             height: "100%",
00342:             objectFit: "cover",
00343:             display: "block",
00344:             imageRendering: "auto",
00345:           }}
00346:         />
00347:       ) : (
00348:         <div style={{ color: "rgba(255,255,255,0.55)", fontSize: 12 }}>
00349:           sem imagem
00350:         </div>
00351:       )}
00352:     </div>
00353:   );
00354: }

00356: function Top3Card({
00357:   item,
00358:   idx,
00359:   theme,
00360:   copiedAllKey,
00361:   copiedCellKey,
00362:   setCopiedAllKey,
00363:   setCopiedCellKey,
00364:   copyText,
00365:   build16,
00366:   buildMilhares,
00367:   build20,
00368: }) {
00369:   const t = theme;
00370: 
00371:   const grupoTxt = formatGrupo(item?.grupo);
00372:   const animal = String(item?.animal || "").trim();
00373: 
00374:   const samplesRaw = Number(item?.meta?.samples ?? item?.samples ?? 0);
00375:   const samples = Number.isFinite(samplesRaw)
00376:     ? Math.max(0, Math.trunc(samplesRaw))
00377:     : 0;
00378: 
00379:   const freqRaw = Number(item?.freq ?? 0);
00380:   const freq = Number.isFinite(freqRaw)
00381:     ? Math.max(0, Math.trunc(freqRaw))
00382:     : 0;
00383: 
00384:   const denom = samples > 0 ? samples * 5 : 0;
00385:   const derivedScore = denom > 0 ? freq / denom : 0;
00386: 
00387:   const pct = toPercent(
00388:     item?.probPct ??
00389:       item?.prob ??
00390:       item?.probCond ??
00391:       item?.score ??
00392:       derivedScore
00393:   );
00394: 
00395:   const iconSrcs =
00396:     Array.isArray(item?.imgIcon) && item.imgIcon.length
00397:       ? item.imgIcon
00398:       : Array.isArray(item?.imgBg)
00399:         ? item.imgBg
00400:         : [];
00401: 
00402:   const hasCols =
00403:     Array.isArray(item?.milharesCols) &&
00404:     item.milharesCols.length >= 4 &&
00405:     Array.isArray(item.milharesCols[0]?.items);
00406: 
00407:   let dezenasHeader = [];
00408:   let gridRows = Array.from({ length: 5 }, () => Array(4).fill(""));
00409:   let flat20 = [];
00410: 
00411:   if (hasCols) {
00412:     const cols4 = clampColsFromItemMilharesCols(item.milharesCols, 4, 5);
00413:     dezenasHeader = cols4.map((c) => String(c.dezena || ""));
00414:     gridRows = Array.from({ length: 5 }, (_, r) =>
00415:       cols4.map((c) => c.items[r] || "")
00416:     );
00417:     flat20 = gridRows.flat().filter(Boolean);
00418:   } else {
00419:     let milharesBase = [];
00420: 
00421:     const m20 = Array.isArray(item?.milhares20) ? item.milhares20 : null;
00422:     const mAny = Array.isArray(item?.milhares) ? item.milhares : null;
00423: 
00424:     if (m20 && m20.length) milharesBase = m20.slice(0);
00425:     else if (mAny && mAny.length) milharesBase = mAny.slice(0);
00426: 
00427:     if (!milharesBase.length) {
00428:       const g = Number(item?.grupo);
00429: 
00430:       if (Number.isFinite(g) && g > 0) {
00431:         if (typeof build20 === "function") {
00432:           const out20 = build20(g);
00433:           const slots20 = Array.isArray(out20?.slots) ? out20.slots : [];
00434:           milharesBase = slots20.map((x) => x?.milhar).filter(Boolean);
00435:         } else if (typeof buildMilhares === "function") {
00436:           const out = buildMilhares(g, 20);
00437:           if (Array.isArray(out)) {
00438:             milharesBase = out.slice(0);
00439:           } else if (out && Array.isArray(out.slots)) {
00440:             milharesBase = out.slots.map((x) => x?.milhar).filter(Boolean);
00441:           }
00442:         } else if (typeof build16 === "function") {
00443:           const out16 = build16(g);
00444:           const slots16 = Array.isArray(out16?.slots) ? out16.slots : [];
00445:           milharesBase = slots16.map((x) => x?.milhar).filter(Boolean);
00446:         }
00447:       }
00448:     }
00449: 
00450:     const grupoNum = Number(item?.grupo);
00451:     const grid = build20ByDezena({
00452:       grupo: grupoNum,
00453:       baseMilhares: milharesBase,
00454:       perCol: 5,
00455:     });
00456: 
00457:     dezenasHeader = grid.dezenas;
00458:     gridRows = grid.rows;
00459:     flat20 = grid.flat20;
00460:   }
00461: 
00462:   const key = `${String(item?.grupo ?? "g")}__${animal || "x"}__${idx}`;
00463:   const rank = idx + 1;
00464:   const isHero = idx === 0;
00465: 
00466:   const title =
00467:     idx === 0 ? "1º MAIS FORTE" : idx === 1 ? "2º MAIS FORTE" : "3º MAIS FORTE";
00468: 
00469:   const doCopyAll = async () => {
00470:     const payload = flat20.join(" ").trim();
00471:     if (!payload) return;
00472: 
00473:     const ok = await copyText(payload);
00474:     if (ok) setCopiedAllKey(key);
00475:   };
00476: 
00477:   const doCopyOne = async (m, rIdx, cIdx) => {
00478:     const mm = normalizeMilharStr(m);
00479:     if (!mm) return;
00480: 
00481:     const ok = await copyText(mm);
00482:     if (ok) setCopiedCellKey(`${key}__${rIdx}__${cIdx}`);
00483:   };
00484: 
00485:   return (
00486:     <article
00487:       className={`top3-card ${isHero ? "top3-card--hero" : "top3-card--secondary"}`}
00488:     >
00489:       <div className="top3-card__topline">
00490:         <div className="top3-rankBadge">{rank}</div>
00491:         <div className="top3-card__label">🏅 {title}</div>
00492:       </div>
00493: 
00494:       <div className="top3-card__summary">
00495:         <div className="top3-card__identity">
00496:           <ImgWithFallback
00497:             srcs={iconSrcs}
00498:             alt={animal ? animal : `G${grupoTxt}`}
00499:             size={isHero ? 92 : 84}
00500:           />
00501: 
00502:           <div className="top3-card__identityText">
00503:             <div className="top3-card__group">GRUPO {grupoTxt}</div>
00504:             <div className="top3-card__animal">
00505:               {animal ? animal.toUpperCase() : "—"}
00506:             </div>
00507:           </div>
00508:         </div>
00509: 
00510:         <div className="top3-card__confidence">
00511:           <div className="top3-card__confidenceLabel">CONFIANÇA</div>
00512:           <div className="top3-card__confidenceValue">{pct.toFixed(2)}%</div>
00513:           <div className="top3-card__confidenceBar">
00514:             <div
00515:               className="top3-card__confidenceBarFill"
00516:               style={{ width: `${pct}%`, background: t.accent }}
00517:             />
00518:           </div>
00519:         </div>
00520:       </div>
00521: 
00522:       <div className="top3-card__body">
00523:         <div className="top3-card__actions">
00524:           <div className="top3-card__sectionTitle"></div>
00525: 
00526:           <button
00527:             type="button"
00528:             onClick={doCopyAll}
00529:             className="pp-btn"
00530:             title="Copiar as 20 milhares"
00531:           >
00532:             {copiedAllKey === key ? "✅ Copiado" : "Apostar"}
00533:           </button>
00534:         </div>
00535: 
00536:         {dezenasHeader.length ? (
00537:           <div className="pp-chipRow">
00538:             {dezenasHeader.map((dz, i) => (
00539:               <div key={`${dz}-${i}`} className="pp-chip">
00540:                 {dz || "—"}
00541:               </div>
00542:             ))}
00543:           </div>
00544:         ) : null}
00545: 
00546:         <div className="pp-gridBox">
00547:           {gridRows.map((row, rIdx) => (
00548:             <div key={rIdx} className="pp-row">
00549:               {row.map((m, cIdx) => {
00550:                 const mm = normalizeMilharStr(m);
00551:                 const empty = !mm ? "1" : "0";
00552:                 const cKey = `${key}__${rIdx}__${cIdx}`;
00553:                 const isCopied = copiedCellKey === cKey;
00554: 
00555:                 return (
00556:                   <div
00557:                     key={`${rIdx}-${cIdx}`}
00558:                     className="pp-pill"
00559:                     data-empty={empty}
00560:                     title={mm ? "Clique para copiar" : ""}
00561:                     onClick={() => doCopyOne(mm, rIdx, cIdx)}
00562:                   >
00563:                     {mm || "—"}
00564:                     {isCopied ? <div className="pp-copiedBadge">COPIADO</div> : null}
00565:                   </div>
00566:                 );
00567:               })}
00568:             </div>
00569:           ))}
00570:         </div>
00571:       </div>
00572:     </article>
00573:   );
00574: }

00576: function CompactTop3Result({ slot, status, statusLabel, statusDetail }) {
00577:   const slotTop3 = Array.isArray(slot?.top3) ? slot.top3.slice(0, 3) : [];
00578:   const resultGrupo = getSlotResultGrupo(slot);
00579:   const hasResult = Number.isFinite(resultGrupo) && resultGrupo >= 1 && resultGrupo <= 25;
00580:   const resultAnimal = hasResult ? String(getAnimalLabel(resultGrupo) || "") : "";
00581:   const resultImg = hasResult ? getImgFromGrupo(resultGrupo, 64) : "";
00582: 
00583:   return (
00584:     <div className="timeline-compact">
00585:       <div className="timeline-compact__grid">
00586:         <div className="timeline-compact__predictions">
00587:           {slotTop3.map((item, idx) => {
00588:             const grupo = Number(item?.grupo);
00589:             const animal = String(item?.animal || getAnimalLabel(grupo) || "").trim();
00590:             const img = Number.isFinite(grupo) ? getImgFromGrupo(grupo, 64) : "";
00591: 
00592:             return (
00593:               <div
00594:                 key={`${String(slot?.targetYmd || "y")}_${String(slot?.targetHour || "h")}_${idx}_${grupo}`}
00595:                 className="timeline-compact__pick"
00596:               >
00597:                 <div className="timeline-compact__pickRank">{idx + 1}</div>
00598:                 <ImgWithFallback
00599:                   srcs={img ? [img] : []}
00600:                   alt={animal || `G${formatGrupo(grupo)}`}
00601:                   size={44}
00602:                   style={{ borderRadius: 10 }}
00603:                 />
00604:                 <div className="timeline-compact__pickText">
00605:                   <div className="timeline-compact__pickGroup">G{formatGrupo(grupo)}</div>
00606:                   <div className="timeline-compact__pickAnimal">
00607:                     {animal ? animal.toUpperCase() : "—"}
00608:                   </div>
00609:                 </div>
00610:               </div>
00611:             );
00612:           })}
00613:         </div>
00614: 
00615:         <div className="timeline-compact__result">
00616:           <div className="timeline-compact__resultLabel">Resultado</div>
00617: 
00618:           {hasResult ? (
00619:             <div className="timeline-compact__resultBox">
00620:               <ImgWithFallback
00621:                 srcs={resultImg ? [resultImg] : []}
00622:                 alt={resultAnimal}
00623:                 size={48}
00624:                 style={{ borderRadius: 10 }}
00625:               />
00626:               <div className="timeline-compact__resultText">
00627:                 <div className="timeline-compact__resultGroup">G{formatGrupo(resultGrupo)}</div>
00628:                 <div className="timeline-compact__resultAnimal">
00629:                   {String(resultAnimal || "").toUpperCase()}
00630:                 </div>
00631:               </div>
00632:             </div>
00633:           ) : (
00634:             <div className="timeline-compact__resultPending">PENDENTE</div>
00635:           )}
00636:         </div>
00637: 
00638:         <div className="timeline-compact__performance">
00639:           <div
00640:             className={`timeline-compact__performanceBadge timeline-compact__performanceBadge--${status}`}
00641:           >
00642:             {statusLabel}
00643:           </div>
00644:           <div className="timeline-compact__performanceText">{statusDetail}</div>
00645:         </div>
00646:       </div>
00647:     </div>
00648:   );
00649: }

00651: function TimelineSlot({
00652:   slot,
00653:   theme,
00654:   copiedAllKey,
00655:   copiedCellKey,
00656:   setCopiedAllKey,
00657:   setCopiedCellKey,
00658:   copyText,
00659:   build16,
00660:   buildMilhares,
00661:   build20,
00662: }) {
00663:   const t = theme;
00664:   const slotTop3 = Array.isArray(slot?.top3) ? slot.top3.slice(0, 3) : [];
00665: 
00666:   const resultGrupo = getSlotResultGrupo(slot);
00667:   const hasResult = hasOfficialResult(slot);
00668:   const resultAnimal =
00669:     hasResult && Number.isFinite(resultGrupo)
00670:       ? String(getAnimalLabel(resultGrupo) || "")
00671:       : "";
00672: 
00673:   const analysis = analyzeTop3Hit(
00674:     slotTop3,
00675:     resultGrupo,
00676:     extractResultMilhar(slot)
00677:   );
00678: 
00679:   const status =
00680:     !hasResult
00681:       ? "pending"
00682:       : analysis.type === "miss"
00683:         ? "miss"
00684:         : "hit";
00685: 
00686:   const statusLabel =
00687:     !hasResult
00688:       ? "⏳ PENDENTE"
00689:       : analysis.type === "hit_exact"
00690:         ? `🎯 ACERTO TOTAL (${analysis.score}%)`
00691:         : analysis.type === "hit_centena"
00692:           ? `🟡 CENTENA (${analysis.score}%)`
00693:           : analysis.type === "hit_grupo"
00694:             ? `✅ GRUPO (${analysis.score}%)`
00695:             : "❌ ERRO (0%)";
00696: 
00697:   const statusDetail =
00698:     !hasResult
00699:       ? "Aguardando resultado oficial."
00700:       : analysis.type === "hit_exact"
00701:         ? `Milhar exata acertada no palpite #${analysis.position}.`
00702:         : analysis.type === "hit_centena"
00703:           ? `Centena acertada no palpite #${analysis.position}.`
00704:           : analysis.type === "hit_grupo"
00705:             ? `Grupo acertado no palpite #${analysis.position}.`
00706:             : "Nenhum dos 3 palpites foi sorteado.";
00707: 
00708:   return (
00709:     <section className="top3-shell">
00710:       <div className="timeline-slot__header">
00711:         <div className="timeline-slot__title">
00712:           {formatYmdHour(slot?.targetYmd, slot?.targetHour)}
00713:         </div>
00714: 
00715:         <div className={`timeline-slot__badge timeline-slot__badge--${status}`}>
00716:           {statusLabel}
00717:         </div>
00718:       </div>
00719: 
00720:       <div className="timeline-slot__metaGrid">
00721:         <div className="top3-metaItem">
00722:           <div className="top3-metaItem__label">Base usada</div>
00723:           <div className="top3-metaItem__value">
00724:             {formatYmdHour(slot?.baseYmd, slot?.baseHour)}
00725:           </div>
00726:         </div>
00727: 
00728:         <div className="top3-metaItem">
00729:           <div className="top3-metaItem__label">Resultado real</div>
00730:           <div className="top3-metaItem__value">
00731:             {hasResult
00732:               ? `G${formatGrupo(resultGrupo)} • ${resultAnimal.toUpperCase()}`
00733:               : "Pendente"}
00734:           </div>
00735:         </div>
00736: 
00737:         <div className="top3-metaItem">
00738:           <div className="top3-metaItem__label">Status</div>
00739:           <div className="top3-metaItem__value">{statusDetail}</div>
00740:         </div>
00741:       </div>
00742: 
00743:       {!slotTop3.length ? (
00744:         <div className="top3-empty">Sem Top3 calculado para este horário.</div>
00745:       ) : status === "pending" ? (
00746:         <div className="timeline-slot__cards">
00747:           {slotTop3.map((item, i) => (
00748:             <Top3Card
00749:               key={`${slot?.targetYmd || "y"}_${slot?.targetHour || "h"}_${String(item?.grupo ?? i)}`}
00750:               item={item}
00751:               idx={i}
00752:               theme={t}
00753:               copiedAllKey={copiedAllKey}
00754:               copiedCellKey={copiedCellKey}
00755:               setCopiedAllKey={setCopiedAllKey}
00756:               setCopiedCellKey={setCopiedCellKey}
00757:               copyText={copyText}
00758:               build16={build16}
00759:               buildMilhares={buildMilhares}
00760:               build20={build20}
00761:             />
00762:           ))}
00763:         </div>
00764:       ) : (
00765:         <CompactTop3Result
00766:           slot={slot}
00767:           status={status}
00768:           statusLabel={statusLabel}
00769:           statusDetail={statusDetail}
00770:         />
00771:       )}
00772:     </section>
00773:   );
00774: }
00775: 
00776: export default function Top3View(props) {
00777:   const {
00778:     loading,
00779:     error,
00780:     top3,
00781:     timelineTop3,
00782:     layerMetaText,
00783:     lastLabel,
00784:     prevLabel,
00785:     theme,
00786:     LOTTERY_OPTIONS,
00787:     lotteryKeySafe,
00788:     setLotteryKey,
00789:     build16,
00790:     buildMilhares,
00791:     build20,
00792:     analysisYmd,
00793:     analysisHourBucket,
00794:     lastHourBucket,
00795:   } = props || {};
00796: 
00797:   const list = Array.isArray(top3) ? top3.slice(0, 3) : [];
00798:   const timeline = (Array.isArray(timelineTop3) ? timelineTop3 : [])
00799:   .slice()
00800:   .sort((a, b) => {
00801:     const ta = `${a?.targetYmd || ""} ${a?.targetHour || ""}`;
00802:     const tb = `${b?.targetYmd || ""} ${b?.targetHour || ""}`;
00803:     return ta.localeCompare(tb);
00804:   });
00805: 
00806:   const meta = useMemo(() => {
00807:     const last = lastLabel || "—";
00808:     const prev = prevLabel || "—";
00809:     const layer = cleanLayerText(layerMetaText || "—");
00810:     return { last, prev, layer };
00811:   }, [lastLabel, prevLabel, layerMetaText]);
00812: 
00813:   const headerBase = useMemo(() => meta.last || "—", [meta.last]);
00814: 
00815:   const headerForecast = useMemo(() => {
00816:     return formatYmdHour(analysisYmd, analysisHourBucket);
00817:   }, [analysisYmd, analysisHourBucket]);
00818: 
00819:   const headerTransition = useMemo(() => {
00820:     const from = String(lastHourBucket || "").trim();
00821:     const to = String(analysisHourBucket || "").trim();
00822:     if (from && to) return `${from} → ${to}`;
00823:     return "—";
00824:   }, [lastHourBucket, analysisHourBucket]);
00825: 
00826:   const t = theme || {
00827:     bg: "#050505",
00828:     panel: "rgba(0,0,0,0.55)",
00829:     border: "rgba(255,255,255,0.18)",
00830:     text: "rgba(255,255,255,0.92)",
00831:     muted: "rgba(255,255,255,0.72)",
00832:     accent: "rgba(201,168,62,0.92)",
00833:   };
00834: 
00835:   const [copiedAllKey, setCopiedAllKey] = useState("");
00836:   const [copiedCellKey, setCopiedCellKey] = useState("");
00837:   const [log, setLog] = useState([]);
00838: 
00839:   useEffect(() => {
00840:     try {
00841:       const data = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem("top3_log") || "[]");
00842:       setLog(Array.isArray(data) ? data : []);
00843:     } catch {
00844:       setLog([]);
00845:     }
00846:   }, []);
00847: 
00848:   useEffect(() => {
00849:     if (!copiedAllKey) return;
00850:     const id = setTimeout(() => setCopiedAllKey(""), 900);
00851:     return () => clearTimeout(id);
00852:   }, [copiedAllKey]);
00853: 
00854:   useEffect(() => {
00855:     if (!copiedCellKey) return;
00856:     const id = setTimeout(() => setCopiedCellKey(""), 750);
00857:     return () => clearTimeout(id);
00858:   }, [copiedCellKey]);
00859: 
00860:   const copyText = useCallback(async (txt) => {
00861:     const s = String(txt || "").trim();
00862:     if (!s) return false;
00863: 
00864:     try {
00865:       if (typeof navigator !== "undefined" && navigator?.clipboard?.writeText) {
00866:         await navigator.clipboard.writeText(s);
00867:         return true;
00868:       }
00869:     } catch {}
00870: 
00871:     try {
00872:       if (typeof document === "undefined") return false;
00873: 
00874:       const ta = document.createElement("textarea");
00875:       ta.value = s;
00876:       ta.style.position = "fixed";
00877:       ta.style.left = "-9999px";
00878:       ta.style.top = "0";
00879:       document.body.appendChild(ta);
00880:       ta.focus();
00881:       ta.select();
00882:       document.execCommand("copy");
00883:       document.body.removeChild(ta);
00884:       return true;
00885:     } catch {
00886:       return false;
00887:     }
00888:   }, []);
00889: 
00890:   const baseLots = Array.isArray(LOTTERY_OPTIONS) ? LOTTERY_OPTIONS : [];
00891:   const mustHave = [
00892:     { value: "PT_RIO", label: "PT_RIO (RJ)" },
00893:     { value: "FEDERAL", label: "Federal" },
00894:   ];
00895: 
00896:   const map = new Map();
00897: 
00898:   [...baseLots, ...mustHave].forEach((op) => {
00899:     const rawVal = String(op?.value ?? op?.key ?? "").toUpperCase();
00900:     if (!rawVal) return;
00901: 
00902:     if (!map.has(rawVal)) {
00903:       map.set(rawVal, {
00904:         value: rawVal,
00905:         label: op?.label || rawVal,
00906:       });
00907:     }
00908:   });
00909: 
00910:   const lotOptions = [
00911:     map.get("PT_RIO"),
00912:     map.get("FEDERAL"),

00914:       (x) => !["PT_RIO", "FEDERAL"].includes(String(x?.value || "").toUpperCase())
00915:     ),
00916:   ].filter(Boolean);
00917: 
00918:   const curLot = String(lotteryKeySafe || "PT_RIO").toUpperCase();
00919:   const heroItem = list[0] || null;
00920:   const secondaryItems = list.slice(1, 3);
00921: 
00922:   const historyAnchorYmd = useMemo(() => {
00923:     const y = String(analysisYmd || "").trim();
00924:     if (isYMD(y)) return y;
00925: 
00926:     const last = log[log.length - 1];
00927:     const fallback = String(last?.target?.ymd || "").trim();
00928:     return isYMD(fallback) ? fallback : "";
00929:   }, [analysisYmd, log]);
00930:

00932:     if (!log.length || !historyAnchorYmd) return [];
00933: 
00934:     return [...log]
00935:       .filter((item) => String(item?.target?.ymd || "").trim() === historyAnchorYmd)
00936:       .sort((a, b) => {
00937:         const ah = hourBucketToSortValue(a?.target?.hour);
00938:         const bh = hourBucketToSortValue(b?.target?.hour);
00939:         return ah - bh;
00940:       });
00941:   }, [log, historyAnchorYmd]);

02082:                 const h = String(item?.target?.hour || "");

02088:     : "--- - --- - ---";

02093:                 const hasResult =

02101:                   : "";
